Existing approaches typically retrieve a textual counterpart for each image and then refine multimodal representations by directly enforcing cross-modal agreement, e.g., maximizing image-text similarity inherited from pretrained VLMs. However, such a strategy aligns heterogeneous representations across modalities without explicitly modeling the intrinsic structure within each modality and thus might yield unreliable alignment or distort modality-specific structures that are crucial for clustering. In this paper, we propose a simple but principled approach, termed deep modality-shared self-expressive model (DeepMORSE), which discovers cross-modal structures via a modality-shared self-expressive model and simultaneously learns structured representations that conform to a union of modality-specific subspaces. Moreover, we theoretically justify that the modality-shared self-expressive coefficients suppress inter-class noise towards a subspace-preserving solution, and show that mini-batch optimization procedure introduces an implicit regularization onto the self-expressive model. We evaluate our DeepMORSE on six widely used image clustering benchmarks and observe performance improvements exceeding 3% on the UCF-101, DTD-47, and ImageNet-Dogs datasets. In addition, we demonstrate the strong transferability of the learned representations by achieving state-of-the-art performance on downstream tasks such as image retrieval and zero-shot classification---without requiring any task-specific losses or post-processing. The code is available at: https://github.com/mengxianghan123/DeepMORSE.

</details>


### [100] [FreCast: Refining Radar Echo Intensity via Phase-Preserving Amplitude Residual Diffusion for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08436)
*Heping Fang,Zihuai Yin,Kaicheng Mao,Peiguang Zhang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreCast是一个两阶段的降水临近预报框架：第一阶段生成未来雷达回波的初始预测，第二阶段利用初始预测的空间结构作为约束，逐位置修正回波强度偏差。在三个数据集上，FreCast在预报技能指标上取得一致性提升，并在较长的预报时效下更好地保持雨带连续性和强降水结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的深度学习降水临近预报模型虽能捕捉未来降水的整体空间分布，但在单个位置上的雷达回波强度预测仍存在显著偏差。作者认为与其在没有空间约束的情况下重新生成整个回波序列，不如利用预测的降水结构来引导逐位置的强度精细修正，从而更有针对性地降低预报误差。

Method: FreCast采用两阶段框架：第一阶段生成未来雷达回波的初始预报；第二阶段将初始预报的空间结构作为约束条件，对第一阶段预测中各个位置的强度偏差进行进一步修正。这实现了结构引导的强度细化，直接针对回波强度偏差进行优化。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，FreCast在各类预报技能指标上均取得了一致性的改进。定性结果显示，FreCast在较长的预报时效下能更好地保持雨带连续性和强降水结构。

Conclusion: 结构引导的强度细化策略（即先预测空间结构、再基于该结构修正逐点强度）能够有效降低降水临近预报中单个位置的雷达回波强度预测偏差，为提升临近预报精度提供了可行的改进方向。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting predicts the spatiotemporal evolution of future radar echoes from historical radar echo sequences, thereby estimating the occurrence, development, and movement of precipitation over the near term. In recent years, deep learning has become an important approach to precipitation nowcasting. Although state-of-the-art models can generally capture the overall spatial distribution of future precipitation, their predictions still exhibit substantial biases in radar echo intensity at individual locations. This observation motivates a more targeted strategy for reducing forecast errors. Instead of regenerating an entire radar echo sequence without spatial constraints, the predicted precipitation structure can be used to guide the refinement of echo intensities at individual locations. This structure-guided refinement directly targets echo intensity biases. Accordingly, we propose FreCast, a two-stage framework for radar echo prediction. The first stage generates an initial forecast of future radar echoes. The second stage uses the spatial structure of the initial forecast as a constraint to further correct intensity biases at individual locations in the first-stage prediction. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that FreCast achieves consistent improvements across forecast skill metrics. Qualitative results further show that FreCast better preserves rainband continuity and intense precipitation structures at longer lead times.

</details>


### [101] [InstructionCrafter: Generating Consistent and High-Fidelity Visual Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08460)
*Shun Okamoto,Satoshi Iizuka,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructionCrafter，一种基于扩散模型的框架，用于从文本指令生成逐步视觉指令图像序列。其核心思想是通过空间层冻结训练和指令感知适配器，将时序与指令对齐的优化与逐帧视觉质量分离，在保持高质量生成的同时实现跨帧一致性和步骤忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本任务指令生成逐步视觉指令图像序列需要同时满足三个属性：步骤忠实度、跨图像一致性和逐帧视觉质量。现有文本到图像生成方法很少能同时满足这三者，原因包括：独立采样破坏一致性、在低质量视频上微调降低逐帧质量、以及冻结的骨干网络缺乏多步骤理解能力。

Method: InstructionCrafter基于预训练的视频扩散骨干构建，采用两个关键策略：(1) 空间冻结训练——冻结控制逐帧细节的空间层，仅更新时序和文本条件路径，以学习指令语义和步骤间关系，同时保留生成先验以保证逐帧质量，相比全微调减少了约50%的可训练参数；(2) 引入两个轻量适配器——Consistent Adapter聚合整个指令序列和相邻步骤的文本线索以保持跨帧对象身份和属性一致性；Context-Aware Temporal Adapter将交叉注意力输出转换为时序自注意力的偏置，显式传播帧间关系。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验证明，InstructionCrafter在步骤忠实度、跨图像一致性和逐帧视觉质量方面均达到最先进的整体性能，同时显著减少了噪声、模糊和虚假字幕。

Conclusion: 通过解耦时序/指令对齐与逐帧质量的优化，InstructionCrafter成功地在文本到逐步视觉指令生成中同时满足了三个关键属性，且参数效率高。代码和训练模型将公开提供。

Abstract: Given textual task instructions, generating step-by-step visual instructions as an image sequence requires the simultaneous satisfaction of multiple properties, specifically step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality. Existing text-to-image generation approaches rarely meet all three properties, owing to independent sampling that breaks consistency, finetuning on low-quality video that degrades per-frame quality, and frozen backbones that lack multi-step understanding. In this work, we propose InstructionCrafter, a diffusion-based framework with the key idea of separating the optimization of temporal and instructional alignment from per-frame visual quality via (1) spatial-freeze training and (2) instruction-aware adapters. Built on a pretrained video diffusion backbone, InstructionCrafter freezes the spatial layers that control per-frame detail and updates only temporal and text-conditioning pathways to learn instruction semantics and inter-step relations, which preserves the generative prior for per-frame quality and reduces trainable parameters by about 50 percent compared with full finetuning. We also introduce two lightweight adapters that enhance the model's understanding of instructional context. The Consistent Adapter aggregates textual cues from the entire instruction sequence and from neighboring steps to keep object identity and attributes consistent across frames, and the Context-Aware Temporal Adapter converts cross-attention outputs into biases for temporal self-attention, explicitly propagating inter-frame relations. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance on step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality while significantly reducing noise, blur, and spurious subtitles. Our code and trained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [RayLift: Lifting Complementary Ray-Wise Evidence with 3D Geometry Priors for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08476)
*Meng Wang,Hongxia Yu,Wenzhe He,Xingdong Song,Huilong Pi,Jiapeng Zhang,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayLift是针对相机三维语义场景补全（SSC）的框架，将立体几何作为度量参考，同时引入互补的射线证据来自适应地恢复可靠的三维结构。它包含三个模块：互补上下文编码器、深度射线证据提升模块和语义感知体素集成器，在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的方法通常将立体深度估计视为确定性几何约束，导致深度不确定性和局部匹配误差直接传播到体素表示中，影响场景补全的准确性。

Method: 提出RayLift框架，包含三个核心组件：1）互补上下文编码器，从冻结的三维视觉基础模型中提取几何感知先验来丰富场景上下文；2）深度射线证据提升模块，联合建模几何不相似性、深度置信度和空间不确定性，沿每条相机射线自适应采样并加权候选表面位置；3）语义感知体素集成器，通过显式建模空间支撑将射线证据注入体素特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360基准上的大量实验表明，RayLift实现了竞争性性能并持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将立体几何作为度量参考而非确定性约束，并引入射线级证据的互补建模，有效缓解了深度不确定性与局部匹配误差带来的不利影响，提升了三维语义场景补全的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic scene completion (SSC) provides comprehensive scene understanding for autonomous driving and robotics. However, existing methods often treat stereo depth estimates as deterministic geometric constraints, causing depth uncertainty and local correspondence errors to propagate directly into voxel representations. To address this issue, we propose RayLift, a framework that uses stereo geometry as a metric reference while incorporating complementary ray evidence to recover reliable 3D structures adaptively. RayLift first employs a Complementary Context Encoder that extracts geometry-aware priors from a frozen 3D vision foundation model, thereby enriching the scene context. It then introduces a Depth Ray Evidence Lifter module that jointly models geometric dissimilarity, depth confidence, and spatial uncertainty to adaptively sample and weight candidate surface locations along each camera ray. Finally, a Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator injects the resulting ray evidence into voxel features by explicitly modeling their spatial support. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that RayLift achieves competitive performance and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [103] [RenderMatte: Exact-Alpha Rendering and Group-Relative Alignment for Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08487)
*Zecheng Ren,Yafei Hu,Jianing Zhao,Ruichen Cong,Qun Jin,Yiren Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RenderMatte，一种基于trimap引导的抠图框架，通过对FLUX.1 Kontext进行全参数微调来利用图像编辑先验进行结构保持的alpha预测。同时引入alpha-edge目标和group-relative alpha alignment后训练策略，并构建了包含精确线级alpha标注的大规模合成数据集RenderMatte dataset，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准的alpha估计在开放世界场景中仍然充满挑战，因为真实前景具有高度多样化的外观和不透明度模式，现有方法在语义模糊和细粒度不透明度变化上表现不佳，尤其是在稀疏边界区域难以监督。

Method: 1) 使用FLUX.1 Kontext进行全参数微调，适配为trimap引导的抠图框架；2) 引入alpha-edge目标函数，在监督适应阶段保留潜在流匹配信号的同时增强像素空间边界监督；3) 提出group-relative alpha alignment后训练策略，在同一trimap条件下采样多个matte，使用抠图特定奖励（alpha精度、边界保真度、trimap一致性、合成一致性）进行比较优化；4) 构建RenderMatte数据集，结合3D渲染RGBA前景和多源资产，提供精确的线级alpha标注和多样化背景合成。

Result: 在所有基准测试上均达到最先进性能，展示了面向开放世界高保真抠图的可扩展路径。

Conclusion: RenderMatte通过利用大规模图像编辑先验、精确边界的合成数据以及专门的训练策略，成功解决了开放世界场景中语义歧义和细粒度不透明度变化问题，证明了向高保真开放世界抠图的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Image matting is an essential enabling technology for modern visual content production, where foreground extraction determines the realism and editability of downstream creation workflows. However, precise alpha estimation in open-world scenes remains challenging because real foregrounds exhibit highly diverse appearances and opacity patterns. This makes existing methods struggle with semantic ambiguity and fine-grained opacity variation, especially in sparse boundary regions that are fragile and difficult to supervise. To address this gap, we present RenderMatte, a trimap-guided matting framework that adapts FLUX.1 Kontext through full-parameter fine-tuning, leveraging image editing priors for structure-preserving alpha prediction. During supervised adaptation, an alpha-edge objective preserves the latent flow-matching signal while strengthening pixel-space boundary supervision. We further introduce group-relative alpha alignment for post-training. It compares multiple mattes sampled under the same trimap condition using matting-specific rewards for alpha accuracy, boundary fidelity, trimap compliance, and compositional consistency. To overcome the lack of precise edge annotations, we construct the RenderMatte dataset, a large-scale synthetic dataset combining 3D-rendered RGBA foregrounds with diverse multi-source assets. It features exact strand-level alpha annotations and diverse background composites. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks, demonstrating a scalable path toward high-fidelity matting in open-world scenes.

</details>


### [104] [Linguistically-Aligned and Visually-Grounded Preference Optimization for Clinically-Augmented Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08494)
*Qiang Hu,Yuxuan Luo,Yingjie Guo,Hao Wang,Qimei Wang,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DPO-Clin，一个面向医学报告生成的定向偏好优化后训练框架，通过实体级临床诊断模块（ECD）隔离临床与语言差异、检索增强多模态DPO变体（M²DPO）实现细粒度跨模态对齐，以及反事实修改构造偏好数据进行隐态风险缓解，在MIMIC-CXR、IU X-Ray和内部内镜数据集上显著提升临床指标并优于现有DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO-based MRG方法采用朴素的偏好构造策略（直接将模型生成报告与真实报告配对），导致（1）临床关键发现与临床无关的语言特征被混为一谈，（2）缺乏显式的视觉-语言对齐，从而限制了模型生成报告的可靠性并滋生事实性错误。

Method: 提出DPO-Clin框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）实体级临床诊断（ECD）模块，进行精确的实体级事实诊断，引导生成语言对齐的报告偏好对，将临床差异与语言变异分离；（2）M²DPO，一种检索增强的多模态DPO变体，通过视觉上下文切换触发文本偏好反转，实现细粒度跨模态对齐；（3）定位正确但高不确定性的预测实体，施加反事实修改构造针对性偏好数据，用于隐态风险缓解。

Result: 在两个公开胸部X光数据集（MIMIC-CXR和IU X-Ray）及一个内部内镜数据集上的大量实验表明，DPO-Clin在临床感知指标上显著优于SFT基线，同时超越现有DPO-based MRG方法，在不同基线架构和多种医学影像模态上展现出较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现和跨模态对齐，而非泛化的语言风格匹配，能有效提升医学报告生成模型的可靠性；检索增强的多模态偏好反转与反事实风险缓解进一步增强了模型的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Medical Report Generation (MRG), the reliability remains constrained by the prevalence of factual errors. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm to enhance the performance of Supervised Fine-Tuned (SFT) MRG models, existing DPO-based MRG methods typically adopt a naive preference construction that directly pairs model-generated reports with ground truth reports. This strategy inadvertently entangles critical clinical findings with clinically irrelevant linguistic characteristics, and fundamentally lacks explicit vision-language alignment. To address these challenges, we propose DPO-Clin, a novel post-training framework that focuses preference optimization on clinical findings and cross-modal alignment. First, we introduce the Entity-level Clinical Diagnostic (ECD) module to perform a precise entity-level factual diagnosis. ECD guides the generation of linguistically-aligned report preference pairs, isolating clinical discrepancies from linguistic variations. Second, to achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment, we develop M$^2$DPO, a retrieval-augmented multi-modal DPO variant that enforces textual preference inversion triggered by visual context switches. Third, we locate correct yet highly uncertain predicted entities and apply counterfactual modifications to construct targeted preference data for latent risk mitigation, thereby further enhancing the model reliability. Extensive experiments on two public chest X-ray datasets (MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray) and an in-house endoscopy dataset demonstrate that DPO-Clin significantly improves the SFT baselines on clinical-aware metrics. Furthermore, it achieves superior performance over existing DPO-based MRG methods, exhibiting robust generalizability across distinct baseline architectures and diverse medical imaging modalities.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Adaptive Super-Resolution and Quality Assessment via Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08508)
*Ajeet Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本篇博士论文研究在真实世界条件下，通过测试时自适应（TTA）来提升视频超分辨率的鲁棒性和感知质量。核心贡献包括：1）基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估框架，为VSR提供感知引导；2）面向屏幕内容的Transformer超分架构，保持文字清晰度与结构保真；3）区域感知的TTA策略，在无需高清真值的情况下选择性优化文本与非文本区域。实验证明感知质量和可读性均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频超分辨率（VSR）方法在面对异构设备、不同编码器和网络环境造成的未知退化时泛化能力不足，难以在真实场景下保持良好性能。

Method: 采用测试时自适应（TTA）作为统一范式，包含三个技术要点：基于TTA的无参考VQA框架提供感知质量引导；Transformer架构用于屏幕内容超分以保持文字清晰与结构保真；区域感知TTA策略区分文本与非文本区域进行选择性精化，全程无需重训练或高清监督。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验显示，感知质量和可读性均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: TTA是一种无需重训练即可提升VSR鲁棒性的有效范式；未来工作聚焦于构建能在未知领域间泛化的全自适应视频增强系统。

Abstract: This paper presents doctoral research on adaptive video super-resolution and perceptual quality modeling under real-world conditions. Existing video super-resolution (VSR) methods struggle to generalize under unknown degradations arising from heterogeneous devices, codecs, and network environments. We address this challenge through test-time adaptation (TTA), a unified paradigm that improves robustness and perceptual quality without retraining or high-quality supervision. Specifically, we: 1) propose a TTA-based framework for no-reference video quality assessment (VQA), where adapted quality predictions provide perceptual guidance for VSR under unseen distortions; 2) develop a transformer-based architecture for screen-content super-resolution that preserves text clarity and structural fidelity; and 3) introduce a region-aware TTA strategy that selectively refines text and non-text regions without requiring high-resolution ground truth. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in perceptual quality and readability. We also outline ongoing work toward fully adaptive video enhancement systems capable of generalizing across unseen domains.

</details>


### [106] [eBIRD: Event-based Intensity Image Reconstruction Using Controllable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08519)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Fabian Valderrama,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出eBIRD框架，将去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）与ControlNet条件机制相结合，用于事件流驱动的强度图像重建。在N-MNIST和RGBE-Gaze数据集上使用33ms事件窗口进行测试，结果显示N-MNIST上通用模型表现最佳（MSE 0.0052，SSIM 0.8982，PSNR 23.34dB），而RGBE-Gaze上专用模型更优（MSE 0.0161，SSIM 0.7605，PSNR 19.08dB），表明可控扩散模型是事件引导重建的有前景方法，但最优学习策略取决于重建领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机产生的二元、稀疏、异步事件流数据使得从事件流重建强度图像极具挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾重建质量与事件数据的时序特性，需要探索新的重建框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出eBIRD框架，将DDPM（去噪扩散概率模型）与基于ControlNet的条件生成机制相结合，利用事件流作为条件引导扩散模型进行强度图像重建。同时对比分析了通用扩散学习策略与专用扩散学习策略在不同数据集上的表现。

Result: 在手写数字数据集N-MNIST上，通用模型达到最佳重建质量（MSE 0.0052，SSIM 0.8982，PSNR 23.34dB）；在面部数据集RGBE-Gaze上，专用模型表现最优（MSE 0.0161，SSIM 0.7605，PSNR 19.08dB）。两数据集均使用33ms事件窗口。

Conclusion: 可控扩散模型是事件引导强度图像重建的一种有前景的方法，但通用与专用学习策略的选择依赖具体重建领域，不存在放之四海而皆准的最佳策略。

Abstract: Intensity-image reconstruction from event streams remains a challenging problem due to the binary, sparse, and asynchronous nature of event data. This work proposes eBIRD, an event-guided reconstruction framework that combines a DDPM with ControlNet-based conditioning. We analyze generic and specialized diffusion learning strategies for handwritten digit (N-MNIST) and face (RGBE-Gaze) reconstruction using 33ms event windows. On N-MNIST, the general model achieves the best reconstruction quality (MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB), whereas the specialized model performs best on RGBE-Gaze (MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB). These preliminary results suggest that controllable diffusion models are a promising approach for event-guided intensity-image reconstruction, while highlighting that the preferred learning strategy depends on the reconstruction domain.

</details>


### [107] [A Combined Feature-Based Framework for Disguise and Spoofing Detection in Face Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08521)
*Sangiya Pararajasingham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了五种特征提取与分类流水线（PM、LPM、HPM、SM、HM），在统一框架内同时解决人脸识别的两大失效模式：照片/视频欺骗（spoofing）和伪装（disguise）。实验表明，基于HOG的HPM流水线在伪装与姿态/光照变化下表现最稳定（混合伪装94.59%、姿态与光照81.5-93.2%、欺骗检测91.67%），而基于LBP的LPM流水线在欺骗检测上准确率最高（93.2%）但对姿态变化鲁棒性较差，揭示了经典特征表示在欺骗敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间存在可量化的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统面临两个通常被分开处理的不同失效模式：欺骗攻击（冒名者使用授权用户的照片或视频）和伪装识别（合法用户因配饰、胡须、光照或姿态等外观变化被拒绝）。现有研究往往将这两个问题分开处理，作者希望在一个统一框架内同时应对这两个挑战。

Method: 基于统一的二阶段流程（预处理、特征提取、特征过滤、分类），提出并比较了五种流水线：PM（PCA+最小欧氏距离）、LPM（局部二值模式LBP+PCA+MED）、HPM（方向梯度直方图HOG+PCA+MED）、SM（SURF特征+MED）和HM（Harris角点特征+MED）。使用FEI、伪装人脸数据库和NUAA数据库中的115个受试者进行训练，在六种测试条件下评估。

Result: HPM在混合伪装条件下准确率为94.59%，在姿态与光照变体下为81.5-93.2%，欺骗检测为91.67%，综合表现最稳定；LPM在欺骗检测上达到最高准确率93.2%，但对姿态变化的鲁棒性较弱。结果显示欺骗敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间存在可测量的权衡。

Conclusion: 经典特征表示在欺骗检测与伪装鲁棒性之间存在固有权衡，HOG特征在综合性能上表现更优。这一发现为后续的深度学习与跨数据库扩展提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: Face recognition systems face two distinct, commonly-separated failure modes: spoofing, where an impostor presents a photograph or video of an authorized user, and disguise, where a legitimate user is rejected because their appearance differs from their enrolled template due to accessories, facial hair, illumination, or pose. This paper proposes and compares five combined feature-extraction and classification pipelines that address both problems within a single framework: PM (PCA and Minimum Euclidean Distance, MED), LPM (Local Binary Patterns with PCA and MED), HPM (Histogram of Oriented Gradients with PCA and MED), SM (Speeded-Up Robust Features with MED), and HM (Harris corner features with MED). Each pipeline follows a common two-phase process comprising pre-processing, feature extraction, feature filtering, and classification. The methods were trained on 115 subjects drawn from the FEI, Disguised Faces Database, and NUAA databases and evaluated on six test conditions covering mixed appearances, frontal faces, dark illumination, left- and right-turned poses, and photo-spoofing attempts. The HOG-based pipeline (HPM) achieved the most consistent performance across conditions, with 94.59% accuracy on mixed-appearance disguise, 81.5-93.2% across pose and illumination variants, and 91.67% on spoofing, while the LBP-based pipeline (LPM) achieved the highest spoofing-detection accuracy (93.2%) but weaker robustness to pose change. These results reveal a measurable trade-off between spoof sensitivity and disguise robustness among classical feature representations, motivating the deep-learning and cross-database extensions discussed in the concluding sections.

</details>


### [108] [ERF-GS: Reconstructing Fast Motion from Disjoint Event-RGB Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08531)
*Xiaoyang Bai,Zhenyang Li,Weiwei Xu,Edmund Y. Lam,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了事件-RGB融合高斯泼溅（ERF-GS）框架，将事件信息集成到高斯泼溅的优化和致密化阶段，解决了快速运动物体的动态3D场景重建难题，在复杂真实视频场景中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于神经辐射场和3D高斯泼溅的动态场景重建方法在视觉精度和可扩展性上取得突破，但对快速运动物体（如体育赛事、动物拍摄）的重建仍具挑战，传统基于帧的视频方法在低帧率、运动模糊等场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出ERF-GS（事件-RGB融合高斯泼溅）框架，将事件数据集成到高斯泼溅管线的优化阶段和致密化阶段两个环节中，利用新型事件传感器的高帧率优势。该方法采用现实模拟设置开发，实现事件学习与RGB输入的分离，使其能应用于复杂的自然视频（包括复杂布局、低帧率和严重运动模糊）。

Result: 在Neu3D和Nvidia数据集的不同变体（包含模糊RGB帧和分离的RGB-事件视点）上，ERF-GS在性能上优于4DGS基线和同期方法E-D3DGS。

Conclusion: ERF-GS通过将事件信息同时纳入优化与致密化阶段，成功实现了从合成数据到自然视频的泛化，为解决快速运动动态场景重建提供了有效方案，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-driven representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) have revolutionized the field of dynamic 3D scene reconstruction with improved visual precision and scalability. However, the reconstruction of fast-moving objects remains a challenge; existing methods based on conventional frame-based videos often struggle in scenarios such as sports events and animal videography. We propose an event-RGB fusion Gaussian splatting (ERF-GS) framework that integrates event information into both optimization and densification stages of the Gaussian splatting pipeline, taking advantage of novel event sensors with high frame-rate. Unlike many other event-assisted scene reconstruction methods, ERF-GS was developed using realistic simulation settings and realizes event-based learning detached from RGB inputs. This design enables its application beyond straightforward synthetic data into the realm of natural video with complex layout, low frame rates and severe motion blur. Our experiments show that ERF-GS outperforms both the 4DGS baseline and the concurrent E-D3DGS on different variants of the Neu3D and Nvidia datasets which include blurry RGB frames and disjoint RGB-event viewpoints. Our code is available at https://github.com/andrewbxy/ERF-GS.

</details>


### [109] [Rethinking Attention Locality in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08541)
*Zeqi Zheng,Zizheng Zhu,Yuping Yan,Wenxuan Pan,Zhaofei Yu,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCLA-BCP方法，通过空间连续局部注意力与边界连续性路径增强脉冲Transformer的空间局部性，并采用层级化局部部署策略适配不同架构，在多个静态和神经形态数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲Transformer中的无Softmax脉冲自注意力机制难以建立空间局部化的token交互。尽管已有的局部性增强方法能提升精度，但作者通过平均注意力距离(MAD)分析揭示：计算局部性并不必然转化为空间局部性，且统一应用相同的局部性增强忽略了不同脉冲Transformer架构的部署需求差异。

Method: 提出SCLA-BCP：(1) SCLA在空间相邻token的非重叠区域内计算注意力；(2) BCP通过轻量卷积路径促进跨边界信息交换；(3) 开发层级化局部性部署策略，将SCLA-BCP有效应用于两种主流脉冲Transformer架构。

Result: 在7个静态和神经形态数据集上的分类、检测和分割任务中均取得一致改进，且参数量和能耗开销有限。具体而言，在COCO 2017上mAP@50最高提升9.50%，在ADE20K上mIoU最高提升3.42%。可视化、MAD分析及消融实验进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SCLA-BCP有效解决了脉冲Transformer中计算局部性与空间局部性不一致的问题，通过空间连续局部注意力与边界连续性路径结合层级化部署策略，以极小的代价显著提升下游任务性能，为脉冲Transformer的局部性建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers provide a promising paradigm for efficient visual processing with spike-driven computation, yet their Softmax-free Spiking Self-Attention (SSA) struggles to establish spatially localized token interactions. Although existing locality-enhanced SSA methods improve accuracy, it remains unclear whether they consistently induce spatial locality across layers and different Spiking Transformer architectures. Through Mean Attention Distance (MAD) analysis, we reveal that computational locality does not necessarily translate into spatial locality and show that uniformly applying the same locality enhancement overlooks architecture-dependent deployment requirements. Motivated by these observations, we propose Spatially Contiguous Local Attention with Boundary Continuity Pathway (SCLA-BCP). SCLA computes attention within non-overlapping regions of spatially adjacent tokens, while BCP facilitates cross-boundary information exchange through a lightweight convolutional pathway. Furthermore, we develop a hierarchical locality deployment strategy to effectively apply SCLA-BCP across the two major Spiking Transformer architectures. Extensive experiments on seven static and neuromorphic datasets covering classification, detection, and segmentation demonstrate consistent improvements with limited parameter and energy overhead. Notably, our approach improves mAP@50 by up to 9.50% on COCO 2017 and mIoU by up to 3.42% on ADE20K. Visualizations, MAD analysis, and ablation studies further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [110] [MotionCraft: Latent World Modeling with Sparse Attention for Visual Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08553)
*Rong Fu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Xiaowen Ma,Yongtai Liu,Wangyu Wu,Shuo Yin,Zijian Zhang,Sicheng Li,Yingrui Ji,Chenhao Wang,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCraft是一个可控的视频超分辨率框架，将恢复问题建模为受运动驱动的隐状态预测（受世界模型启发），结合自适应稀疏注意力与用户可控接口，在流式约束下实现时间一致的高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VSR方法在局部细节保真、长程时空建模、感知真实性和效率之间存在权衡：卷积对齐方法在大运动或复杂退化下失效，Transformer方法计算开销大，扩散/潜在生成方法需专门的时间约束维持一致性。亟需一个在各项指标间可控可调的统一框架。

Method: MotionCraft包含三个核心组件：1）鲁棒运动融合模块处理帧间运动对齐；2）潜在世界变换器（Latent World Transformer）平衡局部性与定向非局部交互，采用自适应稀疏注意力降低计算复杂度；3）紧凑条件解码器在流式约束下输出高质量重建。同时提供显式用户可访问的控制接口，实现时间平滑度与重建保真度之间的可预测权衡。

Result: 实验评估表明MotionCraft在重建质量和感知性能上均表现优异，同时能够实现时间平滑度与重建保真度之间的可预测权衡。

Conclusion: MotionCraft验证了将VSR形式化为运动感知的隐状态预测（世界模型范式）的有效性，为视频超分辨率提供了一种兼顾质量、效率与可控性的全新解决方案，尤其适用于流式应用场景。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) aims to recover high-fidelity high-resolution videos from low-resolution inputs and is central to applications ranging from mobile capture to streaming and archival restoration. Existing approaches trade off among local-detail fidelity, long-range spatio-temporal modeling, perceptual realism, and efficiency: convolutional alignment techniques preserve local structure but suffer when motion is large or degradations are complex; transformer-based methods capture long-range dependencies yet require architectural or algorithmic adaptations to remain computationally feasible; and recent latent or diffusion-based generators synthesize rich texture but require specialized temporal constraints to maintain coherence. We present MotionCraft, a controllable VSR framework that formulates restoration as motion-aware latent state prediction inspired by world models and integrates adaptive sparse attention with an explicit user-accessible control interface. MotionCraft combines robust motion fusion, a Latent World Transformer that balances locality and targeted non-local interactions, and a compact conditional decoder to deliver temporally consistent, high-quality reconstructions under streaming constraints. Empirical evaluations show that MotionCraft achieves strong reconstruction and perceptual performance while enabling predictable trade-offs between temporal smoothness and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [111] [SC-Diff: Semantically Calibrated Diffusion for Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08555)
*Junyin Zhang,Siyu Huang,Jianxiong Ye,Haowei Gong,Ruicheng Zhang,Deyu Meng,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SC-Diff，一个语义校准的潜在扩散框架，用于可见光到红外的图像翻译。它利用SAM3提取的语义掩码同时作为条件输入和内部自注意力校准信号，通过Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration（SGSC）机制，对同类别query-key对施加自适应正偏置，减少跨类别干扰，提升红外图像生成中的语义一致性和物体定位与形状保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光到红外图像翻译可以利用丰富的可见光图像扩充红外训练数据。现有基于扩散模型的方法仅将语义先验作为外部条件使用，没有显式调控去噪网络内部的token交互，导致生成的图像难以保持物体的位置、形状和语义布局，无法可靠地复用标注信息。

Method: 1）使用预训练的SAM3模型配合预定义文本提示，从可见光图像中提取类别特定的语义掩码；2）将语义掩码合并为语义图，并与可见光图像融合作为输入条件；3）将语义图转换为token级别的语义标签，用于校准去噪网络中的自注意力；4）提出Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration（SGSC），根据注意力在语义类别上的离散程度和query自身类别的注意力分配，自适应地调节校准强度，对同类别query-key对施加正偏置；5）原始注意力分数进一步调节偏置幅度，使响应更强的同类别key获得更大校准。

Result: 大量实验表明，SC-Diff显著提升了生成红外图像的感知质量，并为下游红外目标检测提供了更有效的合成训练数据。

Conclusion: 软校准方法在减少跨类别干扰的同时保留了全局上下文交互，从而提升了生成红外图像的语义一致性。该工作验证了将语义先验同时用于条件引导和内部注意力校准的有效性。

Abstract: Visible-to-infrared image translation provides a practical way to expand infrared training data using abundant visible images. Diffusion models are promising for this task because of their strong generative performance. However, existing diffusion-based methods typically use semantic priors only as external conditions, without explicitly regulating token interactions within the denoising network. Consequently, they struggle to preserve object locations, shapes, and semantic layouts required for reliable annotation reuse. We propose SC-Diff, a semantically calibrated latent diffusion framework that uses semantic priors for both conditional guidance and internal self-attention calibration. A pretrained SAM3 model with predefined text prompts first extracts category-specific semantic masks from visible images. These masks are merged into a semantic map and fused with the visible image as the input condition. The same map is converted into token-level semantic labels to calibrate self-attention in the denoising network. Based on these labels, we introduce Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration (SGSC), which adaptively applies positive biases to query-key pairs of the same category. The query-wise calibration strength depends on the dispersion of attention across semantic categories and the attention assigned to the query's own category. The original attention scores further modulate the bias, giving greater calibration to same-category keys with stronger responses. This soft calibration reduces cross-category interference while retaining global contextual interactions, thereby improving semantic consistency in generated infrared images. Extensive experiments show that SC-Diff improves perceptual quality and produces more effective synthetic training data for downstream infrared object detection.

</details>


### [112] [On-Device Multi-Species Malaria Detection with Uncertainty-Calibrated Slide-Level Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08566)
*Idaya Seidu,Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Charles B. Delahunt,Carine Mukamakuza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向资源有限地区临床部署的疟疾自动诊断流水线，针对国家卫生中心提出的六项临床约束（停止准则、人机协同、多物种判别、厚血膜检测、不确定性计算、边缘设备部署），基于YOLOv13n和TensorFlow Lite实现全离线设备端推理，在2,739张标注图像上达到mAP@0.5为0.863的检测性能，并实现符合WHO标准的虫血症量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在资源有限地区仍是主要致死原因，但专家显微镜师稀缺。自动诊断具有改善医疗服务的潜力，然而现有机器学习疟疾文献大多忽略了从临床角度至关重要的非显而易见约束（如停止准则、人工复核、多物种区分等），导致算法难以真正落地部署。作者与国家级卫生中心紧密合作，识别并满足这些被忽视的临床要求。

Method: 系统采用YOLOv13n作为检测模型，通过TensorFlow Lite部署于边缘设备实现全端设备推理。流水线处理Giemsa染色的厚血膜图像，检测四种疟原虫物种及白细胞，并将逐图像检测结果聚合为玻片级别的虫血症量化（采用WHO标准），同时集成了停止准则以缩短采集时间和出结果时间、人机协同接口、多物种判别、厚膜检测支持、计算高效的不确定性估计以及适合网络不稳定地区的离线运行机制。

Result: 在包含所有四种物种的2,739张标注图像上评估：mAP@0.5达到0.863，逐图像疟原虫计数相关性r=0.812，玻片级相关性r=0.951（软计数，每玻片10张图像）；系统完全离线运行，单张图像流水线耗时10.27±1.65秒。

Conclusion: 本文强调了临床部署中常被机器学习研究忽视的关键约束，并提供了相应的解决方法和完整流水线实现，展示了如何在满足临床实际要求的前提下实现高性能的自动疟疾诊断，为类似资源受限场景下的医学AI系统落地提供了参考范式。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in resource-limited settings, where expert microscopists are scarce. Automated diagnosis based on microscopy images thus has strong potential to improve care delivery. But for an algorithm to deploy, a necessary requirement is that it meet a suite of non-obvious (from a machine learning (ML) perspective) clinical constraints. Therefore, in close consultation with a national health center we developed a malaria diagnosis pipeline which addresses key requirements listed by the health care center but typically ignored in the ML malaria literature. In particular, it includes: (i) stopping criteria (to reduce image acquisition and time-to-result); (ii) human-in-the-loop functionality (for review and accountability); (iii) multi-species discrimination (since treatment varies by species); (iv) thick film detection (standard for microscopy); (v) computationally-efficient uncertainty calculations (to aid clinician review); and (vi) an edge device platform (since internet can be spotty in this catchment area). The mobile system performs all inference on-device using YOLOv13n deployed via TensorFlow Lite. It detects four species and white blood cells from Giemsa-stained thick blood smear images, aggregating per-image detections into slide-level parasitemia with World Health Organization (WHO)-standard quantification. This paper highlights these various clinical constraints and offers methods to address them. Evaluated on 2,739 annotated images across all four species, the system achieves mAP@0.5 of 0.863, per-image parasite count correlation of r = 0.812, slide-level r = 0.951 (soft counting, 10 images/slide), and runs entirely offline with a pipeline time of 10.27 +- 1.65 s per image.

</details>


### [113] [CDGC-Net: 3D Medical Image Segmentation with Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention and Grouped Channel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08575)
*Zheyang Jing,Qin Lu,Jianwang Li,Yujie Yang,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CDGC-Net，一种3D医学图像分割网络，通过协同双尺度空间注意力（CDSA）与分组分层通道注意力（GHCA）在同一特征层面融合长程解剖上下文与细粒度边界细节，共享关键投影以保持特征一致性。在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA数据集上取得领先的DSC分数，同时参数和FLOPs相比UNETR++分别降低39.87%和40.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学图像分割方法通常在分离的模块或特征层面建模全局和局部特征，并独立进行通道重校准，导致全局上下文与局部边界之间的语义不匹配、通道关系建模不足、空间-通道交互弱以及表征冗余等问题。

Method: CDGC-Net的核心是CDGC块，包含两个模块：1) 协同双尺度自注意力（CDSA），将注意力头分配到并行的局部窗口分支和全局稀疏分支，在同一特征层面同时捕获细粒度空间细节和长程解剖上下文；2) 分组分层通道注意力（GHCA），将通道组织为r个组，建模组内和组间依赖关系。CDSA和GHCA共享关键（Key）投影以维持一致的特征参考，并通过残差特征对齐将精炼特征与原始表征整合。

Result: 在四个数据集上取得最优表现：Synapse DSC 86.96%、ACDC 92.91%、BraTS 82.56%、LA 93.52%，分别超过次优结果0.39、0.47、0.17和0.32个百分点。模型参数25.83M、FLOPs 28.62G（输入64×128×128），相比UNETR++分别减少39.87%和40.30%。

Conclusion: CDGC-Net在分割精度与计算复杂度之间取得了良好的权衡，证明了在同一特征层面协同建模空间和通道信息、并共享投影策略的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D medical image segmentation requires the integration of long-range anatomical context with fine boundary detail. Existing methods often model global and local features in separate modules or feature levels and perform channel recalibration independently. This may cause semantic mismatch between global context and local boundaries, insufficient channel relationship modeling, weak spatial-channel interaction, and redundant representations. We propose CDGC-Net, a 3D medical image segmentation network that combines cooperative dual-scale spatial attention with grouped hierarchical channel modeling. With-in each CDGC block, Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention (CDSA) assigns attention heads to parallel local-window and global-sparse branches. The two branches capture fine spatial details and long-range anatomical context at the same feature level. Their outputs are concatenated into an $N\times C$ spatial representation and directly passed to Grouped Hierarchical Channel Attention (GHCA). GHCA organizes the channels into $r$ groups and models both within-group and cross-group dependencies. CDSA and GHCA reuse a shared key projection to maintain a consistent feature reference. Residual feature alignment subsequently integrates the refined features with the original representation. On the Synapse, ACDC, BraTS, and LA datasets, CDGC-Net achieved mean DSC values of 86.96\%, 92.91\%, 82.56\%, and 93.52\%, respectively, exceeding the next-highest reported values by 0.39, 0.47, 0.17, and 0.32 percentage points. CDGC-Net contains 25.83M parameters and 28.62G FLOPs for an input size of $64\times128\times128$, reducing these quantities by 39.87\% and 40.30\%, respectively, relative to UNETR++. These results indicate a favorable trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>


### [114] [Where Is the Bee? Detecting Tiny Pollinators with a Single Collaborative-Head Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08580)
*Junsu Kim,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot挑战赛的解决方案，通过对比10种检测器配置选择Co-DINO+Swin-L作为骨干，采用crop-mosaic池和类别加权ETF损失解决小目标与类别不平衡问题，最终以0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95]在FinalTest上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛数据存在两大难点：目标框极小（中位数仅占帧面积的0.16%），以及蜜蜂类占据80%标签导致的类别严重不平衡。需要针对性地设计检测方案以提升对蜜蜂、熊蜂、食蚜蝇和蛾子四类目标的检测性能。

Method: 1) 在保留关键帧上对比10种检测器配置，选择mAP最高的Co-DINO（Swin-L骨干）；2) 通过crop-mosaic池微调，使3个稀有类别的组合标注占比从19.9%提升至55.1%；3) 采用类别加权simplex等角紧框架（ETF）损失，将匹配的decoder查询投影状态拉向固定类别方向；4) 完整训练计划共12+3+2个epoch。

Result: 在不使用推理时集成或测试时增强的情况下，在FinalTest上以0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95]排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的检测器配置对比、数据增强策略（crop-mosaic）以及类别加权ETF损失，有效解决了极小目标检测和类别不平衡两个核心挑战，证明了该方案在野外昆虫检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: The CVPPA@ECCV 2026 BuzzSpot Challenge asks us to detect bees, bumblebees, hoverflies, and moths in 1920x1080 field keyframes. Its annotations carry 2 difficulties: the median box occupies 0.16% of a frame, and bees account for 80% of the labels. To cope with the small boxes, we compare 10 recorded detector configurations on held-out keyframes; plain Co-DINO with a Swin-L backbone has the highest mAP in this comparison, so we select it. Training then addresses the bee dominance in 2 ways: fine-tuning on a crop-mosaic pool in which the combined annotation share of the 3 rare classes rises from 19.9% to 55.1%, and a class-weighted simplex equiangular tight frame (ETF) loss that pulls the projected states of matched decoder queries toward fixed class directions. The full schedule spans 12+3+2 epochs. Without inference-time ensembling or test-time augmentation, we rank first on FinalTest at 0.5062 mAP@[.5:.95].

</details>


### [115] [EvTrajGS: Accurate and Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08585)
*Zixuan Chen,Jiakai Zhang,Junhao Dong,Guangcong Wang,Jianhuang Lai,Yew-Soon Ong,Xiaohua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvTrajGS 是一个面向无位姿事件流的3D高斯溅射框架，通过将相机运动参数化为连续时间轨迹并从粗位姿先验初始化，实现联合位姿-场景优化，在保持高计算效率的同时，在几何重建质量和位姿估计精度上均超越现有方法（PSNR提升3.8 dB，SSIM提升0.1，ATE RMSE降低超过40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机密集3D重建方法存在两难：基于现成位姿估计的传统方法虽高效但位姿初始化不准确导致累积重建误差、重建保真度低；而SLAM式方法通过增量式跟踪与建图稳定联合优化，重建保真度高但计算开销大。作者旨在解决这一效率与精度的权衡问题。

Method: EvTrajGS提出三个核心组件：(1) 将相机运动参数化为从离散相机位姿初始化的连续时间轨迹，为位姿精化提供统一表示；(2) 将相邻轨迹状态聚合为时间耦合位姿，在联合优化中促进时间一致的位姿更新；(3) 引入损失重加权的事件采样策略，自适应地强调时间上欠重建的区间。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，EvTrajGS在几何重建质量和位姿估计精度上均优于现有最优方法，PSNR提升3.8 dB，SSIM提升0.1，ATE RMSE降低超过40%，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过连续时间轨迹参数化和时间耦合的位姿更新策略，可以在无需SLAM式增量管线的前提下，从粗位姿先验实现可靠的联合位姿-场景优化，有效弥合了传统方法与SLAM式方法在效率与精度的权衡鸿沟。

Abstract: Event cameras, with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and asynchronous sensing characteristics, have shown great potential for dense 3D reconstruction. Traditional reconstruction methods based on off-the-shelf pose estimates achieve high efficiency but produce low-fidelity results, as inaccurate pose initialization introduces cumulative reconstruction errors. In contrast, recent SLAM-style methods stabilize joint pose-scene optimization through incremental tracking and mapping, yielding higher reconstruction fidelity at the expense of considerable computational overhead. To address this trade-off, this paper presents EvTrajGS, an accurate and efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for unposed event streams. Our method enables reliable joint pose-scene optimization initialized from coarse pose priors, eliminating the need for computationally expensive SLAM-style pipelines. EvTrajGS parameterizes camera motion as a continuous-time trajectory initialized from discrete camera poses, providing a unified representation for pose refinement. We then aggregate adjacent trajectory states into a temporally coupled pose, promoting temporally consistent pose updates during joint optimization. Additionally, we introduce a loss-reweighted event sampling strategy to adaptively emphasize temporally under-reconstructed intervals. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that EvTrajGS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of both geometric reconstruction quality and pose estimation accuracy, achieving 3.8 dB higher PSNR, 0.1 higher SSIM, and over 40\% lower ATE RMSE while retaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [116] [RobustDefect-LLM: Explainable and Robustness-Aware Industrial Surface Defect Classification with Decision Support and AI-Assisted Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08589)
*Nazlıcan Düşünmez,Halûk Gümüşkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustDefect-LLM，一个集成深度学习分类、视觉证据、置信度感知决策、AI辅助报告、可追溯存储和移动交互的工业表面缺陷检测框架。在NEU-DET数据集上，MobileNetV3-Large取得了最高测试准确率（99.26%）和宏F1分数（0.9926），但在强图像退化下准确率降至40%以下。保守的人机协作策略在33个案例中实现100%选择性准确率，并成功路由了两个观测到的分类错误至人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表面缺陷检测需要从单纯的高精度模型拓展为一体化质量控制系统，整合分类能力、可视化证据、置信度决策支持、报告生成、数据追溯和移动端交互，以满足实际工厂部署中对可靠性和人机协作的综合需求。

Method: 作者评估了四种基于迁移学习的CNN模型（ResNet50、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet121、MobileNetV3-Large），在NEU-DET六类数据集的1,799张图像上进行训练和验证。采用bootstrap置信区间和精确配对McNemar检验进行统计比较。模型基于置信度低于0.90或top-2间隔低于0.10的策略路由至人工审查。使用Grad-CAM提供视觉证据，并在受控条件下生成和验证100份报告。

Result: MobileNetV3-Large达到最高数值测试准确率99.26%和宏F1=0.9926，bootstrap 95% CI为0.9815-1.0000。McNemar检验显示与DenseNet121无显著差异（p=1.000）。CPU推理时间0.060秒/帧（16.66 FPS）。在合成退化下，轻度退化准确率降至87.78%，强度退化时低于40%。保守审查策略提供12.22%自动覆盖率，33个合格案例中取得100%选择性准确率（95% CI: 89.43%-100.00%），并成功路由两个错误至审查。100份报告均通过确定性一致性检查，平均时延1.66秒。

Conclusion: 集成工作流在受控条件下具备可行性，但结果强调需要模型校准、可重复评估以及真实工业场景验证。模型在严重图像质量退化下的性能显著下降，凸显了鲁棒性测试和人工审查机制的重要性。

Abstract: This paper presents RobustDefect-LLM, an industrial surface-defect inspection framework integrating deep-learning classification, operator-facing visual evidence, confidence-aware decision support, controlled AI-assisted reporting, traceable storage, and mobile interaction in a unified quality-control workflow. Here, robustness-aware denotes explicit evaluation under controlled image degradation and confidence-aware review routing, not an intrinsic robustness guarantee. Four transfer-learning-based convolutional neural networks, ResNet50, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet121, and MobileNetV3-Large, were evaluated on 1,799 images from the six-class NEU-DET dataset using fixed training, validation, and held-out in-domain test partitions. MobileNetV3-Large achieved the highest numerical test accuracy (99.26%) and macro F1-score (0.9926), with a bootstrap 95% accuracy CI of 0.9815-1.0000. An exact paired McNemar test found no significant difference from DenseNet121 (p = 1.000). The selected model averaged 0.060 s per CPU forward pass (16.66 FPS). Under combined synthetic degradation, accuracy fell to 87.78% at mild intensity and below 40% at stronger intensities, revealing sensitivity to severe image-quality deterioration. Grad-CAM supplied visual evidence, while predictions with confidence below 0.90 or a top-2 margin below 0.10 were routed to HUMAN REVIEW. This conservative policy provided 12.22% automatic coverage and 100% observed selective accuracy among 33 eligible cases (95% CI: 89.43%-100.00%), while routing both observed classification errors to review. Under nominal controlled conditions, all 100 generated reports passed deterministic consistency checks, with a mean latency of 1.66 s. Results support the feasibility of the integrated workflow while emphasizing the need for calibration, repeated evaluation, and real-world industrial validation.

</details>


### [117] [Goal-oriented Navigation Instruction Generation with Tour Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08596)
*Fangdi Li,Juncheng Liao,Changxu Cheng,Jiazhi Wang,Senda Chen,Tao Wang,Wuyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoNIG，一个基于视频的目标导向导航指令生成任务，从第一视角导览视频、初始观察和文本/视觉目标生成导航指令，无需借助图或地图等中间表示。作者构建了一个包含60K导览视频和37K多模态提示的受控模拟器基准，并提出了两阶段课程学习框架来训练模型，实验证明该方法优于现有MLLM基线且生成指令可直接用于下游VLN代理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航指令生成（NIG）主要作为VLN的辅助任务用于数据增强或多任务学习，且依赖于图、地图等中间表示。然而，从紧凑的环境先验中生成导航指令需要精细的空间推理，尤其当目标路线不单纯遵循示范导览路径时，这对当前多模态模型仍是巨大挑战。作者旨在探索不依赖中间表示、直接从第一视角视频生成导航指令的可行性。

Method: 1) 任务定义：VideoNIG，从第一视角导览视频+初始观察+目标（文本或视觉）生成导航指令，无图无地图。2) 基准构建：在连续室内环境的受控模拟器中生成60K导览视频和37K多模态提示，难度渐进。3) 诊断评估协议：结合文本相似度、基于选择的时空一致性测试和下游导航执行。4) 模型方法：两阶段课程学习框架，第一阶段Action Warmup进行空间动作-视角对齐，第二阶段Complexity Progression使用递增探索难度的轨迹进行训练。

Result: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在VideoNIG任务上表现不佳，而提出的课程学习框架在互补的诊断指标上显著提升了指令质量。此外，将VideoNIG生成的指令与VLN代理结合，验证了该任务定义可用于端到端导航执行。

Conclusion: VideoNIG证明了一种更轻量级的导航指令生成范式——无需图/地图等中间表示，仅依赖第一视角视频和初始观察即可生成可执行的导航指令。两阶段课程学习有效缓解了MLLM在长视野空间推理上的不足，且其生成质量可直接支撑下游导航执行，为NIG和VLN研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Navigation Instruction Generation (NIG) aims to produce step-by-step natural language instructions for navigation guidance. Existing studies primarily treat NIG as an auxiliary task for vision-andlanguage navigation (VLN), focusing on data augmentation or multi-task learning. However, generating navigation instructions from compact environmental priors requires meticulous spatial reasoning, especially when the target route does not simply follow the demonstrated tour, and remains challenging for current multimodal models. In this work, we introduce VideoNIG, a goal-oriented video-grounded NIG task that generates navigation instructions from ego-centric tour videos, an initial observation, and a textual or visual goal, without relying on intermediate representations such as graphs and maps. We instantiate VideoNIG in a controlled simulator benchmark with 60K tour videos across continuous indoor environments and 37K multimodal prompts with progressive difficulty levels. We further introduce a diagnostic evaluation protocol that combines text similarity, choice-based spatial consistency tests, and downstream navigation execution. To address this task, we propose a two-stage Curriculum Learning framework that decomposes the learning into foundational motion perception and long-horizon navigation reasoning. Specifically, we first employ Action Warmup for spatial action-view alignment, followed by Complexity Progression using trajectories with increasing exploratory difficulty. Extensive experiments show that existing MLLMs struggle with VideoNIG, while our approach significantly improves instruction quality across complementary diagnostic metrics. Finally, integrating VideoNIG-generated instructions with a VLN agent demonstrates the executability of this task formulation for end-to-end navigation.

</details>


### [118] [Population-Scalable Multi-Agent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08600)
*Renjie Zhao,Yuxiang Wu,Mingyu Zhang,Jiaxin Li,Sisi Li,Yimin Sheng,Tianxi Tan,Zhenkai Zhang,Jianyi Zhu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Khora，一种可扩展的多智能体世界模型，通过将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，支持推理阶段任意数量智能体的扩展而无需重新训练，实现了近似线性的实际扩展和跨视角一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在多智能体环境下假设训练和推理时智能体数量固定，将模型绑定到预定智能体群体，限制了推理时的可扩展性，这是一个根本性的扩展性挑战。

Method: 核心洞察是跨视角一致性应源于共享世界状态（其演化不预设智能体数量），智能体特定观察通过统一渲染接口查询该状态生成。具体而言，Khora将世界状态演化与视觉渲染解耦，引入种群无关的渲染机制来整合其他智能体信息，通过共享世界状态维持跨视角一致性，而非在昂贵的视频生成器内部进行观察流之间的密集交互。

Result: 定性实验表明该方法能泛化到未见过的智能体数量，同时保持视觉质量和多智能体一致性；实现了近线性实际扩展；并构建了实时交互系统以展示可扩展的开放世界模拟。

Conclusion: Khora通过解耦世界状态演化与视觉渲染，并采用种群无关的渲染接口，成功突破了多智能体世界模型对固定智能体数量的限制，实现了推理时的任意智能体扩展和实时交互式模拟。

Abstract: World models have recently achieved impressive progress in visual prediction and interactive generation, but extending them to multi-agent environments introduces a fundamental scalability challenge. Existing methods generally assume a fixed number of agents during training and inference, which ties the model to a pre-determined agent population and limits inference-time scalability. Our key insight is that cross-view consistency should arise from a shared world state whose evolution does not assume a predefined number of agents, while agent-specific observations should be generated by querying this state through a unified rendering interface. Based on this insight, we propose Khora, a scalable multi-agent world model that supports inference-time expansion to arbitrary numbers of agents without retraining. Our framework decouples world-state evolution from visual rendering and introduces a population-agnostic rendering mechanism for incorporating other agent information. This design maintains cross-view consistency through the shared world state rather than through dense interactions among observation streams inside the expensive video generator, enabling approximately linear practical scaling with the number of queried views. Qualitative experiments demonstrate that our approach generalizes to unseen numbers of agents while maintaining visual quality and multi-agent consistency. We further implement a real-time interactive system to demonstrate scalable open-world simulation.

</details>


### [119] [REVEAL: A Rubric-Guided Agent for Explicit Evidence Sufficiency Verificationin Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08612)
*Caijun Yan,Yang Zhou,Meixing Shi,Haoran Sun,Yichen Li,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVEAL是一个基于rubric引导的智能体框架，用于长视频问答。它通过自适应视觉相似性预处理将连续帧分组为自然事件单元，构建离线-在线视频记忆，并利用自动构建的rubric库显式验证检索证据是否满足充分性标准，在证据不足时定位缺失线索并定向补充检索。无需额外训练即可在广泛实验中超越闭源和开源SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频问答方法存在两大缺陷：一是依赖固定长度的时间切块（如10秒），破坏了连续事件的完整性，且静态离线记忆库无法在实时推理中自适应；二是现有方法（无论基于多尺度摘要还是多模态知识图谱）只关注检索相关性而忽视证据充分性，一旦检索到语义相关线索就停止回答，即使关键的时间、因果或细粒度动作证据仍然缺失。

Method: REVEAL框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 自适应视觉相似性预处理管线——将视觉上连贯的相邻帧分组为自然事件单元，构建离线-在线视频记忆（离线捕获全局视频上下文，在线动态维护问题条件化记忆）；(2) 自动构建的rubric库——显式验证检索证据是否满足充分性标准，在验证失败时定位缺失的具体线索，并指导针对性的重新检索以获取补充信息。整个框架无需额外训练。

Result: 在广泛的实验中，REVEAL在无需任何额外训练的情况下，一致性地优于闭源和开源的现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，显式验证证据充分性而非仅停留在语义相关性层面，能够检索到先前方法遗漏的决定性线索，从而产生更可靠的长视频推理结果。

Abstract: Recently, retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented methods have emerged as two promising paradigms for long-video question answering. However, existing methods typically rely on rigid, fixed-length temporal chunking (e.g., 10s) and static offline memory banks, which not only fragment coherent continuous events but also fail to adapt during real-time reasoning. Moreover, whether using multi-scale summaries or multimodal knowledge graphs, current approaches prioritize retrieval relevance while overlooking evidence sufficiency, often stopping to answer once only semantically relevant clues are retrieved, even when key temporal, causal, or fine-grained action evidence is still missing. To tackle these challenges, we propose REVEAL, a rubric-guided agent framework. As a foundation, we introduce an adaptive visual-similarity-based preprocessing pipeline that groups visually coherent adjacent frames into natural event units to construct an offline-online video memory---capturing global video context offline while dynamically maintaining question-conditioned memory online. Built upon this structured memory, REVEAL uses an automatically constructed rubric library to explicitly verify whether retrieved evidence satisfies sufficiency criteria, pinpoints missing clues upon verification failure, and directs targeted re-retrieval for complementary information. Without any extra training, REVEAL consistently outperforms both closed-source and open-source state-of-the-art methods across extensive experiments. These results show that explicitly verifying evidence sufficiency, rather than stopping at semantic relevance, retrieves the decisive clues that prior methods miss and yields more reliable long-video reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [VADER: Adaptive Debiasing for Hallucination Mitigation in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08622)
*Dong Xing,Jiaxin Chen,Hang Yang,Peixun Liu,Qiushi Yang,Yuqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VADER框架，一个无需训练的、用于减少大型视频语言模型（LVLMs）在开放域视频理解中产生与视频证据不符的流畅但错误回答的框架。VADER由两个互补模块组成：视觉焦点重分配（VFR）自适应地为每个视频-问题输入实例化干预策略，通过诊断层级的视觉到文本证据流来重新分配softmax前的注意力；选择性证据擦除（SEE）独立地遮蔽每帧中高重要性的视觉token，构建难以被相邻帧补偿的先验偏置分支。对比解码随后对在选择性证据擦除后仍保持自信的预测进行降权。实验表明VADER在事件级定位和时间一致性上显著提升，在LLaVA-Video-7B上EventHallusion准确率达到72.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费方法存在两个主要局限：一是使用全局固定的视觉干预，无法适应视频依赖的融合路径；二是通过输入扰动构建对比分支，容易被跨帧冗余所补偿。因此需要一种能自适应于视频内容、且对比分支不易被补偿的防幻觉机制。

Method: VADER框架包含两个模块：(1) 视觉焦点重分配（VFR）：诊断层级的视觉到文本证据流，自动决定在何处干预以及以多强力度将softmax前的注意力从系统token重新分配到视频token块；(2) 选择性证据擦除（SEE）：在每一帧中独立地遮蔽高重要性的视觉token，构建先验偏置分支，使其难以通过相邻帧补偿。最后通过对比解码对在证据擦除后仍保持高置信度的预测进行降权。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM上，VADER在事件级定位和时间一致性方面均取得了显著改进；在LLaVA-Video-7B上，EventHallusion基准的准确率达到72.60%。

Conclusion: VADER通过视频自适应干预和难以补偿的对比分支有效缓解了LVLMs在视频理解中的幻觉问题，证明了无需训练的防幻觉策略可以在多个模型上泛化并显著提升事实性视频理解能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in open-ended video understanding, yet they remain prone to fluent responses unsupported by video evidence. Existing training-free methods typically apply a globally fixed visual intervention or construct a contrastive branch through input perturbation. The former cannot accommodate video-dependent fusion paths, while the latter can be compensated by cross-frame redundancy. We therefore propose Video-Adaptive Debiasing via Evidence Reweighting (VADER), a training-free framework with two complementary modules. Visual Focus Reallocation (VFR) automatically instantiates an intervention policy for each video-question input: it diagnoses layer-wise visual-to-text evidence flow, determines where to intervene, and derives how strongly to reallocate pre-softmax attention from system-token to video-token blocks. Selective Evidence Erasure (SEE) independently masks high-importance visual tokens in every frame, constructing a prior-biased branch that is difficult to compensate through neighboring frames. Contrastive decoding then down-weights predictions that remain confident after selective evidence erasure. Across multiple VideoLLMs, VADER yields substantial improvements on event-level grounding and temporal consistency; on LLaVA-Video-7B, it reaches 72.60% accuracy on EventHallusion.

</details>


### [121] [VLZip: Unified Visual and Textual Compression for Interleaved Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08630)
*Yuqi Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yuyu Guo,Wenjie Yang,Lingchen Meng,Peng Di,Hang Yu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLZip 是一个面向超长交错图文序列的统一视觉与文本压缩框架，通过分层蒸馏将视觉和文本片段提炼为逐层"软前缀"注入解码器各层，大幅缩短注意力序列长度。同时提出 LongVLBench 基准用于叙事级长上下文多模态推理评估。实验表明 VLZip 支持 120K token 训练、280K+ token 推理、最多可扩展至 2M token，在极限上下文长度下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理超长交错图文序列时，自注意力机制的二次复杂度造成巨大计算负担。当前解决方案要么采用激进的 token 剪枝（存在不可逆的信息丢失风险），要么采用高效但不精确的架构，且普遍忽略了同样重要的文本组件，导致长上下文多模态推理能力不足。

Method: VLZip 在纯 Transformer 架构内统一视觉与文本压缩：其核心机制是层级化地将视觉和文本片段蒸馏为紧凑的、逐层特定的"软前缀"（soft prefixes），并注入到每个解码器层的隐藏状态中，从而大幅缩短注意力序列的同时保留细粒度的全局上下文信息。此外还提出了来自视频叙事的 LongVLBench 基准以弥补现有评估的不足。

Result: VLZip 在长上下文多模态推理任务上取得领先性能：支持最多 120K token 的训练（相比基线提升 6 倍）、280K token 以上的推理（内存显著降低），并展现出可处理高达 2M token 的内存可扩展性。在现有方法失效的极限上下文长度下仍能保持卓越表现。

Conclusion: VLZip 通过统一的图文压缩机制，在超长上下文多模态推理中同时兼顾了高效性与高保真度，为长上下文多模态 AI 建立了新的高效、强大标准。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) face significant challenges with ultra-long, interleaved image-text sequences due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Current solutions either resort to aggressive token pruning, risking irreversible information loss, or adopt efficient but less precise architectures, while largely ignoring the equally vital textual component. We introduce VLZip, a framework that unifies visual and textual compression for high-fidelity reasoning within a pure Transformer. At its core, VLZip hierarchically distills visual and textual segments into compact, layer-specific "soft prefixes" and injects them into each decoder layer's hidden states, drastically shortening the attention sequence while preserving fine-grained global context. To address deficient evaluations in the field, we also introduce LongVLBench, a new benchmark derived from video narratives that demands holistic, narrative-level reasoning. Extensive experiments show VLZip achieves leading performance on long-context multimodal reasoning, enabling training up to 120K tokens, a 6x increase over the baseline, and inference beyond 280K tokens with significantly reduced memory, while demonstrating the memory scalability to handle up to 2M tokens. By excelling at extreme context lengths where existing methods collapse, VLZip establishes an efficient and powerful new standard for long-context multimodal AI. Code is available at https://github.com/ShareLab-SII/VLZip.

</details>


### [122] [Agentic Visual Reasoning in Whole-Slide Pathology Images via Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08648)
*Jingyun Chen,Fengchun Liu,Linghan Cai,Songhan Jiang,Shenjin Huang,Hongpeng Wang,Lequan Yu,Yongbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptivePath，一个基于主动感知的全切片病理图像推理框架。它将WSI证据获取建模为序贯决策问题：Navigator学习异常驱动的导航策略（通过PPO强化学习训练）从低到高倍率分层获取稀疏观测，Morphology Interpreter将观测转化为问题条件证据，Deliberator跨倍率评估证据并修正中间答案，Arbiter整合推理历史输出最终答案。在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上达到SOTA零样本性能，六种TCGA队列癌症亚型分类准确率达80.14%，盲法诊断效用研究中病理学家使用导航序列准确率达82.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI方法存在根本缺陷：基于密集采样的压缩方法割裂了预测与形态学特征之间的联系，而基于预训练VLM的方法采用启发式区域选择，缺乏病理学训练的观察策略。整张切片视觉推理需要识别千兆像素级别中的稀疏诊断证据，并跨空间尺度整合观测，现有方法无法有效建模这一过程。

Method: AdaptivePath采用四模块架构：(1) Navigator：学习与问题无关的异常驱动导航策略，通过交替表征学习和PPO近端策略优化训练，配合几何与外观一致性目标微调以稳定聚焦轨迹；(2) Morphology Interpreter：将导航得到的观测转化为问题条件证据；(3) Deliberator：跨倍率评估证据并修正中间答案；(4) Arbiter：整合推理历史产生最终答案。推理时Navigator在有限ROI预算下从低到高倍率分层获取稀疏观测。

Result: 在WSI和区域病理VQA基准上取得SOTA零样本性能；六种TCGA队列癌症亚型分类达到80.14%准确率；盲法诊断效用研究中，使用AdaptivePath选择观测序列的病理学家达到82.9%准确率。同时该方法提供了可追踪的推理过程。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过学习获得的主动感知策略能够实现对千兆像素病理切片的高效、可追踪的视觉推理，弥合了预测结果与底层形态学证据之间的联系，在保持诊断准确性的同时提升了推理的可解释性和可验证性。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual reasoning requires identifying sparse diagnostic evidence in gigapixel pathology slides and integrating observations across spatial scales. Existing WSI methods either compress densely sampled patches into global representations or use pretrained vision-language models with heuristic region selection, weakening links between predictions and morphology or lacking pathology-trained observation policies. We present AdaptivePath, an active-perception framework that formulates WSI evidence acquisition as sequential decision making. The Navigator learns question-agnostic abnormality-driven navigation from pathologist-reviewed labels to select observation locations and spatial extents, avoiding costly question-specific trajectory annotations. We train this policy through alternating representation learning and proximal policy optimization, followed by fine-tuning with geometric and appearance consistency objectives to stabilize focus trajectories. During inference, the Navigator hierarchically acquires sparse observations from low to high magnification under a limited ROI budget. A Morphology Interpreter converts observations into question-conditioned evidence, while the Deliberator evaluates evidence and revises intermediate answers across magnifications. The Arbiter integrates deliberation history to produce final answers. AdaptivePath achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on WSI and region pathology VQA benchmarks and reaches 80.14% accuracy for cancer subtype classification across six TCGA cohorts. In a blinded diagnostic-utility study, pathologists using AdaptivePath-selected observation sequences achieve 82.9% accuracy. These results demonstrate that learned active perception enables effective and traceable visual reasoning over gigapixel pathology slides.

</details>


### [123] [JSGS: JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08659)
*Jinhua Cui,Anhong Wang,Kai Hu,Donghan Bu,Peihao Li,Tammam Tillo,Hao Jing,Shiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JSGS提出一种基于JPEG量化表的监督方法，用于3D高斯泼溅在混合质量JPEG图像输入下的重建。通过构建视图特定的JPEG观测算子、对低中频DCT系数采用不同损失的加权监督，并利用块不一致性引导高斯控制器正则化，在七个场景三种混合质量配置下均取得最低LPIPS和最高SSIM，同时保持约150 FPS的渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS假设输入图像忠实采样场景辐射度，但混合质量的JPEG图像因压缩引入的块效应和振铃伪影不满足该假设，会导致共享高斯参数被跨视图的压缩噪声错误更新，从而降低重建质量。

Method: 利用每个JPEG文件中存储的亮度和色度量化表构建视图特定的JPEG观测算子，对渲染视图进行编解码以与对应解码输入图像进行域匹配比较。亮度量化表在中频带提供连续权重，低频带损失锚定粗结构，加权中频损失在选定DCT坐标上重分配监督。此外，块不一致性引导高斯控制器对不一致区域中高不透明度的小图元进行正则化。

Result: 在七个场景和三种混合质量调度下，JSGS在所有调度中均取得最低平均LPIPS和最高平均SSIM，渲染速度约为150 FPS。

Conclusion: JSGS通过显式建模JPEG压缩过程并利用量化表信息进行域匹配监督，有效缓解了混合质量JPEG输入对3DGS重建的负面影响，在保持渲染效率的同时显著提升了感知质量。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assumes that every input image faithfully samples scene radiance. However, mixed-quality JPEG images violate this assumption because compression-induced blocking and ringing artifacts can corrupt updates to Gaussians shared across views. To address this problem, we propose JPEG State-Guided Supervision for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Mixed-Quality Views (JSGS). JSGS uses luminance and chrominance quantization tables stored in each JPEG file to construct a view-specific JPEG observation operator. This operator encodes and decodes each rendered view for domain-matched comparison with the corresponding decoded input image. The luminance quantization table supplies continuous weights within a fixed middle frequency band. A loss in the low frequency band anchors coarse structure, while the weighted middle frequency loss redistributes supervision among the selected DCT coordinates. The resulting block disagreement also guides the Gaussian Controller to regularize small primitives with high opacity in disagreement regions. Across seven scenes and three mixed-quality schedules, JSGS achieves the lowest mean LPIPS and the highest mean SSIM under every schedule while rendering at approximately 150 FPS. Code: https://github.com/Jayden-Cui/JSGS.

</details>


### [124] [Degradation-Guided Underwater Image Restoration with Task-Oriented Latent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08661)
*Xu Zhang,Xuhui Cao,Kangzhe Yuan,Laibin Chang,Yichu Xu,Shi Chen,Huan Zhang,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PROTEUS模型，通过耦合退化引导的特征适应与面向任务的潜在控制，解决水下图像复原中退化信息未被充分利用或编码器特征被无调节直接转发的问题。该方法在特征层面利用GDFM块自适应处理空间变化的退化线索，在表示层面利用任务导向潜在控制器进行通道级调制。在五个配对和四个非参考数据集上取得具有竞争力的复原性能，并在质量与计算成本间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像的退化信息具有双重作用：其空间和光谱线索可引导自适应复原，但退化解缠特征在解码阶段可能未经显式调节就被传播。现有方法要么未充分利用退化线索，要么通过跳跃连接直接转发编码器特征，忽略了这一双重角色。

Method: 从两个互补视角构建PROTEUS框架：(1) 特征层面：Guided Dynamic Feature Modulation Block (GDFM) 利用空间变化的退化线索在不同网络阶段自适应调整特征处理；(2) 表示层面：任务导向潜在控制器在判别性正则化约束下学习结构化控制码，用于跳跃特征的通道级调制，且无需该控制码形成度量上更干净的嵌入。

Result: 在五个配对数据集和四个非参考基准上的大量实验表明，PROTEUS取得了极具竞争力的复原性能，并在复原质量与计算成本之间保持了良好平衡。

Conclusion: PROTEUS通过将退化引导的特征适应与任务导向的潜在控制相结合，有效解决了水下图像复原中退化线索的双重角色问题，验证了该耦合策略在提升复原性能的同时兼顾计算效率。

Abstract: Degradation information in underwater images plays a dual role: its spatial and spectral cues can guide adaptive restoration, while degradation-entangled features may be propagated without explicit regulation during decoding. Existing methods largely overlook this dual role, either underexploiting degradation cues or directly forwarding encoder features through skip connections. To address this issue, we propose PROTEUS, which couples degradation-guided feature adaptation with task?oriented latent control. PROTEUS tackles this problem from two complementary perspectives. At the feature level, the Guided Dynamic Feature Modulation Block exploits spatially varying degradation cues to adapt feature processing across network stages. At the representation level, the task-oriented latent controller learns a structured control code under discriminative regularisation and uses it for channel-wise modulation of skip features, without requiring the code to form a metrically cleaner embedding. Extensive experiments on five paired and four non-reference underwater benchmarks demonstrate that PROTEUS achieves highly competitive restoration performance, with a favourable balance between restoration quality and computational cost.

</details>


### [125] [FiRe: Fixed-Noise Refinement for Visual Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08664)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Oskar Kristoffersen,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiRe是一个固定噪声精炼框架，用于视觉反事实解释。它不沿着反向去噪轨迹进行，而是将输入映射到固定噪声水平并迭代精炼。通过像素均值流直接预测干净图像，并引入动态双掩码、自适应引导和早停三种控制机制，实现了比现有方法约3倍更快的推理速度和8倍更少的FLOPs，同时保持或超越现有反事实质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DDPM的视觉反事实解释方法沿着长反向去噪轨迹进行分类器引导编辑，面临两个核心问题：(1) 噪声水平不断变化导致语义可编辑性和空间控制难以平衡；(2) 可编辑状态是带噪的，而目标分类器在干净图像上训练，导致要么需要昂贵的递归去噪，要么使用低质量的一步估计来获得分类器可用的干净图像。

Method: FiRe将输入映射到固定噪声水平并在该水平反复精炼噪声状态，避免长反向轨迹。具体包括：(1) 将Pixel Mean Flow适配到视觉反事实解释场景，直接从噪声状态预测干净图像用于分类器引导；(2) 引入三个FiRe特有控制机制——动态双掩码策略确定编辑累积位置、自适应引导控制哪些变化可见、早停决定精炼何时终止，以实现最小化和局部化的反事实编辑。

Result: 在三个数据集的五个任务上进行实验，与最强近期基线相比，FiRe实现了约3倍更快的在线推理速度和8倍更少的FLOPs，同时获得相当或最先进的反事实质量。

Conclusion: FiRe通过固定噪声精炼框架有效解决了DDPM基方法中噪声水平变化带来的编辑可控性和分类器引导不匹配问题，在显著提升计算效率的同时保持或超越了反事实解释的质量。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to change classifier decisions through realistic and localized edits while preserving decision-irrelevant content. Existing DDPM-based methods typically perform classifier-guided editing along a long reverse denoising trajectory. The changing noise levels make semantic editability and spatial control difficult to balance, and the editable state is noisy, whereas the target classifier is trained on clean images. As a result, these methods require either costly recursive denoising or low-quality one-step estimates to obtain classifier-facing clean images. We propose FiRe, a Fixed-noise Refinement framework for visual counterfactual explanations. Rather than following a reverse denoising trajectory, FiRe maps the input to a fixed noise level and iteratively refines the noisy state at that level. To provide clean images for classifier guidance, FiRe first adapts Pixel Mean Flow to visual counterfactual explanation, enabling direct clean-image prediction from noisy states. To make fixed-noise refinement produce minimal and localized counterfactual edits, FiRe introduces three FiRe-specific controls: a dynamic dual-mask strategy, adaptive guidance, and early stopping, which determine where edits accumulate, which changes become visible, and when refinement stops. Experiments on five tasks across three datasets show that, compared with the strongest recent baseline, FiRe achieves about 3$\times$ faster online inference and 8$\times$ fewer FLOPs while obtaining comparable or state-of-the-art counterfactual quality.

</details>


### [126] [UniSpace: Unified Visual Representation and Scalable Multimodal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08676)
*Jinbo Yan,Limeng Qiao,Jie Qin,Junyan He,Feize Wu,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"补丁重参数化"（Patch Reparameterization）方法，将预训练语义ViT改造为统一的视觉表示空间，使同一表示既可支持多模态理解，又能实现高保真图像重建、生成和编辑，并扩展到8B参数规模的UniSpace模型，无需独立的VAE路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义视觉编码器的最终token丢弃了细粒度视觉细节，导致像素重建质量差，限制了其在图像生成和编辑等重建敏感任务中的使用。作者追问：能否在单个由预训练语义ViT构建的视觉表示空间中同时建模理解、生成和编辑？

Method: 首先证明冻结的语义ViT Transformer块本身并未固有丢失视觉细节，问题是原始补丁参数化使表示偏向语义抽象。基于此观察，提出Patch Reparameterization：保留原有语义路径的同时，添加一个重建感知的补丁嵌入，为同一冻结ViT块提供细粒度视觉信息。该统一表示支持多模态理解和高保真图像重建，进一步扩展为UniSpace——8B参数的Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts模型，在同一视觉空间中执行理解、生成和编辑，无需独立VAE。

Result: 统一表示保持了多模态理解能力，同时实现了高保真图像重建和良好的重建-生成权衡。UniSpace在系统级评估中展示了实用的文本到图像生成和基于指令的图像编辑能力。

Conclusion: 重参数化的预训练ViT可以作为可扩展多模态建模的统一视觉接口，证明理解、生成和编辑可以在单一视觉表示空间中统一建模。

Abstract: Semantic vision encoders have become a central visual interface for multimodal understanding and semantic conditioning in image generation. However, their final tokens discard fine-grained visual details, leading to poor pixel reconstruction and limiting their use in reconstruction-sensitive tasks such as image generation and editing. In this work, we ask whether understanding, generation, and editing can be modeled in a single visual representation space built from a pretrained semantic ViT. We show that the frozen Transformer blocks of a semantic ViT are not intrinsically unable to preserve visual details. Instead, the original patch parameterization drives the representation toward semantic abstraction, making fine-grained information difficult to recover from the final tokens. Based on this observation, we introduce \emph{Patch Reparameterization}, which preserves the original semantic pathway while adding a reconstruction-aware patch embedding that provides fine-grained visual information to the same frozen ViT blocks. The resulting unified representation preserves multimodal understanding while enabling high-fidelity image reconstruction and a favorable reconstruction--generation trade-off. We further scale this representation into \emph{UniSpace}, an 8B Mixture-of-Transformer-Experts model that performs understanding, generation, and editing in the same visual space without a separate VAE pathway. System-level evaluations demonstrate practical text-to-image generation and instruction-based image editing, showing that a reparameterized pretrained ViT can serve as a unified visual interface for scalable multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [127] [Semi-Dense Matching Uncertainty Is Not Just Local Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08685)
*Khoa Hoang,Hoang-Tuan Nguyen,Huong Ninh,Hai Tran,Long Q. Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的、后处理的整体不确定性估计框架，用于半稠密几何匹配。该方法引入仅含9个可学习参数的两分量校准Laplace混合模型，显式捕捉局部精化噪声与粗分配失败的尾部效应。同时提出CoRe（粗分配成功后验重拟合）模块，利用粗分配成功的后验概率作为软对应权重进行几何重拟合，在不增加显著计算开销的情况下提升各种预训练匹配器和鲁棒估计器的下游几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半稠密匹配方法基于coarse-to-fine范式，虽然取得了性能与计算成本的良好平衡，但往往无法提供良好的量化不确定性。特别是灾难性的粗分配失败被忽略，导致误差分布被截断，严重误判几何估计结果。缺乏对粗分配失败尾部效应的显式建模，是现有方法的主要不足。

Method: 提出一个轻量级、即插即用的整体不确定性估计框架，包含：(1) 两分量校准Laplace混合模型，仅9个可学习参数，显式建模尖峰状的局部精化噪声和重尾状的粗分配失败；(2) CoRe（Coarse-success posterior Refit）几何重拟合模块，利用粗分配成功的后验概率作为软对应权重进行鲁棒几何估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种预训练匹配器和鲁棒估计器上一致地提升了下游几何精度，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理不确定性估计框架能够有效量化粗分配失败带来的不确定性尾部，并通过CoRe软加权重拟合显著改善下游几何估计的准确性，具有高度的通用性和极低的计算开销。

Abstract: Reliable semi-dense matching is essential for modern geometric vision systems. Designed under a coarse-to-fine paradigm, it achieves an optimal balance between performance and computational cost. However, existing methods often struggle to provide well-quantified uncertainties, where catastrophic coarse-assignment failures are ignored, leading to truncated error distributions and severely misjudged geometric estimations. In this paper, we propose a lightweight, post-hoc overall uncertainty estimation framework that introduces a two-component calibrated Laplace mixture model with only 9 learnable parameters. The objective is to explicitly capture both the sharp local refinement noise and the broader tail of coarse-assignment failures. We introduce the Coarse-success posterior Refit (CoRe) method, a geometric refitting module that utilizes the posterior probability of coarse-assignment success as soft correspondence weights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently improves downstream geometric accuracy across various pretrained-only matchers and robust estimators with minimal computational overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/khoavpt/Probabilistic-matching.

</details>


### [128] [AnchorFold: A Focus-Then-Fold Framework via Recursive Attention Propagation for Efficient Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08732)
*Haoyu Zuo,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnchorFold，一个免训练的"先聚焦后折叠"（focus-then-fold）文档侧索引压缩框架，通过递归注意力传播选择高中心性锚点token，并将非锚点token分配到最相似锚点群组进行中心性加权聚合，在5倍压缩下保持98.3%的NDCG@5，20倍压缩下仍达92.4%，实现近无损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉语言检索器依赖late interaction实现细粒度视觉文档检索（VDR），但每页存储和打分数百个视觉patch嵌入带来巨大开销。现有免训练方法（剪枝和合并）各有缺陷：剪枝在激进压缩下性能急剧下降，合并则未显式优先处理重要区域。因此需要一种既能高效压缩索引又能保留重要token贡献的免训练方法。

Method: AnchorFold采用两阶段框架：(1) 聚焦阶段（Focus）：在视觉自注意力图上执行递归注意力传播（Recursive Attention Propagation），在每个注意力头内进行多步传播并跨头跨层整合分数，选择最高中心性的token作为锚点；(2) 折叠阶段（Fold）：将剩余token分配到与之最相似的锚点（在归一化检索空间中），并通过中心性加权聚合汇总每个锚点为中心的组，保留非锚点贡献的同时集中容量于结构上重要的token。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1/v2和REAL-MM-RAG三个数据集、三种不同检索骨干网络上，AnchorFold在压缩率γ≤0.20下一致优于所有评估的免训练基线。ViDoRe v1/v2上，5倍压缩时平均保留98.3%的完整索引NDCG@5，实现近无损压缩；20倍压缩时仍保留92.4%。

Conclusion: AnchorFold通过显式优先处理结构上重要的token，同时保留非锚点贡献，在激进的索引压缩下实现了近无损性能，为多向量视觉文档检索提供了一种高效且有效的训练免费索引压缩方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector vision-language retrievers enable fine-grained Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) through late interaction, but storing and scoring hundreds of visual patch embeddings per page incurs substantial overhead. Existing training-free methods rely on pruning or merging: pruning degrades sharply under aggressive compression, whereas merging does not explicitly prioritize important regions when forming representatives. We introduce AnchorFold, a training-free focus-then-fold framework for document-side index compression. AnchorFold applies Recursive Attention Propagation over visual self-attention graphs, performing multi-step propagation within each attention head and integrating scores across heads and layers. The focus stage selects the highest-centrality tokens as anchors. The fold stage assigns remaining tokens to their most similar anchors in the normalized retrieval space and summarizes each anchor-centered group through centrality-weighted aggregation. This preserves non-anchor contributions while concentrating capacity on structurally important tokens. Across ViDoRe v1/v2 and REAL-MM-RAG with three diverse retrieval backbones, AnchorFold consistently outperforms all evaluated training-free baselines at $γ\leq 0.20$. On ViDoRe v1/v2, it retains 98.3% of full-index NDCG@5 on average at $5\times$ compression, achieving near-lossless compression, and 92.4% at $20\times$ compression.

</details>


### [129] [CUPA-T2*: Covariance-Aware Uncertainty Propagation and Alignment for T2* Mapping in Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08693)
*Gideon N. L. Rouwendaal,Natascha Niessen,Hannah Eichhorn,Dirk H. J. Poot,Christine Preibisch,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CUPA-T2*是一个将随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建的体素级帧间不确定性显式传播到下游T2*拟合的框架，通过协方差感知采样和异方差MLP实现不确定性对齐，在加速脑MRI数据上达到了具有竞争力的T2*拟合性能，并提供了体素级不确定性图以支持定量T2*估计的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量T2*图在生物标志物发现方面具有巨大潜力，但受限于较长的扫描时间，在临床环境中不实用。虽然结合欠采样和学习重建可以显著加速，但重建伪影和噪声会传播到下游T2*拟合中，降低其精度。因此需要一种显式考虑重建不确定性并将其传播到T2*拟合的方法。

Method: 提出CUPA-T2*框架：1) 利用随机蒙特卡洛dropout重建来获取体素级帧间不确定性；2) 通过协方差感知采样将不确定性显式传播到下游T2*拟合；3) 使用异方差MLP进行T2*拟合；4) 引入基于相关性的正则化器，鼓励预测方差与重建不确定性之间的对齐。

Result: 在加速脑MRI数据上的实验显示组织依赖性行为：CUPA-T2*在整体T2*拟合性能上具有竞争力，并在高加速倍率下改善了白质区域的性能。与异方差基线相比，该方法显著提高了重建不确定性与预测T2*方差之间的对齐，但也揭示了与校准（ECE）和选择性预测性能（AURC）之间的权衡。

Conclusion: CUPA-T2*实现了重建不确定性感知的T2*拟合，提供体素级不确定性图来支持定量T2*估计的解释。该框架展示了不确定性传播在量化成像中的潜力，但也需要通过权衡分析来平衡不确定性对齐与校准、选择性预测性能之间的关系。

Abstract: Quantitative T2* maps have strong potential for biomarker discovery but are limited by long scan times, rendering them impractical in clinical settings. Significant acceleration can be achieved through undersampling in k-space combined with learning-based reconstruction. However, reconstruction artifacts and noise can propagate into downstream T2* fitting, degrading its accuracy. We introduce CUPA-T2*, a framework that explicitly propagates voxel-wise inter-echo uncertainty from stochastic Monte Carlo dropout reconstructions to downstream T2* fitting via covariance-aware sampling. T2* fitting is performed with a heteroscedastic MLP and a correlation-based regularizer that encourages alignment between predicted variance and reconstruction uncertainty. Experiments on accelerated brain MRI data show tissue-dependent behavior: CUPA-T2* achieves competitive overall T2* fitting performance and improves white-matter performance at higher accelerations. Compared with a heteroscedastic baseline, the proposed framework substantially increases alignment between reconstruction uncertainty and predicted T2* variance, while also revealing a trade-off with calibration (ECE) and selective prediction performance (AURC). CUPA-T2* enables reconstruction uncertainty-aware T2* fitting and delivers voxel-wise uncertainty maps to support the interpretation of quantitative T2* estimates.

</details>


### [130] [OccAnyScene: Towards Unified Indoor-Outdoor 3D Occupancy Predictio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08696)
*Junjie Liu,Wanshui Gan,Zitong Dai,Guiping Cao,Yan Li,Ke Chen,Dongmei Jiang,Xiangyuan Lan,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨场景3D语义占用预测任务（Cross-Scene 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction），要求单一模型同时处理异构室内外场景（不同相机、空间范围、体素规格和语义体系）。为此提出OccAnyScene框架，基于预训练深度基础模型，采用像素平截头体中心的高斯表示，通过像素对齐平截头体特征聚合和平截头体参数化高斯构建，在室内Occ-ScanNet和室外SurroundOcc-nuScenes上均取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占用方法通常针对固定场景类型和占用协议设计，缺乏通用性。作者提出跨场景3D语义占用预测这一新任务设置，要求单一模型适应异构室内外场景中不同的相机配置、空间范围、体素规格和语义标签体系，核心挑战在于如何在变化的相机配置和场景尺度下实现度量一致且场景自适应的图像到3D提升（image-to-3D lifting）。

Method: 提出OccAnyScene框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Pixel-Aligned Frustum Feature Aggregation：为每个特征像素构建相机感知的平截头体查询；(2) Frustum-Parameterized Gaussian Construction：将每个查询解码为多个高斯，高斯的位置和大小由预测的像素深度及对应平截头体几何约束。框架构建在预训练深度基础模型之上，采用像素-平截头体中心的高斯表示。

Result: 在室内Occ-ScanNet上达到59.92% mIoU，在室外SurroundOcc-nuScenes上达到23.06% mIoU，均创下新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: OccAnyScene通过像素-平截头体中心的高斯框架有效解决了跨场景3D语义占用预测中度量一致性与场景自适应性的挑战，实现了单一模型在异构室内外场景上的统一占用预测。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction is fundamental to scene understanding, yet existing 3D semantic occupancy methods are typically specialized to fixed scene types and occupancy protocols. We introduce Cross-Scene 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction, a new task setting which requires a single model to handle heterogeneous indoor and outdoor scenes with varying cameras, spatial ranges, voxel specifications, and semantic taxonomies. This setting poses a fundamental challenge: achieving metric-consistent yet scene-adaptive image-to-3D lifting across varying camera configurations and scene scales. To address this challenge, we propose OccAnyScene, a pixel-frustum-centered Gaussian framework built upon a pretrained depth foundation model. Specifically, the framework employs Pixel-Aligned Frustum Feature Aggregation to construct a camera-aware frustum query for each feature pixel, and Frustum-Parameterized Gaussian Construction to decode each query into multiple Gaussians whose positions and sizes are constrained by the predicted pixel depth and corresponding frustum geometry. OccAnyScene sets new state-of-the-art results, achieving 59.92% mIoU on the indoor Occ-ScanNet and 23.06% mIoU on the outdoor SurroundOcc-nuScenes.

</details>


### [131] [SRE-FER: Regional residual evidence learning for mitigating local evidence dilution in fine-grained facial expression recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08702)
*Jiaye Song,Ruochen Zhang,Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRE-FER，一种基于读头级区域残差证据学习的细粒度表情识别框架。其核心模块RERA通过零初始化的残差logits在保持骨干网络全局预测的同时细化类别边界，训练时利用FACS解剖先验引导区域特征聚焦表情相关区域，推理时无需外部面部管线。在RAF-DB、FERPlus和AffectNet-7三个基准上分别达到92.76%、91.32%和67.78%的准确率，表现出强竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度表情识别依赖捕捉区分相邻情绪的细微肌肉线索，但现有方法存在两难困境：检测器方法依赖脆弱的面部关键点管线；而直接使用DINOv3等基础模型在传统全局读头下会导致局部证据稀释——早期全局聚合冲刷掉稀疏的肌肉信号，造成恐惧/惊讶、悲伤/中性等类别间的持续混淆。

Method: 提出SRE-FER框架，核心模块RERA引入零初始化的残差logits来细化类别边界，同时保留骨干网络的全局预测。训练阶段使用基于FACS解剖先验的动作单元（AU）引导，将区域特征导向表情相关区域，推理时无需外部面部管线。可选Full设置进一步路由样本特定的非冗余token。
