*Wenxiao Zhao,Shu Wang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Se-DPO，一种自演化的token级信用分配机制，用于改进直接偏好优化（DPO）。传统DPO对所有token的log概率比进行均匀求和，忽略了不同token对偏好信号的贡献差异。Se-DPO从模型自身的内部信号实时推导token信用，并基于token贡献的强度和置信度进行校准，无需外部模型，仅增加轻量级校准网络。实验表明，Se-DPO在AlpacaEval 2上比DPO提升最高9.8分，在Arena-Hard上提升12.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DPO通过均匀求和聚合token级对数概率比，隐含假设所有token对偏好信号的贡献相同。但实际中不同token的贡献存在显著差异。论文证明有效token信用与每个token隐式奖励的幅度成正比，且该量在训练过程中大幅演化，导致静态token信用随训练推进而逐渐失准。由此提出动态的token信用分配机制。

Method: 提出Se-DPO（Self-Evolving Token Credit for DPO），一种实时机制，从模型自身在DPO训练过程中不断演化的内部信号中推导token信用。由于奖励信号在不同位置上的可靠性不同，Se-DPO同时基于每个token贡献的强度和置信度来校准token信用。该方案无需外部模型，仅引入一个轻量级校准网络，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明，Se-DPO相比标准DPO在AlpacaEval 2上最高提升9.8个点，在Arena-Hard上提升12.2个点。

Conclusion: 通过自演化的token级信用分配，Se-DPO能够更准确地反映不同token对偏好信号的实际贡献，消除静态均匀权重带来的错配问题，在保持极低额外开销的同时显著提升DPO的对齐性能。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aggregates token-level log-probability ratios via uniform summation, implicitly treating all tokens as contributing equally to the preference signal. However, the contribution of individual tokens to the preference signal varies. We introduce token credit, which modulates each token's KL regularization based on its contribution to the preference outcome. We derive that effective token credit is proportional to the magnitude of each token's implicit reward, and observe that this quantity evolves substantially during training. This implies that static token credit becomes increasingly misaligned as training progresses. In this work, we propose Se-DPO (Self-Evolving Token Credit for DPO), a live mechanism that derives token credit from the model's own evolving internal signals during DPO training. Since the reward signal varies in reliability across positions, Se-DPO calibrates token credit based on both the strength and the confidence of each token's contribution. Se-DPO requires no external models, adding only a lightweight calibration network with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that Se-DPO improves over DPO by up to 9.8 points on AlpacaEval~2 and 12.2 points on Arena-Hard.

</details>


### [230] [MDB-Link: Hierarchical Schema Linking for Multi-Database Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09588)
*Beiyu Xu,Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Riza theresa Batista-navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDB-Link，一个面向多数据库场景的层级式schema链接框架，解决传统Text-to-SQL假设单一已知数据库的局限。该框架通过全局索引检索相关列、聚合证据筛选候选数据库、以及预算感知的LLM进行数据库重排序、表选择和列对齐，在MMQA、Spider2-Snow和BIRD-dev基准上大幅超越现有方法，同时运行速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Text-to-SQL研究和基准假设目标数据库已知，忽略了在大型异构数据库集合中必须先定位目标数据库再进行查询路由的实际场景。这种假设与现实世界的应用不符，需要研究多数据库设置下的schema链接问题。

Method: 提出MDB-Link层级式schema链接框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 从全局索引中检索与问题相关的列；(2) 聚合检索证据以短list候选数据库；(3) 使用预算感知的大语言模型（LLM）进行数据库重排序、表选择和列grounding，最终生成紧凑且与SQL相关的schema子集。基于Qwen2.5-14B实现。

Result: MDB-Link在MMQA、Spider2-Snow和BIRD-dev三个基准上均优于LinkAlign，具体表现为：MMQA的Exact Match从16.88提升至51.41，Spider2-Snow从2.50提升至9.17，BIRD-dev从12.52提升至38.01。同时生成的schema子集大小接近gold schema，且运行速度比LinkAlign和AutoLink更快。

Conclusion: 实验证明层级式schema缩减对下游SQL生成是有效的，MDB-Link在多数据库定位和列选择任务上表现优越，同时兼具效率优势，为多数据库Text-to-SQL场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional Text-to-SQL research and benchmarks assume a known target database, overlooking settings in which a query must be routed within a large, heterogeneous database collection. We therefore study schema linking in a multi-database setting, where the system must first locate the target database and then construct a compact, SQL-relevant schema for generation. We propose MDB-Link, a hierarchical schema-linking framework that retrieves question-relevant columns from a global index, aggregates retrieval evidence to shortlist databases, and uses a budget-aware large language model (LLM) for database reranking, table selection, and column grounding. With Qwen2.5-14B, MDB-Link outperforms LinkAlign on MMQA, Spider2-Snow, and BIRD-dev in database localization and column selection while producing schema subsets close in size to the gold schemas. Exact match improves from 16.88 to 51.41 on MMQA, 2.50 to 9.17 on Spider2-Snow, and 12.52 to 38.01 on BIRD-dev. MDB-Link also runs faster than LinkAlign and AutoLink, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical schema reduction for downstream SQL generation.

</details>


### [231] [Measuring the Wrong Thing: Internal Harmfulness Scores Anti-Rank Successful Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09624)
*Mingyu Luo,Ming Deng,Zilang Qiu,Yiming Cheng,Ci Tao,Xue Tan,Sijin Sun,Yangfu Li,Ping Chen,Jun Dai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了一个常见假设：内部安全分数能分离有害提示与良性提示，就意味着它也能捕获后续真正成功的攻击。作者引入Active Attention Probing固定测量坐标，发现在包装（wrapped）提示下，危害生成率从0.05升至0.27，而有害意图AUROC从0.936降至0.803——攻击变得更危险，但分数却觉得提示更安全。包装有害提示的结果AUROC为0.220，成功攻击反而排在失败攻击之下。该反转在三个目标模型、七种攻击家族和两个独立评判器上持续存在，且分布偏移优先破坏校准与阈值迁移，之后才破坏排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文质疑并审计了一个广泛使用的推断：用注意力的内部安全分数来预先过滤有害提示。作者的核心关切是，过滤器的评分衡量的可能是"有害意图"（提示的属性），而不是"越狱成功"（由目标模型、解码策略和评判器共同产生的结果）。一个测量了错误量的分数，会把错误允许（false positive）预算浪费在本来就会失败的攻击上。

Method: 作者引入Active Attention Probing（主动注意力探测），提供固定的、内容独立的测量坐标，因为基于注意力的测量通常从提示相关的位置读取，而包装器（wrapper）会同时改变被判断的内容和信号提取位置。他们将每个基础目标配对一个朴素版本和一个包装版本，从目标模型生成真实补全。通过注意ID匹配，跨多个变量（三个目标模型、七个攻击家族、两个独立评判器）系统检验有害意图AUROC与攻击结果（生成危害）AUROC之间的关系。

Result: 在Llama上，包装使有害生成从0.05升至0.27，而有害意图AUROC从0.936降至0.803。在包装的有害提示中，结果AUROC为0.220——即成功攻击排在失败攻击之下。稀有token、被动和检测器衍生通道在相同的匹配设计上复现了该反转，且反转在三个目标模型、七个攻击家族和两个独立评判器上均成立。分布偏移首先破坏校准和阈值迁移，其次才破坏排序。

Conclusion: 论文表明，内部安全分数在包装攻击下不仅测量了错误的数量（有害意图而非越狱成功），还会将成功的攻击错误地排序在失败攻击之下。这意味着依赖此类分数进行先验过滤在实际部署中会导致预算浪费和安全性风险；分布偏移下的校准退化先于排序退化，进一步说明这类分数的可靠性被系统性高估。

Abstract: Internal safety scores judge a prompt before any text is generated, and they are validated by how well they separate harmful prompts from benign ones. That separation is then read as evidence that the score will also catch the attacks that succeed. Harmful intent is a property of the prompt. Jailbreak success is an outcome produced later by a particular target model, decoding policy, and judge. A filter tuned on a score that measures the wrong quantity spends its false positive budget on attacks that would have failed anyway. In this paper we audit that inference. Attention based measurements are usually read from prompt dependent locations, so a wrapper changes both the content being judged and the place the signal is taken from. We therefore introduce Active Attention Probing, which supplies a fixed content independent measurement coordinate. We pair every base goal with a plain and a wrapped version and generate real completions from the target models. On Llama, wrapping raises harmful generation from 0.05 to 0.27 while harmful intent AUROC falls from 0.936 to 0.803, so the attacks grow more dangerous while the prompts look safer to the score. Among wrapped harmful prompts the outcome AUROC is 0.220, which places the attacks that succeeded below the attacks that failed. Rare token, passive, and detector derived channels reproduce the reversal on the same matched design, and the reversal itself persists across three target models, seven attack families, and two independent judges. Distribution shift then degrades calibration and threshold transfer before it degrades ranking.

</details>


### [232] [How Do Large Language Models Judge Social Attraction? Evidence from Theory-Grounded Persona Ratings Across Multiple LLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09717)
*Hasan Mahmud,Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Jamison Heard,Prabu David*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究考察大语言模型（LLM）作为"社会法官"评估社交吸引力的有效性。通过基于十个心理学和关系构念构建的三层次人格画像（社交吸引、社交混合、社交不吸引），三项研究分别检验了34个LLM的评分稳定性与排序一致性、对性别呈现的敏感性，以及与198名人类参与者的评分对比。结果表明LLM评分具有跨运行稳定性和三层次结构，且与人类评分排序一致，但LLM对极端画像存在更强烈的评分偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于替代人类进行主观评估，但其作为社会判断工具的有效性尚不明确。本文旨在系统检验LLM能否基于理论驱动的人格画像可靠地评估社交吸引力，以验证其作为"社会法官"的效度。

Method: 该研究采用三阶段实验设计：（1）研究1中，34个LLM对基于十个心理学和关系构念构建的12个人格画像进行三次重复评分，检验稳定性、分层排序和一致性；（2）研究2通过六对匹配的姓名-代词组合及单独的代词测试，检验性别呈现对LLM评分的影响；（3）研究3中198名人类参与者对研究2的六组匹配画像进行评分，与LLM结果进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：LLM评分在重复运行中表现稳定，能一致地产生三层次排序，且在相对画像排序上具有高一致性；性别呈现对LLM评分无显著影响；人类评分复现了三层次结构且与LLM排序一致。然而，LLM对吸引力高的画像评分更正面、对吸引力低的画像评分更负面（即存在极端化偏向），两群体均未表现出显著的性别呈现效应。

Conclusion: LLM能够稳定、一致地根据理论驱动的人格画像评估社交吸引力，且其排序与人类判断高度吻合，支持其作为社会评估工具的潜在效度。但LLM可能比人类更倾向于放大对刻板画像的评价差异，未来需注意这种极端化偏向在实际应用中的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to perform subjective evaluations traditionally made by humans, yet their validity as social judges remains unclear. This paper examines whether LLMs can assess social attraction from theory-grounded persona profiles constructed from ten psychological and relational constructs and organized into three tiers: socially attractive, socially mixed, and socially unattractive. We examine LLM ratings in two studies and compare them with human judgments in a third study. In Study 1, 34 LLMs rated 12 profiles across three repeated runs. Although some models tended to give higher or lower ratings overall, they showed strong stability across runs, consistent three-tier ordering, and high agreement in relative profile ordering. Study 2 examined sensitivity to gender presentation using six matched name-and-pronoun profile pairs and a separate pronoun-only test with a gender-neutral name, finding no significant effects in either analysis. In Study 3, 198 human participants evaluated the six matched profiles from Study 2. Their ratings reproduced the three-tier structure and followed a profile ordering consistent with that of the LLMs. However, LLMs rated attractive profiles more positively and unattractive profiles more negatively than humans, while neither group showed a significant overall effect of gender presentation.

</details>


### [233] [REFRAMED: Towards Realistic Audio Description Generation for Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09765)
*Igor Sterner,Mirella Lapata,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的音频描述(AD)生成任务公式化方法，要求模型同时决定"描述什么"和"何时描述"，并发布了REFRAMED数据集（2,023个视频、3,302个场景、206部电影，含专业AD转录、字幕和对齐剧本），以及包含人工策划挑战集和多参考评估协议的全新基准。实验表明现有SOTA系统远未达到人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD生成方法被简化为剪辑级字幕生成，其中描述的内容和时间都是预先指定的，忽略了AD作为结构化编辑任务的核心特性。此外，现有方法依赖有噪声的转录和对齐流程，且缺乏用于建模叙事上下文的丰富并行数据。

Method: 本文提出将AD生成重新公式化为一个联合决策任务（决定内容和时机），并构建了REFRAMED高质量数据集（包含美国版和英国版专业AD、专业字幕和对齐剧本），同时设计了利用对话间隙和多参考比较的评估协议，以及人工策划的全电影多参考挑战集。

Result: 实验表明，即使是最先进的AD系统和多模态大语言模型（LLM），虽然优于简单基线，但仍与专家人类表现存在巨大差距，证明了该任务具有充分的挑战性。

Conclusion: REFRAMED数据集和基准为视频理解研究建立了新的基础，凸显了"何时描述"与"描述什么"同等重要这一核心见解，为该领域未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Audio Description (AD) is a verbal narration of key visual content in videos, enabling access for visually impaired audiences. Unlike standard video captioning, AD is a structured editorial task: descriptions must be inserted into gaps in dialogue and must convey only what is needed to understand the narrative being told. However, existing approaches formulate AD generation in an artificial setting where both the content and timing of descriptions are pre-specified, reducing the task to clip-level captioning. They further rely on noisy transcription and alignment pipelines, and lack the rich parallel data required for modeling narrative context. We introduce a new formulation of AD generation in which models must jointly decide what to describe and when to do it. To support this, we present REFRAMED, a high-quality dataset of 2,023 videos that span 3,302 scenes from 206 movies, with professional AD transcripts (both American and British versions), professional subtitles and aligned screenplays. We also provide a manually curated challenge set that pairs full movies with multiple AD references, together with evaluation protocols that leverage dialogue gaps and multi-reference comparisons. Experiments with state-of-the-art AD systems and multimodal LLMs show that they outperform trivial baselines but fall far short of expert human performance. Our dataset and benchmark establish a new foundation for research on video understanding.

</details>


### [234] [Cultivar: A Contrastive and Locale-Oriented Translation Benchmark for Investigating Contamination and Localisation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09766)
*Pinzhen Chen,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Xiaoya Xu,David Tan,Doreen Osmelak,Ona de Gibert,Ariun-Erdene Tumurchuluun,Ashok Urlana,Fedor Sizov,Hale Sirin,Jesujoba Alabi,Karrar Talib Abed,Mateusz Klimaszewski,Nikolay Bogoychev,Niyati Bafna,Patricia Schmidtova,Preksha Manjunath Shanbhag,Sherrie Shen,Vilem Zouhar,Vivek Iyer,Yasser Hamidullah,Yusser Al Ghussin,Zheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出源对比评估（source-contrastive evaluation）范式，并构建了Cultivar数据集——基于FLORES的本地化子集，用于评测多语言翻译模型对地区与文化本地化的鲁棒性。通过对比32个开源权重模型在本地化与未本地化文本上的表现差异，发现MT专用模型鲁棒性较差、部分模型可能过拟合FLORES，且模型普遍对美国内容翻译更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言翻译基准大多以英语为源语言翻译成其他语言，以语言对为评估单位，这种方式容易随时间产生数据污染，且忽略了地区（locale）与文化差异。作者因此倡导转向源对比评估，以更真实地反映翻译系统的本地化能力。

Method: 提出Cultivar数据集，作为FLORES的本地化子集，实现针对特定地区的翻译评估。将Cultivar与其未本地化的对应版本配对，通过两者的性能差异来探测数据污染和本地化鲁棒性。对32个开源权重翻译模型进行基准测试。

Result: 发现MT专用模型对本地化内容鲁棒性较差；少数模型可能在FLORES上过拟合；无论目标语言如何，模型普遍对美国本地化内容翻译优于其他地区内容。

Conclusion: 源对比评估是一种更能反映翻译系统真实本地化能力、且能抵抗数据污染的新型评估范式，Cultivar为实现这一范式提供了可用的数据集与评测框架。

Abstract: Multilingual translation benchmarks are typically sourced in English and translated into other languages, treating language pairs as the unit of evaluation---a design that is prone to contamination over time and overlooks locale and cultural considerations. We therefore advocate for source-contrastive evaluation and instantiate it with Cultivar, a localised subset of FLORES, which enables locale-specific translation evaluation. When paired with unlocalised counterparts, performance discrepancy allows the probing of data contamination and localisation robustness. We benchmark 32 open-weight models and find that MT-specialised models are less robust, a few models potentially overfit FLORES, and models tend to translate US content better than that of other locales, regardless of language.

</details>


### [235] [Structured Phonological Representations for Audio-Articulatory rtMRI Speech Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09767)
*Abner Hernandez,Tomás Arias Vergara,Daiqi Liu,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究PhonoQ（一个基于音频、识别结构化音系特征的模型）的表示是否对音频-发音建模有用。提取PhonoQ Conformer模块的表示，结合发音轮廓与同步音频特征，在unseen-speech和unseen-subject设置下，相比WavLM-large和HuBERT-large基线，提升了方式、部位、清浊音、元音高低和前后的macro-F1分数，以及细粒度39音素分类。在仅轮廓推理中，音频教师监督带来小幅但一致的提升。后验分析显示了与闪音/t/实现、/t/-/r/后缩或塞擦化、鼻音部位同化一致的可解释表层敏感模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时MRI可以在语音期间观察声道发音运动，但将这些发音模式映射到语音学和音系学类别仍然具有挑战性。作者希望探索音系特征知识能否桥接音频信号与发音轮廓之间的鸿沟，提升从发音数据推断音系类别的能力。

Method: 使用PhonoQ（一个训练于识别方式、部位、清浊音和元音特征的音频模型）从Conformer模块提取表示，将这些表示与发音轮廓及同步音频特征结合，训练模型以预测音系特征和音素类别。对比基線为WavLM-large和HuBERT-large，在unseen-speech和unseen-subject两种评估设置下进行。

Result: PhonoQ特征在两种设置下均提升了音系特征（方式、部位、清浊音、元音高度、元音后位）的macro-F1以及39音素细粒度分类。在仅发音轮廓的推理中，音频教师监督相比仅轮廓训练带来小幅但一致的提升。后验分析显示与闪音/t/实现、/t/-/r/后缩或塞擦化、鼻音部位同化一致的可解释模式。

Conclusion: PhonoQ的音频衍生的音系特征表示能有效增强发音-音系映射建模，音频中的音系信息可以部分迁移到发音模型中，且模型表现出与真实音系过程一致的可解释发音敏感模式。

Abstract: Real-time MRI makes it possible to observe vocal-tract articulation during speech, but mapping these articulatory patterns to phonetic and phonological categories remains challenging. We investigate whether PhonoQ, an audio-based model trained to recognize structured phonological features, provides useful information for audio--articulatory modeling. Specifically, we extract representations from PhonoQ's Conformer module, whose training is shaped by supervision for manner, place, voicing, and vowel features. Using articulatory contours with synchronized audio-derived features, we compare WavLM-large and HuBERT-large baselines with models that incorporate PhonoQ-derived representations. Across unseen-speech and unseen-subject settings, these features improve macro-F1 for phonological targets including manner, place, voicing, vowel height, and vowel backness, and also improve fine-grained 39-phoneme classification. In a contour-only inference setting, audio-derived teacher supervision yields modest but consistent gains over contour-only training, indicating that phonological information from synchronized audio can be partially transferred to articulatory models. Finally, posterior analyses show interpretable surface-sensitive patterns consistent with flapping-like /t/ realizations, /t/-/r/ retraction or affrication, and nasal place assimilation.

</details>


### [236] [PragMatch: Separating Pragmatic Incongruity from Cross-Modal Mismatch in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09772)
*Zhanna Mukhametsharip,Vera Demberg,Varsha Suresh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PragMatch基准测试，包含3,000个图像-文本对，用于评估大规模视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态讽刺检测中的推理能力。研究发现LVLMs的预测受到词汇、OCR提取和风格等浅层捷径线索的显著影响，说明当前模型依赖表面相关性而非真正的多模态语用推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs在多模态基准上表现优异，但尚不清楚它们是否真正理解图像与文本之间的关系，还是依赖浅层相关性（捷径学习）。这一问题的研究对多模态讽刺检测尤为重要，因为讽刺识别需要理解语用不协调而非简单的图像-文本不匹配。

Method: 基于MMSD2.0构建PragMatch受控基准，包含3,000个图像-文本对（原始讽刺样本、构造的字面样本和硬负样本）。通过系统性掩码识别有影响力的捷径线索，并通过定向注入实验评估这些线索对模型预测的影响。

Result: 实验表明，LVLM的预测对词汇、OCR提取和风格线索高度敏感；即使底层图像-文本关系不变，注入表面信号仍会导致模型预测发生实质性变化，揭示了当前LVLMs在多模态语用推理方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前LVLMs在多模态讽刺检测中倾向于依赖表面线索而非进行真正的多模态语用推理。PragMatch为评估超越表面图像-文本对齐的多模态语用推理提供了一个系统化的测试平台。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on multimodal benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they genuinely reason about relationships between images and text or rely on superficial correlations, known as shortcut learning. This question is particularly important for multimodal sarcasm detection, where successful prediction depends on recognizing pragmatic incongruity rather than treating sarcasm as simple image-text mismatch. We introduce PragMatch, a controlled benchmark of 3,000 image-text pairs derived from MMSD2.0, including original sarcastic examples and constructed literal and hard-negative pairs. We identify influential shortcut cues through systematic masking and evaluate their impact through targeted injection experiments. Our results show that LVLM predictions are sensitive to lexical, OCR-derived and stylistic cues, with injected surface signals causing substantial changes in model predictions despite unchanged underlying image-text relationships. Our findings reveal limitations in current LVLMs while PragMatch provides a systematic testbed for evaluating multimodal pragmatic reasoning beyond surface-level image-text alignment.

</details>


### [237] [KGCaRe: Explainable Complex Conditional Question Answering using Automatic Knowledge Graph Construction and Context Retrieval with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09779)
*Ghanshyam Verma,Simanta Sarkar,Devishree Pillai,Hotaka Shiokawa,Yourong Xu,Fiona Veazey,Peter Hubbert,Hui Su,Paul Buitelaar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KGCaRe，一种结合神经检索与符号推理的混合方法，通过从文档中构建知识图谱并配合迭代图遍历，解决复杂条件问答中通用LLM和RAG表现不佳的问题。该方法在多个数据集和多种LLM上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂条件问答在领域特定场景中对通用LLM和RAG仍然构成挑战，现有方法在这些任务上表现欠佳。作者假设将非结构化和结构化知识（来自文档和知识图谱）同时注入RAG可以改善推理和答案准确性。

Method: KGCaRe通过多提示提取策略从文档构建KG并存入图数据库，同时将文档嵌入向量库实现神经检索。系统执行LLM引导的迭代图遍历来提取相关三元组和剪枝无关信息，若初始遍历未能提供满意上下文，则利用额外线索实体再次遍历。最终将KG路径形式的相关三元组与语义检索的文本片段一起送入自定义KGCaRe提示中生成答案。

Result: 在两个复杂条件问答数据集上，KGCaRe在多种LLM（Mistral、Mixtral、GPT-3.5、GPT-4o）上均一致优于现有基线，包括Vanilla LLM、Code Prompt、Text Prompt、Think-on-Graph、Vanilla RAG和HybridContextQA。

Conclusion: KGCaRe验证了将文档中提取的知识图谱与神经检索相结合的混合方法能有效提升复杂条件问答的推理能力和答案准确率，且已公开发布实现该方法的软件流程。

Abstract: Answering complex conditional questions using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) remains a challenge, particularly in domain-specific contexts where general-purpose LLMs and RAG tend to underperform. We hypothesize that augmenting RAG with unstructured and structured knowledge, extracted from both documents and knowledge graphs (KGs), can improve reasoning and answer accuracy for such tasks.
  To test this, we propose KGCaRe, a hybrid approach that combines neural retrieval with symbolic reasoning over LLM-generated KGs. KGCaRe constructs a KG from documents using a multi-prompt extraction strategy and stores it in a graph database. Simultaneously, the documents are embedded into a vector store to enable neural retrieval. KGCaRe performs innovative iterative graph traversal guided by the LLM to extract relevant triples, prune irrelevant information, and uses additional clue entities to traverse the graph again if the initial traversal does not provide satisfactory context to generate the answer. The relevant triples extracted from the KG in path form, along with semantically retrieved text passages, are then fed into custom KGCaRe prompts to generate answers to the complex conditional questions with explanations.
  We evaluate KGCaRe on two complex conditional QA datasets. Our results on these datasets show that KGCaRe consistently outperforms existing baselines, including Vanilla LLM, Code Prompt, Text Prompt, Think-on-Graph, Vanilla RAG, and HybridContextQA, across multiple LLMs such as Mistral, Mixtral, GPT-3.5, and GPT-4o. We publicly release the software pipeline that we developed to implement the proposed KGCaRe approach.

</details>


### [238] [Comparing British and American Audio Description of Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09792)
*Igor Sterner,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了英国与美国配音描述（audio description）的差异，利用两地指南及从业者意见提出假设，并在包含206部电影的语料库上验证，定量证实了两地在词汇、进行体、被动结构、主观形容词、角色命名、场景提示及与对话/音乐重叠度等方面的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述（为盲人和视障人士叙述电影视觉内容的叙事技术）比一般叙事受到更多约束，需嵌入对话间隙并遵循各地的规范指南。虽然英美两地存在不同的音频描述标准，但此前缺乏基于大规模语料库的定量比较研究，作者希望以量化视角揭示两地音频描述实践的系统性差异。

Method: 作者首先利用英国和美国的音频描述指南文本，并结合领域内从业者的经验印象，提出若干具体假设。随后在一个已有的语料库上进行验证，该语料库包含206部电影，每部均有美国人和英国人分别创作的音频描述文本，从而可以直接对比两种地区风格的差异。

Result: 所有提出的假设均得到定量证据支持，具体差异涵盖：词汇选择、进行体（progressive aspect）的使用频率、被动结构的使用、主观形容词和修饰语的使用、角色命名的时机、场景线索方式，以及与电影对白和音乐的重叠程度等多个维度。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模语料库的定量分析，首次系统性地揭示了英国与美国音频描述实践之间的结构化差异，为音频描述这一叙事技术提供了新的量化研究视角。

Abstract: Narrating the visual component of movies is known as audio description. It is a narrative technique designed to enable blind and visually impaired individuals to follow the story. However, it is far more constrained than most narratives: the descriptions not only need to convey the story in the movie, but they must also fit into gaps between dialogue and they need to conform to guidelines that exist in each region. In this work, we compare audio description created in the United Kingdom against audio description created in the United States. We use guidelines written for these two regions, alongside the impressions from a practitioner in the field, to motivate specific hypotheses about the differences. We test these hypotheses against our pre-existing corpus, which provides both human-authored American and British audio description for each of 206 movies. Results provide quantitative evidence to uphold all tested hypotheses, including differences in lexicon, the use of the progressive aspect, the use of passive constructions, the use of subjective adjectives and modifiers, when characters are named, how scenes are cued, and degree of overlap with movie dialogue and music. Our work offers a quantitative lens into the narrative technique of audio description.

</details>


### [239] [SWE-Bench ProMax: Benchmarking Agents on Large-Scale Multilingual Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09802)
*Yuling Shi,Jinghan Xu,Kelin Fu,Wenhao Zeng,Shilin He,Lei Zhang,Yue Liu,Zelin Zhao,Terry Yue Zhuo,Jialun Cao,Siyu Ye,Tianyu Liu,Kai Cai,Shing-Chi Cheung,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SWE-Bench ProMax，一个专家精选的多语言代码重构基准，包含来自七个编程语言的170个实例，平均每个实例修改11.4个文件、261.6行代码。该基准通过重写问题描述和人工审查测试集，解决了现有基准（如SWE-bench Verified）中近60%未解实例存在有缺陷测试的问题。实验表明，前沿模型在两种agent框架下最高仅达到41.2%的解决率，确认该基准对当前AI编码代理构成有意义且未饱和的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编码代理基准正迅速饱和，且评估质量受到严重质疑：审计发现SWE-bench Verified中近60%未解实例包含有缺陷的测试（过于狭窄或过于宽泛），前沿模型甚至可以从训练数据中逐字复现黄金补丁。代码重构任务虽能提供更难、更真实的代理能力测试，但当前基准对此覆盖不足。

Method: 作者构建了SWE-Bench ProMax基准，采用多阶段严格筛选流程：(1) 从七个编程语言（Python、Java、TypeScript、Go、C、C++、Rust）的真实提交中提取170个实例；(2) 从头重写问题描述以提供精确、无歧义的规格说明；(3) 人工审查测试集，去除过于狭窄和过于宽泛的测试；(4) 过滤复杂度不足或跨文件范围有限的低质量任务，最终保留平均修改11.4个文件、261.6行代码的大规模重构任务。

Result: 在两种agent脚手架下对前沿模型进行实验，最佳模型仅达到41.2%的解决率，表明SWE-Bench ProMax对当前AI编码代理构成了有意义且未饱和的挑战。该基准在任务规模（文件数和代码行数）上大幅超越现有基准。

Conclusion: SWE-Bench ProMax通过严格的质量控制和聚焦高复杂度跨文件重构任务，提供了更可靠、更具挑战性的编码代理评估基准，弥补了现有基准质量缺陷和任务复杂度不足的问题，为未来编码代理能力的提升提供了有效的衡量标准。

Abstract: As AI coding agents take on increasingly complex, long-horizon software engineering tasks, existing benchmarks are rapidly saturating and their evaluation quality has come under serious scrutiny: a recent audit found that nearly 60% of unsolved SWE-bench Verified instances contain flawed tests -- either overly narrow tests that reject correct solutions or overly broad tests that check unstated requirements -- and that frontier models can verbatim reproduce gold patches from training data. Code refactoring, which requires coordinated, behavior-preserving changes across many files, offers a substantially harder and more realistic test of agent capability, yet remains underserved by current benchmarks. We introduce SWE-Bench ProMax, an expert-curated, multilingual code refactoring benchmark of 170 instances drawn from real commits across seven programming languages (Python, Java, TypeScript, Go, C, C++, and Rust). Every instance undergoes rigorous, multi-stage curation that directly addresses the quality problems identified in prior benchmarks: issue descriptions are rewritten from scratch to provide precise, unambiguous specifications, and test suites are manually reviewed to remove overly narrow and overly broad tests. Tasks with insufficient complexity or limited cross-file scope are filtered out, yielding a benchmark of challenging, large-scale refactoring tasks that average 11.4 modified files and 261.6 lines of code per instance, substantially exceeding the scale of existing benchmarks. Experiments with frontier models under two agent scaffolds show that the best model achieves only 41.2% resolve rate, confirming that SWE-Bench ProMax presents a meaningful and unsaturated challenge for current AI coding agents. Our benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/swe-bench-promax/SWE-Bench-ProMax.

</details>


### [240] [RA-FinBERT: Rule-aware LoRA adaptation for low-resource financial sentiment classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09834)
*Fan Zhang,Jiaming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-FinBERT，一个参数高效的金融情感分析框架，将LoRA微调与VADER规则派生的情感特征及来源元数据相结合，仅增加1024个可训练参数，在金融新闻标题及描述的三分类任务上相比纯文本FinBERT提升了约6.5%的准确率和显著的宏F1分数（0.526→0.634），中性类召回率从18.18%提升至45.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源高效的金融NLP研究主要聚焦于压缩或适配预训练语言模型，较少关注将上下文表示与轻量级规则派生特征相结合。作者旨在探索在参数受限条件下，如何利用VADER规则特征和元数据信号来增强FinBERT的金融情感分类性能。

Method: RA-FinBERT采用LoRA低秩适配进行参数高效微调，同时提取三个连续的VADER派生情感比例（正面、负面、中性）和一个来源级元数据特征，形成标准化四维特征向量，与FinBERT最终层的768维[CLS]表示直接拼接，送入轻量级分类头。相比结构匹配的纯文本FinBERT，仅额外引入1024个可训练权重。

Result: 在金融新闻标题和描述的三分类情感任务上，RA-FinBERT在测试集上达到69.89%准确率和0.634宏F1，显著优于纯文本FinBERT（63.44%、0.526）和轻量级DistilBERT基线。中性类召回率从18.18%大幅提升至45.45%。模型同时支持CPU和GPU运行。

Conclusion: 规则派生情感信息和来源元数据能为上下文FinBERT表示提供互补信号，以极小的额外模型复杂度显著提升金融情感分类性能，为计算资源受限场景提供了一种轻量实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis converts unstructured financial news into quantitative signals that can support market analysis and decision-making. Existing work on resource-efficient financial NLP has largely focused on compressing or adapting pretrained language models, with less attention to combining contextual representations with lightweight rule-derived features. This study develops Rule-Aware FinBERT (RA-FinBERT), a parameter-efficient framework that integrates low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with three continuous VADER-derived sentiment proportions (positive, negative, and neutral) and a source-level metadata feature. The standardized four-dimensional feature vector is directly concatenated with the 768-dimensional final-layer FinBERT [CLS] representation and passed through a lightweight classification head. This design introduces only 1,024 additional trainable weights relative to a structurally matched text-only FinBERT model. RA-FinBERT was evaluated against text-only FinBERT and a lightweight DistilBERT baseline for three-class sentiment classification of financial-news titles and descriptions. On the held-out test set, RA-FinBERT achieved 69.89% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.634, compared with 63.44% and 0.526 for text-only FinBERT. Neutral-class recall increased from 18.18% to 45.45%. The framework supports both CPU and GPU execution, offering a lightweight and practical approach to financial sentiment classification under constrained computational resources. These findings indicate that rule-derived sentiment information and source metadata can provide complementary signals to contextual FinBERT representations and improve performance with minimal additional model complexity.

</details>


### [241] [Fusion Training for Mathematical Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09893)
*Congfeng Cao,Pengyu Zhang,Jelke Bloem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了思考模式融合（TMF）中两种模式（思考模式与非思考模式）之间的数据比例和训练调度对模型性能的影响，发现两者之间存在不对称交互和内在张力，为TMF训练设置提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMF使大语言模型能够在单一模型中同时支持简洁回答和长形式推理，但其训练动力学（包括两种模式之间的数据比例和训练调度）尚未被充分探索。

Method: 聚焦数学问题求解，构建了一个包含多种思考/非思考数据比例和三种训练调度的基准测试（Fusion Bench），系统地分析训练调度和数据比例对TMF的影响。

Result: 揭示了两种模式之间的不对称交互：增加非思考监督的比例会降低思考模式的准确性；不同训练调度会调节这种权衡，最优调度取决于数据比例；量化了非思考模式与思考模式监督之间的负相关，表明两者存在内在张力。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计有效的TMF训练设置提供了实用指导，并已开源所有代码和数据以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Thinking Mode Fusion (TMF) enables large language models to support both concise responses and long-form reasoning by unifying a non-thinking mode and a thinking mode within a single model. However, its training dynamics, including the \emph{data ratio} and \emph{training schedule} between the two modes, remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic study of TMF by analyzing the effects of the training schedule and data ratio between thinking and non-thinking modes. Focusing on mathematical problem solving, we construct a benchmark with multiple thinking-to-non-thinking data ratios and three training schedules. Our results reveal an asymmetric interaction between the two modes: increasing the ratio of non-thinking supervision reduces the accuracy of the thinking mode. We further show that different training schedules modulate this trade-off and that the optimal schedule depends on the data ratio. Finally, we quantify a negative correlation between non-thinking and thinking mode supervision, highlighting an inherent tension between these two modes. These findings provide practical guidance for designing effective TMF training settings. All code and data are released to support further research at: \href{https://github.com/caocongfeng/Fusion-Bench.git}{\textbf{Fusion Bench}}.

</details>


### [242] [Consilience for Verifier-Free Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09898)
*Lecheng Kong,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对免验证器测试时扩展（VF-TTS）中的基于置信度的方法，发现其在高复杂度任务上会因"均匀高置信度"而崩溃（倾向于自信但错误的答案），并提出consilience框架，通过评估推理过程中置信度的时间不对称性（低初始置信度+高最终置信度）来筛选高质量rollouts，在研究生级数学问题和自由形式代码生成上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VF-TTS方法，尤其是基于置信度的方法，在复杂任务上会灾难性失效——模型均匀地给出高置信度常常意味着探索失败，倾向选择自信但错误的答案。这一局限性尚未被系统揭示和解决，亟需一种能够捕捉"何时自信"而非仅仅"有多自信"的筛选机制。

Method: 提出consilience框架：核心假设是稳健的认知搜索需要特定的置信度轨迹模式——初始阶段低置信度以促进探索性分支，最终收敛到高置信度的解决方案。该框架通过一个组合度量来衡量置信度的时间不对称性，主动惩罚高初始置信度，同时严格要求最终确定性，以此对rollouts进行排序和筛选。

Result: 在研究生级数学问题和自由形式代码生成两类任务的广泛实验中，consilience显著优于现有VF-TTS基线，验证了"完成置信度的时间特性"这一新颖视角的有效性。

Conclusion: 仅关注置信度的绝对水平是不够的，置信度的演化轨迹（时间不对称性）才是区分高质量与低质量推理的关键信号。consilience为VF-TTS提供了一种零开销、模型无关且可推广的筛选框架。

Abstract: Test-time scaling often uses an external verifier, such as compilers and test cases in coding or trained value functions in robotics applications, to obtain high-quality rollouts. Verifier-free test-time scaling (or VF-TTS) is gaining extensive attention as a mechanism to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, primarily because we do not have access to such high-quality verifiers in many real-world applications. Among existing VF-TTS methods, confidence-based VF-TTS methods, which compute and rank rollouts solely by confidence, are particularly promising. Such methods introduce near-zero overhead for sample evaluation and require minimal access to internal model states, making the methods highly flexible across models and tasks.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a critical limitation of existing confidence-based VF-TTS methods by showing that such methods catastrophically break down on complex tasks. We observe a very interesting phenomenon: uniformly high confidence frequently indicates a failure to explore, favoring confidently wrong answers. To address this, our core insight is that robust cognitive search requires a specific confidence trajectory pattern: such methods perform exploratory branching at the beginning, as manifested by low initial confidence, and converge to a high final confidence solution. To implement this insight, we introduce consilience, a novel selection framework that explicitly evaluates the temporal asymmetry of confidence in reasoning. We operationalize this via a combinatorial metric that actively penalizes high initial confidence while strictly demanding final certainty. Extensive experiments covering both graduate-level mathematics problems and free-form code generation demonstrate that consilience effectively outperforms existing baselines, validating our novel perspective on completion confidence.

</details>


### [243] [Decoding-Level Taboo: A Diagnostic Stress Test for LLM Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09900)
*Tadanobu Chuyo Kamijo,Ori Rottenstreich,Javier Conde,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Decoding-Level Taboo，一种零提示（zero-prompt）诊断性压力测试方法，通过直接在logit空间运行时屏蔽主候选token，强迫模型偏离其惯常的生成路径，从而在部署前揭示模型在非名义条件下的真实鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估大多在名义条件下进行，导致模型在狭窄且高度优化的生成路径上表现良好，产生"能力幻觉"。然而真实部署中，复杂的系统提示、安全护栏和结构性约束会持续将模型推离名义路径，造成基准分数与部署性能之间的鸿沟。作者旨在解决这一评估与真实部署之间的落差问题。

Method: 引入Decoding-Level Taboo——一种零提示诊断性压力测试方法，在运行时直接干预logit空间，在词边界处动态屏蔽主要候选token，从而强制模型进行迂回表达（circumlocution）。该方法通过解码层级的干预将模型推出其名义路径，无需额外提示或任务设计。

Result: 在多个开源权重模型家族上的评估显示，路径外鲁棒性受参数规模和后训练指令对齐的显著影响，通常模型越大、对齐程度越高，鲁棒性越好。

Conclusion: Taboo不仅是一种诊断工具，还提供了一种新颖的原语，可用于生成多样化的合成数据集、压力测试运行时安全护栏，以及在真实部署前审计模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model evaluations typically focus on performance under nominal conditions, creating an illusion of capability where models comfortably walk a narrow, highly optimized generation corridor. In real-world deployments, however, complex system prompts, safety guardrails, and structural constraints continuously force models off this nominal path, driving a divergence between benchmark scores and deployment performance. To address this issue, we introduce Decoding-Level Taboo, a zero-prompt diagnostic stress test that intervenes directly in logit space at runtime, forcing models out of their nominal paths. By dynamically masking primary candidate tokens at word boundaries, Taboo forces machine circumlocution.
  Evaluating Taboo across several open-weight model families reveals that off-path robustness is heavily influenced by both parameter scale and post-training instruction alignment, with robustness generally improving with model size and alignment. Beyond the results presented in this paper, Taboo provides a novel primitive for generating diverse synthetic datasets, stress-testing runtime safety guardrails, and auditing model reliability prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [244] [From Values to Benchmarks: Evaluating Large Language Models for Governmental Use in Dutch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09925)
*Laurens Samson,Iva Gornishka,Gossa Lô,Yuki M. Asano,Sennay Ghebreab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Grip on LLMs"框架，一个面向荷兰政府场景的大语言模型系统评估套件，涵盖事实性、诚实性、社会偏见、能耗、成本和训练数据透明度六个维度，并通过对30多个多语言及荷兰语专属模型的评测发现：没有单一模型在所有维度上表现优异，质量提升与环境和成本代价不可兼得，且事实性与诚实性由不同属性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益被部署到政府场景中，但现有评估框架很少同时兼顾公共行政的价值取向以及非英语语境的语言要求，缺乏系统性、多维度、面向荷兰语政府用途的模型评估工具。

Method: 与荷兰主要市政机构的领域专家合作，通过咨询委员会流程、用户研究以及对公务员聊天机器人用户的调查，识别出六个评估维度（事实性、诚实性、社会偏见、能耗、成本、训练数据透明度），并将其操作化为覆盖30多个多语言和荷兰语专属模型的基准测试套件。

Result: 没有任何单一模型能在所有维度上表现卓越，质量越高意味着环境足迹和财务成本越大，而偏见与二者基本无关；事实性（回答是否正确）和诚实性（是否承认不知道）由不同属性驱动，高事实性并不必然意味着高诚实性。

Conclusion: 为让发现对非技术受众可行，作者发布了一个对公众开放、用户友好的模型概览工具，面向从工程师到政策制定者的政府LLM选型全链条利益相关者。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being deployed in governmental settings, yet few existing evaluation frameworks jointly reflect the values of public administration and the linguistic requirements of non-English contexts. We present the "Grip on LLMs" framework, a systematic evaluation suite for Dutch governmental use developed in collaboration with domain experts from a major Dutch municipal organisation. Through an advisory board process, user research, and a survey of the users of a civil-servant chatbot, we identify six evaluation dimensions (factuality, honesty, social bias, energy consumption, cost, and training data transparency) and operationalise them into a benchmark suite covering more than 30 multilingual and Dutch-specific models. Our results reveal that no single model excels across all dimensions, and that trade-offs are unavoidable: higher quality consistently comes at greater environmental impact and financial cost, while bias remains largely independent of both. We further find that factuality (whether a model answers correctly) and honesty (whether a model acknowledges what it does not know) are governed by distinct properties, with high factuality not implying high honesty. To make these findings actionable for non-technical audiences, we release a publicly accessible, user-friendly model overview designed for the full range of stakeholders involved in governmental LLM selection, from engineers to policymakers.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Differentiate the Solver, Not the Equation: Reverse-Sweep Adjoints for Block Implicit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08559)
*Lei Shu,Ying Jiang,Kui Wu,Yin Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出"求解器级微分"（solver-level differentiation）框架，直接对实际执行的迭代求解器本身进行反向微分，而非对收敛方程或展开的计算图求导。在Vertex Block Descent上实例化后，其反向过程由局部3×3伴随求解构成的彩色Gauss-Seidel扫描组成，不构造任何全局系统，在精度上匹配自动微分到机器精度，速度比展开式自动微分快33倍、省71倍内存，并可将可微弹动力学扩展到单GPU上10^6个接触耦合软体（8M顶点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微模拟中的梯度计算存在根本性权衡：展开式自动微分内存随求解器深度线性增长，而方程级隐式微分需要装配全局Jacobian并求解大规模稀疏伴随系统，丢弃了前向求解器的局部性，且微分的是收敛方程而非实际执行的有穷计算。这促使作者探索是否可以直接微分实际运行的求解器本身，以同时获得两者的优点。

Method: 提出"求解器级微分"范式：当求解器由块隐式更新（block implicit updates）组成时，其离散伴随通过对相应的伴随更新按逆序应用获得，形成反向扫描公式，使得反向传播与前向求解器完全镜像。从算子角度看，前向过程通过有序局部求解实现近似逆，反向则通过反向局部伴随求解应用其转置，不构造全局系统。作者将该思想实例化于Vertex Block Descent（顶点块下降），得到由局部3×3伴随求解组成的反向彩色Gauss-Seidel扫描。

Result: 该方法的反向传播与通过相同前向计算路径的自动微分在任意求解器深度均匹配到机器精度，而方程级伴随在一次扫描后偏差达37%；在受控的同一代码库、同GPU对比中，速度比展开式自动微分快33倍，内存占用低71倍；同样的构造在projective dynamics和extended position-based dynamics上也是精确的；成功将可微弹动力学扩展到单GPU上10^6个接触耦合软体（8M顶点）。

Conclusion: 该工作将求解器结构确立为高效可微模拟的实用组织原则，表明微分"实际执行的有限计算"而非"收敛后的方程"，能够在保持精确性的同时获得显著的效率与可扩展性优势。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation is a key component in learning, control, and inverse problems, where gradients through nonlinear implicit solvers are required. Existing approaches either rely on unrolled automatic differentiation, whose memory grows with solver depth, or on equation-level implicit differentiation, which assembles global Jacobians and solves large sparse adjoint systems, discarding the locality of the forward solver -- and differentiating the converged equation rather than the finite computation that actually ran. We propose solver-level differentiation, which differentiates the executed solver itself. When a solver is composed of block implicit updates, its discrete adjoint is obtained by applying the corresponding adjoint updates in reverse order, yielding a reverse-sweep formulation whose backward pass mirrors the forward solver. From an operator perspective, the forward pass realizes an approximate inverse through ordered local solves, and the backward applies its transpose through reverse local adjoint solves, constructing no global system. We instantiate this idea on Vertex Block Descent, yielding a differentiable solver whose reverse colored Gauss-Seidel sweeps are composed entirely of local $3\times 3$ adjoint solves. The backward matches automatic differentiation through the identical executed forward to machine precision at every solver depth, where the equation-level adjoint is off by 37% after one sweep; in a controlled same-codebase, same-GPU comparison it is 33x faster and uses 71x less memory than unrolled automatic differentiation; and the same construction is exact on projective dynamics and extended position-based dynamics. We scale differentiable elastodynamics to $10^6$ contact-coupled soft bodies (8M vertices) on one GPU. Overall, this work highlights solver structure as a practical organizing principle for efficient differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [246] [Adaptive Volumetric Parameterization of Simply Connected 3-Manifolds with Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08672)
*Zhiyuan Lyu,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向单连通三维流形的体积参数化框架，通过联合控制局部形状与质量畸变，并优化过程中自适应调整目标域（包括预设实心椭球、可变半径的体积归一化自适应椭球、以及免边界域三种设置），实现了低畸变的高质量体积参数化，可应用于多分辨率自适应体积网格重建、体积配准和体积形变等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数先前的体积参数化方法仅使用标准域（如实心球）作为目标域，忽略了给定三维流形的整体形状，导致显著几何畸变并影响后续形状处理与分析任务。研究者希望克服这一局限，提出能适应目标流形形状、降低畸变的体积参数化框架。

Method: 该框架包含三个组成部分：1) 三维拟共形形状更新用于控制局部形状畸变；2) 基于扩散的密度均衡更新用于控制质量畸变；3) 几何校正过程用于移除单元折叠。框架支持三种渐进的、更灵活的目标域设置：预设实心椭球、体积归一化自适应椭球（半径可变）、以及海-嵌入免边界域，每种设置均结合上述三步更新算法实现不同效果的体积参数化。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架对体积参数化问题是有效的；同时该框架可轻松扩展到多分辨率与局部自适应体积网格重建、体积配准和体积形变等多个应用场景。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维流形的表示、处理与分析提供了一种新途径，通过自适应目标域与联合畸变控制策略，显著改善了体积参数化的质量与灵活性。

Abstract: Volumetric parameterization, the process of mapping a 3-manifold onto a simplified volumetric domain, is important for many tasks in computer graphics and imaging science. However, most prior volumetric parameterization approaches have only utilized standardized domains such as a solid ball regardless of the overall shape of the given 3-manifolds, which introduces significant geometric distortion and affects the subsequent shape processing and analysis tasks. To overcome this issue, in this work we propose a novel volumetric parameterization framework for simply connected 3-manifolds. Specifically, the proposed framework jointly controls local shape and mass distortions, while adapting the target domain during the optimization process. It enables three progressively more flexible target-domain settings for the parameterization: a prescribed solid ellipsoid, a volume-normalized adaptive ellipsoid with variable radii, and a sea-embedded free-boundary domain. For each setting, the parameterization algorithm consists of a 3D quasi-conformality shape update, a diffusion-based density-equalizing update, and a geometric correction procedure for removing element foldings, thereby allowing for volumetric parameterizations with different desired effects. Experimental results are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed framework. Moreover, our framework can be easily applied to multiresolution and localized adaptive volumetric remeshing, volumetric registration, and volumetric morphing. Altogether, our work provides a new way for the representation, processing, and analysis of 3-manifolds.

</details>


### [247] [Smooth Motion Stitching via Laplacian Optimization in Rodrigues Vector Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08986)
*Ryosuke Higasayama,Hideki Todo,Jongseong Gwak*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Rodrigues向量空间中拉普拉斯优化的运动编辑框架，用于平滑运动拼接。通过将关节旋转表示为连续Rodrigues向量，运动拼接被建模为时间维度的拉普拉斯优化问题，可在保留参考运动特征性时间变化的同时实现运动片段间的平滑过渡。该方法支持类内替换和跨类别运动拼接，无需学习模型或复杂手动调参，计算高效适用于交互式编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动拼接方法往往依赖学习模型或需要复杂的人工调参，且对旋转表示的选择影响时间优化的数值稳定性。作者希望提出一种无需学习、计算高效的运动拼接框架，同时通过分析旋转连续性来理解并避免数值不稳定性问题。

Method: 将关节旋转表示为连续Rodrigues向量，将运动拼接形式化为时间维度的拉普拉斯优化问题。通过拉普拉斯编辑实现运动片段间的平滑过渡，同时保持参考运动特征性的时间变化，并支持类内替换与跨类别拼接。

Result: 通过一系列拼接实验以及与线性插值的对比，拉普拉斯编辑在广泛运动差异范围内产生稳定且视觉连贯的过渡。旋转连续性分析表明，真实运动数据中旋转轴反转罕见，这解释了合成轴翻转场景中的数值不稳定不会出现在实际运动拼接中。

Conclusion: 旋转表示对稳定时间优化至关重要，该框架不仅适用于动画创作，还可扩展到运动分析和融合感知或生理线索的未来扩展中。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion editing framework for smooth motion stitching based on Laplacian optimization in Rodrigues vector space. By representing joint rotations as continuous Rodrigues vectors, motion stitching is formulated as a temporal Laplacian optimization problem, enabling smooth transitions between motion segments while preserving characteristic temporal variations of reference motions. The proposed approach supports both intra-category replacement and cross-category motion stitching without relying on learning-based models or complex manual tuning, and is computationally efficient for interactive editing. Through a series of stitching experiments and comparisons with linear interpolation, we demonstrate that Laplacian editing produces stable and visually coherent transitions under a wide range of motion differences. Furthermore, an analysis of rotational continuity clarifies that rotation-axis inversions are rare in real motion data and explains why numerical instabilities observed in synthetic axis-flipping scenarios do not arise in practical motion stitching. These results highlight the importance of rotational representation in stabilizing temporal optimization and suggest that the proposed framework is well suited not only for animation authoring but also for motion analysis and future extensions incorporating perceptual or physiological cues.

</details>


### [248] [A Hybrid Neural-Microfacet BRDF Model for Real-Time Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09604)
*Louis De Oliveira,Anastasia Karpova,Georges Nader,Antoine Houdard,Pierre Mezieres,Damien Rioux-Lavoie,Romain Pacanowski*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合BRDF模型，将GGX型微表面模型与神经网络模型相结合，用较小的神经网络修正微表面模型的近似外观，在相同内存成本下比现有神经模型更精确地逼近测量数据，同时保持低计算开销和艺术家可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的微表面BRDF模型无法复现复杂光-表面相互作用产生的微妙外观效果，而专门物理模型虽然精确但缺乏通用性和实时性能。现有的神经BRDF模型虽然能逼近各种参考数据，但网络规模较大，实时渲染成本过高。

Method: 提出混合模型：将GGX型微表面模型与一个较小的神经网络模型结合，利用神经网络组件修正微表面组件的外观逼近误差，从而使网络规模远小于现有纯神经模型。该混合模型保持艺术家可编辑性，并具备重要性采样方案。

Result: 在相同内存成本下，该混合模型对测量数据的逼近效果优于现有最先进的神经模型，同时相比微表面模型的评估开销增加很小，适用于离线和实时渲染。

Conclusion: 通过混合GGX微表面模型和小型神经网络，在保持低计算开销、高编辑性和重要性采样能力的同时，实现了比现有神经模型更精确的BRDF逼近，兼顾了物理模型和神经模型的优势。

Abstract: Over the past decade, microfacet-based BRDF models have formed the foundation of real-time rendering pipelines. Despite their widespread use, they often fail to reproduce subtle appearance effects arising from complex light-surface interactions, which have led to the emergence of specialized physics-based models for specific optical phenomena (e.g., diffraction, iridescence, multilayers). Although more accurate, these models lose versatility and lack performance for real-time rendering. Recently introduced, neural models have demonstrated their ability to approximate BRDF reference data coming from measurements, simulations, or even complex shading networks. However, most current neural models require relatively large networks, making them costly for real-time rendering. In this paper, we introduce a hybrid model that combines a GGX-type microfacet model and a neural model to leverage the best features of both representations. The neural component corrects the appearance approximated by the microfacet component, allowing much smaller network than in existing neural models. We show that, at identical memory cost, our model approximates measurements better than state-of-the-art neural models for a low evaluation overhead compared to a microfacet-based model. Furthermore, our hybrid model remains easily editable by artists and benefits from an important sampling scheme, making it attractive for both offline and real-time rendering.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），主张将（计算）论证（argumentation）作为评估式AI（Evaluative AI, EAI）的形式化、可计算基础，以实现可解释且可质疑的EAI系统，并为面向分布式和人本中心的EAI系统奠定长期研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AI辅助决策通常输出单一推荐结果，缺乏对决策过程的透明性和可质疑性。EAI通过呈现相互竞争的假设及其正反证据来支持人类决策，但需要一个形式化、可计算的理论基础来确保其可解释性和可争议性。

Method: 作者提倡采用（计算）论证作为EAI的形式化基础，利用论证框架中的支持与反对关系来表征竞争假设及其证据，从而为可解释且可争议的EAI设计提供计算模型。

Result: 本文为立场论文，未提供具体实验或实现结果，而是提出研究议程和论证框架，确立计算论证作为EAI形式化基础的研究方向。

Conclusion: 计算论证是支撑可解释、可争议的EAI系统的合适范式，能够为分布式和人本中心的EAI系统奠定长期研究基础。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [250] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Flow-by-Flow"治理范式，指出人类监督AI输出的根本瓶颈不是AI产出速度V本身，而是V×L（产出速度×每项认知负荷）。由于AI能力提升仅降低判断成本，而三大部分（分诊、判断、响应）成本下降不对称，能力提升只是重构而非降低监督负荷。为此，作者提出基于形式化可计数特征的非内容性监督框架，并给出四个设计不变量及Monte Carlo验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理依赖人工监督AI输出正确性，但在高损失场景中当AI产出速度超过人类认知容量（V > C_max）时，人类介入式监督在结构上不可行。且学术界普遍忽视线索：真正约束条件不是V，而是V×L（其中L为每项的认知负荷），因此需要重新审视治理机制的可行性边界。

Method: 作者将认知负荷L拆分为三部分（分诊、判断、响应），逐项分析其对AI能力提升的弹性；提出Flow-by-Flow治理范式，用基于形式化可计数特征（formal, countable features）构建认知成本评分对高产量施加非线性成本，并通过制度性容量上限（institutional capacity cap）将处理量约束在C_max内。推导出四个设计不变量（不评判内容、不可扩展消耗审查者容量、身份绑定的每应用摩擦、无批量放行），并通过1,000次参数抽样的Monte Carlo分析比较多指标流量控制与单纯监督强化的效果。

Result: Monte Carlo分析（1,000次参数抽样）显示，复合多指标流量控制（composite multi-metric flow control）在90.8%的试验中优于单纯监督强化（supervision reinforcement alone）。所提出的四个设计不变量通过一个参考实现被证明可联合满足，但作者也明确承认该实现的实践困难。

Conclusion: AI能力提升无法通过降低人类监督负荷来解决监督瓶颈，因为它只压缩判断成本且往往以"省略"方式实现，而非真正降低负担。治理必须绕过内容评估（content-judgment-bypass），通过形式化结构特征控制流量而非评价质量，Flow-by-Flow范式提供了一条可行的治理路径，但其实践落地仍面临显著挑战。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [251] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于从多元结构理论构建规范诠释的形式化框架，将规范化的非确定性分类为认知多元性（Type E）和结构多元性（Type S），并分别通过封闭算子完成和选择子构造两种机制实现规范化。该框架还应用于LLM辅助推理中对幻觉的形式化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构理论中（由签名、公理和推理策略组成的三元组），如何从多个可能的诠释中选择规范（canonical）诠释是一个基础性问题。现有的规范化方法缺乏统一的形式化框架，非确定性的来源（认知层面还是结构层面）混淆不清，且不同规范化层级（种子收敛、种子无关收敛、唯一可容许诠释）之间的关系缺乏严格的刻画。

Method: 定义结构理论T=(Σ,A,I)及其全局一致可容许诠释族；区分三个规范化层级（闭合稳定化、全局完备化、确定化）；将非确定性分类为认知多元性（Type E）与结构多元性（Type S），并细化出S-强子类（由共同上界缺失刻画）；提出两种规范化机制——基于算子的完备化和基于选择子的构造，并给出其存在的充分结构条件；通过分层算子证明多层级规范化形成结构上非交换的体系，给出将理论内在机制归约为闭合或选择的分类定理。

Result: 纯推理型完备化在正非回归规则外加健全性条件下归结为饱和闭包算子；对Type E理论建立了闭合稳定化，完全确定化取决于一个仍开放的整体汇合性质；对Type S-强理论通过规范选择实现确定化；多层级规范化通过分层算子显示为非交换结构；证明条件性分类定理将理论内在机制归为闭合或选择两类。

Conclusion: 该框架为多元结构理论的规范诠释提供了统一的形式化基础，揭示了非确定性的两种本质来源及其对应的规范化机制，并将理论推广到LLM辅助推理场景，将幻觉形式化为不受支持的规范化操作。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
  Two canonicalization mechanisms arise: operator-based completion and selector-based construction. We provide sufficient structural conditions under which these mechanisms exist, and show that pure inference-based completion reduces to a saturated closure operator under positive, non-retractive rules with an additional soundness condition. For Type E theories, closure stabilization is established, while full determinization depends on a global confluence property that remains open. For Type S-strong theories, determinization is achieved via canonical selection.
  We further show that multi-level canonicalization forms a structurally non-commutative system via staged operators, and provide a conditional classification theorem reducing theory-intrinsic mechanisms to closure or selection. The framework also applies to LLM-assisted reasoning, where hallucination can be viewed as unsupported canonicalization.

</details>


### [252] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的主动推理驾驶模型，将情感状态（效价和唤醒度）纳入连续状态空间的驾驶行为建模中，并通过预测未来结果来调节情感估计，在两种交互驾驶场景中验证了情感信号与真实情感模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推理驾驶模型未能考虑情感状态这一影响决策的重要因素；先前非驾驶领域的主动推理情感建模仅限于离散状态空间的简化场景，无法应用于连续状态下的复杂驾驶情境。

Method: 提出一种在连续状态空间中从主动推理驾驶模型中提取效价和唤醒度指标的扩展公式，情感估计不仅基于当前状态，还基于预测的未来结果进行条件化，从而在交互驾驶场景中生成更符合实际的情感信号。

Result: 在两个交互式驾驶场景中的评估表明，所提出的情感信号与类似场景中报告的真实情感模式相匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将连续状态空间中的情感建模与主动推理驾驶框架相结合，证明了在复杂驾驶场景中生成逼真情感信号的可能性，为更具人性化的自动驾驶决策建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


### [253] [Training Variable Long Sequences with Data-Centric Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07524)
*Geng Zhang,Xuanlei Zhao,Kai Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Data-Centric Parallel (DCP)方法，通过让数据本身驱动运行时，动态调整并行大小、梯度累积和重计算等运行时设置，以匹配每个batch的序列长度，打破传统方法在效率与易用性之间的权衡。在32个H200 GPU上取得最高2.88倍加速，仅需10行代码即可集成到任何模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练变长序列的深度学习模型面临显著的计算挑战，现有方法在效率和易用性之间存在艰难的权衡：简单方法使用静态配置导致工作负载不均衡和低效率，而复杂方法引入显著的复杂性和代码改动。

Method: DCP的核心原则是让数据本身驱动运行时。系统根据每个batch的序列长度，动态调整直接运行时设置，包括并行大小（parallel size）、梯度累积（gradient accumulation）和重计算（recomputation）等，从而适配不同长度的序列负载。

Result: 在32个H200 GPU上实现最高2.88倍的加速，并且设计具有强泛化性，仅需10行代码即可集成到任何模型中。

Conclusion: 这种简单而有效的方法有望作为鲁棒的基线，促进分布式训练在变长序列场景下的未来发展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on variable long sequences poses significant computational challenges. Existing methods force a difficult trade-off between efficiency and ease-of-use. Simple approaches use static configurations that cause workload imbalance low efficiency, while complex methods introduces significant complexity and code change for new models. To break this trade-off, we introduce Data-Centric Parallel (DCP). Its core principle is to let the data itself drive the runtime. It achieves this by dynamically adjusting direct runtime settings (e.g., parallel size, gradient accumulation, recomputation) based on each batch's sequence length. Empirical results demonstrate that our method achieves up to a 2.88$\times$ speedup on 32 H200 GPUs. Designed for generalization, it can be integrated into any model with 10 lines of code. We anticipate this simple yet effective approach will serve as a robust baseline and facilitate future advancements in distributed training for variable long sequences.

</details>


### [254] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现线性探针虽能以近乎完美的准确率检测语言模型中的损坏上下文，却无法可靠预测最终答案的错误，形成"知而不言"的分离现象。该现象在多个模型族（含推理模型）中普遍存在。探针类干预（branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）的收益高度依赖模型与错误类型，没有任何单一干预方案占优，实际部署需采用模型感知和错误类型感知的路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针在检测上下文损坏方面表现出极高的准确率，但这一能力是否能转化为可靠的错误预测尚不清楚。探明探针检测能力与最终答案正确性预测之间的关联，对于部署监控具有直接意义，且涉及多重假设（如"持久性胜过峰值"）需要实证检验。

Method: 在多跳算术链上设计实验：1）构建线性探针检测上下文损坏并评估其对最终答案正确性的预测能力；2）将模型强制置于结构化置信度格式中观察输出分布；3）跨跳追踪探针持久性以检验"persistence beats peak"假设；4）在多种模型族（含推理模型）上对比三种探针干预策略（branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）的效果。

Result: 探针检测损坏准确率近乎完美但与最终答案正确性无关；模型在结构化置信度格式下坍缩为两个值且错误率无法区分；探针跨跳的持久性不能区分正确与错误结果，推翻了预注册假设。branch-and-pick在所有模型中净收益为正且仅在Llama-3.1-8B上无破坏（4救0破），而reprompt和replace-prior在救回错误轨迹的同时几乎以相同速率破坏正确轨迹。

Conclusion: 探针监测是言语化置信度的必要补充，但没有任何单一干预方法全面占优。可部署的解决方案是模型感知、错误类型感知的混合路由策略。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [255] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NL2SHACL-Bench，一个用于评估自然语言到SHACL翻译任务的基准测试套件，并通过评估四个SOTA大语言模型揭示了当前模型在生成语法有效但语义不完全等价SHACL约束方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL是RDF知识图谱验证的核心技术，但编写SHACL形状需要专业技术知识，领域专家难以掌握。将自然语言需求转换为SHACL（NL2SHACL）可以降低这一门槛，但此前缺乏专门的基准测试，且评估生成形状需要超越字符串比较的语义等价性方法，因为语义等价的形状可能在序列化和结构上存在差异。

Method: 作者构建了NL2SHACL-Bench基准套件，用于自然语言到SHACL翻译的评测。基于该基准，对四个SOTA大语言模型（LLMs）进行了系统评估，考察其生成语法有效且语义等价的SHACL约束的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前大语言模型能够高度熟练地生成语法有效的SHACL代码，但在处理复杂的逻辑和结构模式时，仍难以生成语义等价的约束。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench为衡量NL2SHACL技术进展提供了有意义的基础，同时也指出了当前LLM在该任务上的主要瓶颈在于语义等价性而非语法正确性，为其后续改进指明了方向。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [256] [Dynamic Coalition Formation and Communication Pricing in Skill-Based Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07532)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多智能体LLM系统中的智能体选择与通信结构建模为带任务条件净效用的合作博弈，通过边际值激活规则和贪心路由器来优化智能体激活与通信边的选择，在合成实验中达到99.5%的暴力搜索最优效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体AI系统常将多个具有异构技能的LLM智能体组合在一起，但现有架构要么提前固定通信结构，要么允许全广播通信，两者在token成本、延迟、冗余和错误传播上均随活跃智能体数量和通信链路增加而急剧恶化，缺乏高效的自适应智能体选择和通信优化机制。

Method: 将智能体选择和通信建模为合作博弈，提出净效用U(C|x)=V(C|x)-Σ_{i∈C}c_i；设计边际值激活规则和贪心路由器；扩展模型以优化带每边成本的通信边；使用估计的Shapley值在执行前后预测值得联系哪些智能体；从理论上将问题连接至子模最大化，证明曲率修正界和紧的1/2近似界（双贪心），并证明Shapley-子模三明治界。

Result: 合成实验中，贪心路由达到暴力搜索最优效用的99.5%，平均仅激活8个智能体中的1.96个；全广播仅达38.8%。算法对激活成本和冗余权重鲁棒，但在强子模性违反或噪声价值估计下性能降至66%。

Conclusion: 该框架与Shapley定价、享乐联盟形成和通信图剪枝等方法有本质区别，作者建议在真实多智能体LLM基准上进一步验证。

Abstract: Modern agentic AI systems combine multiple large language model agents with heterogeneous skills, yet most architectures either fix communication in advance or allow full broadcast. Both can be inefficient because token cost, latency, redundancy, and error propagation increase with the number of active agents and communication links. We model agent selection and communication as a cooperative game with task-conditioned net utility $U(C\mid x)=V(C\mid x)-\sum_{i\in C}c_i$, separating coalition-level costs from agent activation costs. We propose a marginal-value activation rule and greedy router, extend the model to optimize communication edges with per-edge costs, and use estimated Shapley values to predict which agents are worth contacting before and during execution. We connect the problem to submodular maximization and prove two limited guarantees: a curvature-refined bound for a monotone, cardinality-constrained special case, and a tight $1/2$-approximation, with a correction for signed objectives, for an unconstrained non-monotone case via double greedy. Neither guarantee applies directly to the main router, which remains a heuristic. We also prove a Shapley-submodularity sandwich bound linking the error of marginal-value routing to a per-agent diminishing-returns quantity. In synthetic experiments, greedy routing achieves $99.5%$ of brute-force-optimal utility while activating $1.96$ of $8$ agents on average, compared with $38.8%$ for full broadcast. Performance is robust to activation cost and redundancy weight but falls to $66%$ under strong violations of submodularity or noisy value estimates. We distinguish the framework from Shapley pricing, hedonic coalition formation, and communication-graph pruning, and propose evaluation on real multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [257] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSpace框架，基于蜕变测试原理自动评估具身智能体的空间认知能力。通过将逻辑规则和物理定律编码为Prolog可执行规则，从真实执行轨迹中生成测试用例，并在三个具身场景中检测出SOTA多模态大语言模型智能体的90,422个空间认知错误，其空间认知评分（0.44-0.52）显著低于人类基准（0.96）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估范式存在两大缺陷：人工标注VQA对成本高且标注质量波动大；高层任务完成指标（如导航/操作成功）可能掩盖关键漏洞，允许智能体通过次优手段或违反安全规范完成任务。由于空间认知是具身任务执行的基础，亟需一种能评估智能体执行过程中空间认知鲁棒性的自动化方法。

Method: 受软件工程中蜕变测试原理启发，MetaSpace利用真实执行轨迹中的时空多模态状态，基于逻辑规则和物理定律预设的蜕变关系（MRs）自动生成测试用例，并将MRs编码为Prolog可执行规则；违反这些关系即视为空间认知失效。同时引入空间认知（SC）评分量化性能。

Result: 在三个具身场景中的实证评估表明，MetaSpace成功检测出SOTA MLLM驱动智能体的90,422个空间认知错误；所有SOTA智能体平均SC评分在0.44至0.52之间，显著低于人类基准0.96。

Conclusion: MetaSpace为具身智能体空间认知评估提供了一种自动化、可扩展的框架，揭示了当前SOTA多模态大语言模型智能体在空间认知方面与人类存在巨大差距，证明了该评估方法的有效性和必要性。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution.
  Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>


### [258] [When LLM Agents Negotiate: Private Information and Dynamic Bargaining in Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07538)
*Chen Liang,Fasheng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对九种主流LLM（OpenAI、Google、Alibaba）作为自主采购谈判代理的性能进行了系统审计，在9840次LLM间谈判中，以完美贝叶斯均衡为基准，从折现效率、分配轮廓和运行可靠性三个维度评估了AI代理的能力、供应商身份及提示词作为战略杠杆对剩余创造与分割的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理从决策支持转向自主采购，企业需要知道委托谈判代理能否创造价值、能否可预测地分割剩余，以及能否避免亏损合同。这些核心问题缺乏基于均衡基准的系统性实证证据。

Method: 采用经典的供应链讨价还价问题框架（买方持有私有需求信息、与不知情卖方谈判数量-支付契约），以已验证的完美贝叶斯均衡为基准，执行9840次LLM对LLM谈判实验，覆盖OpenAI、Google、Alibaba的九种模型，分析达成率、轮次、剩余捕获、分配比例及可靠性指标。

Result: 三类关键发现：（1）能力决定价值创造：谈判达成率98.9%，捕获未折现first-best剩余的95.4%，但平均2.98轮 vs 基准1.25轮，延迟侵蚀21-34%剩余；基础模型在19.2%案例中接受个体非理性合同，而中高端模型仅0.0-0.6%。（2）剩余捕获具有关系性：供应商身份比能力排名更能预测剩余分配，自博弈下OpenAI买方份额40%、Google 50%、Qwen 70%，且该排序在受限通信和无折现下依然稳健；Qwen旗舰是跨家族最弱的卖方。（3）提示词是战略杠杆：委托将委托人的经济耐心与代理的提示战略耐心分离，这一免费部署选择解释90%的分配方差。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于均衡参照的AI代理三方审计框架（折现效率、分配轮廓、运行可靠性），揭示能力阈值决定自动化利润验证的必要性，供应商选择是一阶分配决策，提示词成为最强剩余分配杠杆。这为自主采购中AI谈判代理的部署、守门与治理提供了系统性基准。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from decision support to autonomous procurement, firms need to know whether delegated negotiators create value, divide it predictably, and avoid money-losing contracts. We study this in a canonical supply chain bargaining problem: a buyer with private demand information negotiates a quantity-payment contract with an uninformed seller. We benchmark nine LLMs from OpenAI, Google, and Alibaba against a validated Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium across 9,840 LLM-to-LLM negotiations. First, capability governs value creation. Agents agree in 98.9% of negotiations and capture 95.4% of first-best surplus undiscounted, but average 2.98 rounds against the benchmark's 1.25, and this delay erodes 21-34% of surplus. Capability also governs reliability: baseline models accept individually irrational contracts in 19.2% of cases, versus 0.0-0.6% at mid-tier and flagship, making automated profit verification the binding guardrail below that threshold. Second, surplus capture is relational. Provider identity predicts who captures surplus better than capability rank: self-play buyer shares average 40% for OpenAI, 50% for Google, and 70% for Alibaba's Qwen, an ordering that survives restricted communication and no discounting. Reversing which provider sells moves the division by 7-18 percentage points, and the capable Qwen flagship is the weakest cross-family seller: vendor choice is a first-order distributional decision. Third, the prompt is a strategic lever. Delegation separates the principal's economic patience from the agent's prompted strategic patience, a free deployment choice that is the single strongest driver of surplus division (90% of explained variance). Together these establish an equilibrium-referenced audit of AI agents along three dimensions: discounted efficiency, distributional profile, and operational reliability.

</details>


### [259] [TREAT: Evaluating Access to Formal Knowledge across Equivalent Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07540)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TREAT基准，用于评估大语言模型能否从等价变换后的定理公式中恢复标准定理身份。该基准包含737个定理和29,480条变换数据，最佳模型准确率仅为60.73%，显示模型在表示变化下的定理知识具有脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统通常在灵活的输入表示与下游工具使用的形式化对象之间工作，关键挑战是识别陌生表述是否对应已知的形式化对象。现有工作忽略了公式级等价变换（而非文本释义）对定理识别的挑战，缺乏一个受控的标准基准来系统评估这一能力。

Method: TREAT通过从抓取的定理页面筛选可用的数学表达式，提取规范定理条件，并生成等价形式变体（包括残差方程、证据陈述、优化恒等式、集合关系、算子形式和证明中间特征），记录假设和逆映射。最终构建了737个定理身份和29,480个变换行的语料库。

Result: 在测试面板中，最佳模型仅能在60.73%的情况下检索到正确的定理身份。其他系统表现出不同的失败模式，包括弃权、错误检测和格式异常输出，表明等价表示变化下定理知识存在脆弱性。

Conclusion: TREAT提供了一个受控测试平台，用于评估表示鲁棒的形式化知识访问，其方法论可推广到需要稳定目标对象、明确等价关系、验证程序和可审计评分的更广泛领域。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly operate between flexible input representations and formal objects used by downstream tools. A key challenge is recognizing when an unfamiliar formulation denotes a known formal object. We study this challenge through theorem recognition: given an equivalence-preserving transformation of a theorem condition, a model must recover the theorem identity associated with the standard statement. We introduce TREAT, a benchmark for evaluating whether large language models can recover known theorem identities from equivalence-preserving formula-level transformations. Rather than paraphrasing theorem text, TREAT changes the mathematical form of theorem conditions themselves, expressing known results through residual equations, witness statements, optimization identities, set relations, operator forms, and proof-intermediate characterizations. Starting from scraped theorem pages, we filter for entries with usable mathematical expression forms, extract canonical theorem conditions, and generate transformed variants with recorded assumptions and inverse mappings. The final corpus contains 737 theorem identities and 29,480 transformed rows. On a test panel, the best model retrieves the correct theorem identity in only 60.73% of cases. Other systems reveal different failure modes, including abstention, wrong detection, and malformed outputs. These suggest that theorem knowledge can be fragile under equivalent changes in representation. TREAT therefore provides a controlled testbed for evaluating representation-robust access to formal knowledge, with broader relevance to domains that require stable target objects, explicit equivalence relations, validation procedures, and auditable scoring.

</details>


### [260] [An AI Scientist that Doesn't Drift: Taste, Structure, and Falsifiable Findings in a Quadruped Navigation Research Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07542)
*Yiwen Zhang,Eloise Zeng,Jaeha Lee,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于四足机器人导航策略泛化研究的AI科学家系统，通过"不可变实验卡片+专业子代理+偏好预言机(kkanbu)"三组件构建自主研究循环。对照实验表明，该系统能使循环避免指标局部优化漂移，保持假设检验的诚实性，而kkanbu预言机主要影响研究方向而非得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的自主研究循环虽可规模运行机器学习实验，但往往倾向于对优化指标的局部改进，而非检验驱动实验的假设，导致研究失焦和假设被事后篡改。

Method: 基于Karpathy的autoresearch范式，新增三个组件：(1) 不可变实验卡片，以固定模式配对每次迭代的预测与结果，防止被证伪的假设被事后改写；(2) 承担机械性角色的专业子代理；(3) kkanbu偏好预言机，以类型化知识图谱持有用户研究品味，是唯一被允许做主观判断的组件。为了隔离预言机效果，在11条研究流上运行完全相同的循环，一组带kkanbu，一组不带。

Result: 两臂均未漂移：各自证伪了约四分之三的自身假设，最佳训练策略来自无预言机的一组。kkanbu改变的是研究方向而非得分：它单独探索了测试时适应(test-time adaptation)，在其领先的研究流中主导了获胜设计，并将经验跨研究流传递，而另一组反复重新推导这些经验。

Conclusion: 脚手架(实验卡片+子代理)保证循环的诚实性，确保假设检验不被漂移；而kkanbu预言机决定研究探索的方向。两者分工明确：系统结构负责防作弊，预言机负责引导方向。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops driven by large language models can run machine-learning experiments at scale but tend to drift toward local refinements of whichever metric they optimise rather than testing the hypotheses that motivate the experiments. We address this structurally and present an AI Scientist for studying generalisation in quadruped robot navigation policies in simulation. Building on the autoresearch paradigm of Karpathy, our loop adds three components: an immutable experiment card that pairs each iteration's prediction with its outcome under a fixed schema, so a falsified hypothesis cannot be retconned; specialised subagents restricted to mechanical roles; and kkanbu, a preference oracle that holds the user's research taste as a typed knowledge graph and is the only component permitted to make subjective judgements. To isolate the oracle we run the identical loop twice across eleven research streams, with and without kkanbu. Neither arm drifts: both falsify roughly three quarters of their own hypotheses, and the best trained policy comes from the oracle-less arm. What the oracle changes is direction, not score: it alone explores test-time adaptation, it authored the winning designs where its arm led, and it carried lessons across streams that the other arm repeatedly re-derived. The scaffold keeps the loop honest; kkanbu decides where it looks.

</details>


### [261] [The Field Knows: Cross-Dimensional Geometry from Navigation to Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07566)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续度量场框架，通过单一因果对比损失训练，将场景编码为对称矩阵基的系数，组装成李代数元素并指数化为黎曼或洛伦兹度量。该框架在不同维度下能发现从机器人导航的避障测地线到黑洞事件视界的完整几何结构谱系，零样本泛化实验证明其捕获可迁移的几何结构而非记忆特定配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个统一框架能否通过单一因果对比损失，在不同维度和不同物理/机器人场景中自发产生多样化的几何结构（如黎曼度量下的避障路径和洛伦兹度量下的黑洞事件视界），从而验证"几何即物理"这一核心理念。

Method: 构建一个连续度量场框架：将场景编码为固定对称矩阵基的系数，组装成李代数元素，再通过矩阵指数映射生成黎曼或洛伦兹度量。整个框架仅由一个因果对比损失函数驱动训练，无需针对不同任务定制损失或架构。

Result: 该框架在不同维度上自发产生了完整谱系的几何结构：在机器人导航中生成避障测地线（涵盖平面与机械臂构型空间），在洛伦兹时空中生成具有正确洛伦兹符号的黑洞事件视界。广泛的零样本泛化研究证明其捕获的是可迁移的几何结构而非记忆具体配置。

Conclusion: 同一损失、同一架构、同一训练协议在不同维度下产生了完整的几何现象谱系，表明该度量场框架真正"理解"了几何结构，且几何结构本身蕴含了物理规律，为几何与物理的统一建模提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous metric field framework trained by a single causal contrastive loss. The framework encodes a scene into coefficients of a fixed symmetric matrix basis, assembles them into a Lie algebra element, and exponentiates the result to a Riemannian or Lorentzian metric. Across dimensions, this field discovers the full spectrum of geometric structures: from obstacle-avoiding geodesics in robot navigation across planar and manipulator configuration spaces, to event horizons of black holes in Lorentzian spacetime. Extensive zero-shot generalization studies demonstrate that the field captures transferable geometric structure rather than memorizing specific configurations. In the black hole setting, the causal loss spontaneously evolves genuine black-hole-like structures with the correct Lorentzian signature. The same loss, the same architecture, and the same training protocol produce the full range of geometric phenomena across dimensions. The field knows geometry, and geometry knows physics.

</details>


### [262] [TeXFix-Bench: An Empirically Grounded Multi-Format Benchmark for LLM-Based Document Source Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07617)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TeXFix-Bench，一个基于经验性挖掘故障分类法的多格式文档修复基准。通过Grounded-Theory研究从真实崩溃故障中归纳出18类故障分类法，并实现为DocMut工具（48个AST感知操作符，覆盖LaTeX/Typst/Markdown三种格式）。构建10,437个修复实例，评估7个LLM共48,651次尝试，发现DocMut故障比模式化突变难修复5.6-9.2个百分点；Typst比LaTeX和Markdown更难修复；编译成功率会高估修复质量（13.6-18.5%的编译成功修复实质性改变了文档内容）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文档修复评估使用ad-hoc编辑注入故障，缺乏经验性的故障模型，导致基准测试不能真实反映LLM修复能力。作者旨在构建一个基于真实崩溃故障、具有实证基础的文档修复基准，以更准确地评估和指导LLM在科学写作技术文档修复方面的表现。

Method: (1) 通过Grounded-Theory研究从TeX Stack Exchange、GitHub提交和包文档中挖掘168个已验证的硬崩溃LaTeX故障，采用双编码（κ=0.34）归纳出18类故障分类法；(2) 将分类法实例化为DocMut，包含48个AST感知的故障注入操作符，覆盖三种格式；(3) 构建10,437个修复实例（来自743个开源种子）；(4) 在固定零样本协议下评估7个LLM，采用provider-pinned路由，收集48,651次尝试；(5) 使用恢复oracle（28,129个编译成功的修复）评估修复是否实质性改变文档文本。

Result: DocMut故障比模式化突变难修复5.6-9.2个百分点；88个人类真实崩溃案例的修复成功率为67.0%；Typst显著比LaTeX和Markdown更难修复；固定引擎门控下意图治疗编译成功率跨度达27.5个百分点（56.7-84.2%）；13.6-18.5%的编译成功修复实质上改变了文档文本；完全6,613实例×7模型的平衡矩阵确认了所有排名；编译成功率最高的模型并不是恢复效果最好的（恢复排名与编译排名存在分歧）。

Conclusion: 编译成功本身高估了修复质量，文档修复评估不应仅看编译是否通过，还需检查修复是否保留原始文档内容。Typst的更高难度暗示其修复工具和模型能力仍需提升。作者发布了分类法、DocMut和全部实验工件以促进该领域进一步研究。

Abstract: Scientific and technical writing depends on markup sources that must compile: LaTeX, Typst, and Markdown pipelines fail on missing delimiters, mismatched environments, broken imports, or package conflicts. Existing document-repair evaluations inject faults with ad-hoc edits that lack an empirical fault model. We present TeXFix-Bench, a multi-format benchmark for LLM-based full-source document repair grounded in a mined fault taxonomy. A Grounded-Theory study of localized hard-crash LaTeX faults from TeX Stack Exchange, GitHub commits, and package documentation (168 verified faults, dual open coding at $κ$=0.34) yields an 18-category taxonomy instantiated as DocMut: 48 AST-aware operators across three formats.
