Cheap surrogate evaluators can reduce this cost, yet fixed surrogates are vulnerable to search-induced distribution shift and are difficult to fit reliably from sparse, search-biased labels. We introduce Janus, a framework that uses LLMs to co-evolve target programs and executable proxy evaluators. To address label scarcity, Janus leverages domain knowledge encoded in LLMs to generate task-specific evaluator programs and calibrates them using real outcomes. To mitigate distribution shift, Janus evolves evaluators alongside target programs, selects them using a promotion-aligned objective, and maintains region-conditioned portfolios with online credit updates. Because proxy predictions remain fallible, Janus uses them only to prioritize candidates and requires real validation before candidates can enter the target-program population or update the incumbent. Across five scientific and engineering design tasks, Janus achieves a larger area under the best-so-far improvement curve over the real-evaluation budget and higher final performance than a matched baseline that evolves only target programs. On average, Janus reaches 99/% of the baseline's final improvement with 59.1/% fewer real evaluations. Evolved proxy evaluators also rank promising candidates more accurately than their seed versions. Together, these results extend evaluator-guided LLM discovery from tasks with cheap, scalable feedback to scientific domains where trustworthy evaluation is scarce and expensive.

</details>


### [327] [A Minimal $κ$--$τ$ Logic for Risk-Sensitive Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08192)
*Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于κ（假设间认知交互）和τ（规范性承诺阈值）两个原语的κ-τ逻辑框架，用于在风险敏感领域中显式表征"承诺时机"这一受治理的决策。框架包含合成与分析两种互补模式，并作为神经符号架构的符号治理层，其中认知参数由神经组件估计、规范参数由人类治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准溯因推理虽然可以保留多个候选解释，但未能将显式的跨假设合成交互与内部"竞争敏感的承诺判断"相结合。在风险敏感领域（过早承诺带来不对称的下行成本），承诺的时机本身就是一个应被推理机制正式表达和治理的决策，而非仅由推理自然促成。

Method: 提出一个极简的κ-τ逻辑框架，包含两个原语：(1) κ表征假设之间的认知交互（共存、增强、抑制、形成涌现复合解释）；(2) τ作为规范性承诺阈值，通过治理约束而非纯推理来控制向已定结论的坍缩。框架以两种互补模式发展——合成模式（自下而上将原子假设组合为涌现解释）和分析模式（将复杂观测事态分解为潜因因果簇，在簇和因子两个层级上治理承诺）。

Result: 该框架为"高度可能"与"值得承诺"在操作上相区别的领域提供了形式化机制，并作为神经符号架构的符号治理层：认知参数可由神经组件（语义嵌入、生成模型）自然估计，规范参数则保留在显式的人类治理之下，实现透明可审计的高风险溯因推理。

Conclusion: κ-τ逻辑为风险敏感领域中承诺时机的治理提供了形式化基础，通过区分认知交互与规范承诺阈值，将溯因推理中"何时承诺"从隐含假设提升为显式受治的决策，并使规范参数保持在人类可审计的治理范围内。

Abstract: Standard approaches to abductive reasoning can retain multiple candidate explanations, but they do not generally combine explicit compositional cross-hypothesis interaction with an internal, rival-sensitive commitment judgment. This paper argues that in risk-sensitive domains -- where premature commitment carries asymmetric downside costs -- the timing of commitment is itself a governed decision that the inferential apparatus should formally represent. We present a minimal $κ$--$τ$ logical framework built on two primitives: epistemic interaction among hypotheses ($κ$) and a normative commitment threshold ($τ$). Hypotheses may coexist, reinforce or inhibit one another, and form emergent composite explanations, while collapse into committed conclusions is regulated by governance constraints rather than forced by inference alone. The logic is developed in two complementary modes sharing the interaction relation and the governance apparatus: a synthetic mode, in which atomic hypotheses are composed upward into emergent explanations, and an analytic mode, in which complex observed states of affairs are decomposed into causal clusters of latent factors, with commitment governed at both the cluster and the factor level. The framework provides formal machinery for domains in which the distinction between highly likely and commit-worthy is operationally consequential. The $κ$--$τ$ logic is positioned as the symbolic governance layer of a neurosymbolic architecture: its epistemic parameters are naturally estimated by neural components -- semantic embeddings and generative models, as demonstrated in existing computational realizations -- while its normative parameters remain under explicit human governance, yielding transparent and auditable abductive reasoning for deployment in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [328] [Persuasive and Compliant Tendencies Predict Group Decision-Making in Humans and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08199)
*Wenwen He,Wenke Huang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DecisionQE框架，通过问卷方式量化LLM的说服倾向与顺从倾向，并以狼人杀游戏为交互测试平台，研究发现：较强的说服倾向并不能显著改善群体结果，而顺从倾向模型在合作中表现出更稳定的优势；顺从具有双重效应（在诚实角色中促进合作、在对抗角色中增强掩饰）。LLM群体交互可作为社会学观察的透镜，且行为倾向应纳入安全评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与人与机器的群体决策，其影响力究竟源于说服性表达还是顺从性迎合尚不明确。缺乏系统化的工具来衡量模型的劝说与顺从倾向，也缺少对这两种倾向如何影响群体结果与社交影响力的理解。

Method: 引入DecisionQE，一个基于问卷的框架，用于在多种决策场景下测量每个模型的说服倾向和顺从倾向；以狼人杀游戏作为交互测试平台，在不对称信息条件下研究这些行为倾向对社交影响力和群体结果的作用。

Result: 实验表明：更强的说服倾向并未显著改善群体结果，而顺从倾向模型在合作中展现更稳定的优势；同时揭示顺从的双重效应——在诚实角色中促进合作，在对抗角色中提高掩饰能力。

Conclusion: LLM的群体交互不仅反映任务结果，还揭示可测量的内在行为倾向模式。LLM可作为语言介导交互的社会学观察透镜，同时安全评估中需要纳入行为倾向这一维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in group decision-making with other LLMs and humans. Yet it remains unclear whether their influence is driven by persuasion-oriented expression or compliance-oriented accommodation. We introduce DecisionQE, a questionnaire-based framework for measuring each model's persuasive and compliant tendencies across multiple decision scenarios, and use the Werewolf game as an interactive testbed to study their effects on social influence and group outcomes under asymmetric information. Across experiments, stronger persuasive tendency does not significantly improve group outcomes, whereas compliant-oriented models show more stable advantages in cooperation. We further reveal a dual effect of compliance: it supports cooperation in honest roles but improves concealment in adversarial roles. These findings suggest that LLM group interactions reveal not only task outcomes, but also measurable patterns of intrinsic behavioral tendency. LLMs can therefore serve as a lens for sociological observation of language-mediated interaction, while highlighting the need to incorporate behavioral tendencies into safety evaluation of LLM systems.

</details>


### [329] [Illusion of Alignment: Detecting Hidden Disagreement in Collaborative Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08210)
*Kaiming Liu,Fuwen Luo,Ziyue Wang,Jinrui Ju,Yuxuan Liu,Xuanyu Lei,Yunghwei Lai,Peng Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐错觉(IoA)"概念——协作对话中参与者表面达成一致但实则存在隐藏分歧。作者构建了IoA-Suite数据集与评测协议检测隐藏分歧，训练了专门的IoA-Prober-8B模型，在18场真实会议中平均每场发现2.89个未被言明的分歧，并将该模型集成到多智能体协作中提升了下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作对话中普遍存在"对齐错觉"现象：参与者各怀不同目标、假设或执行计划却表面达成一致。这构成一个悖论——若参与者意识到分歧，分歧则已显式；若未意识到，则无法言说，导致分歧对参与者和观察者都不可见。因此需要一种方法使隐藏分歧变得可检测。

Method: 通过生成诊断性多选题来检测IoA——不同参与者在这些题目上的不同答案可直接提供隐藏分歧的行为证据。据此构建IoA-Suite数据集（覆盖5种任务类型、6个领域），并在其上训练IoA-Prober-8B模型。

Result: 最优基线模型在IoA-Suite上仅达49.5% F1，瓶颈在于对话未呈现的私有上下文。IoA-Prober-8B达到51.8% F1。在18场真实会议中，该模型平均每场识别出2.89个参与者确认未曾言明的隐藏分歧，成功迁移至人类实时对话场景。在多智能体协作中，将IoA-Prober-8B与LLM代理结合可提升BigCodeBench-Hard和HiddenBench上的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: IoA是可检测且可量化的现象，通过诊断性问题生成的方法能够在人类协作与多智能体协作场景中有效揭示隐藏分歧，且模型化的IoA检测不仅能增强对协作过程的理解，还能切实提升多智能体系统的下游任务性能。

Abstract: Collaborative dialogue can end with apparent agreement while participants still differ on goals, assumptions, or execution plans, creating an \textbf{illusion of alignment (IoA)}. A real-user study across 18 meetings confirms that IoA arises routinely in human collaboration. Yet IoA poses a paradox: if participants were aware of such disagreements, they would already be explicit; if not, they cannot articulate them when asked, leaving IoA invisible to both participants and observers. In this work, we make IoA detectable by generating diagnostic multiple-choice questions whose divergent answers across participants provide direct behavioral evidence of hidden disagreement. We construct \textbf{IoA-Suite}, a dataset and evaluation protocol for detecting hidden disagreement, spanning five task types and six domains. We find that even the best model attains only 49.5\% F1, with the bottleneck traced to private context that the dialogue does not surface. We then train \textbf{IoA-Prober-8B} based on IoA-Suite, reaching 51.8\% F1 on IoA-Suite. Across the aforementioned 18 real meetings, it surfaces 2.89 hidden disagreements per meeting that participants confirm they had not voiced, transferring to live human dialogue. Further, in multi-agent collaboration, pairing IoA-Prober-8B with LLM agents improves downstream task performance on BigCodeBench-Hard and HiddenBench.

</details>


### [330] [Harmful Content Is Not Enough: Continuation Framing Moderates In-Context Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08212)
*Peiyang Liu,Xi Wang,Ziqiang Cui,Di Liang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文学习（ICL）中的"涌现性错误对齐"（EM）现象，发现有害内容并不必然导致广泛错误对齐，而"延续助手行为"的呈现框架才是关键的调节因素。在相同有害答案下，演示框架相较于证据、助手历史或工具输出框架，会使易感Gemini模型的广泛EM提升30-32个百分点，且该效应具有模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL诱发EM的研究将有害文本暴露与"邀请继续扮演助手"二者混为一谈，缺乏对呈现方式如何影响EM的系统性剖析。作者希望厘清：是"接触有害内容"本身导致EM，还是"以助手演示方式呈现"的框架在起作用。

Method: 在保持有害答案内容完全一致的前提下，操纵其呈现方式（演示、证据、助手历史、工具输出）进行对照实验。采用十个独立采样上下文、领域排除、语义聚类、未见问题及四种提示模板进行稳健性检验，并加入"角色×延续性"双因素设计。同时引入格式与长度匹配的控制组，结合盲化人工审核和模型裁判交叉验证。

Result: 演示框架使易感Gemini模型的广泛EM提高30-32个百分点；该效应在排除领域、语义聚类、未见问题及不同模板下均稳健。格式和长度控制显示有害内容是必要但非充分条件。模型间存在显著差异：Gemini同时受助手和工具历史影响，而Grok抗拒工具框架的延续；多个前沿和开源模型无显著差异。盲化人工审核证实所有主要对比，且发现模型裁判低估了活跃条件失败。

Conclusion: 延续呈现框架是ICL-EM的强有力且具有模型依赖性的调节因素，EM并非有害上下文的普遍必然结果。研究厘清了"内容"与"框架"的分离，对理解和对齐LLM的安全性评估具有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) can induce emergent misalignment (EM), where narrow misaligned examples alter answers to unrelated questions. Existing prompts, however, conflate harmful-text exposure with an invitation to continue assistant behavior. We hold harmful answers fixed while varying their delivery as demonstrations, evidence, assistant history, or tool output. Across ten independently sampled contexts, demonstration framing raises broad EM by $30$--$32$ percentage points on a susceptible Gemini model; the gap survives domain exclusion, semantic clustering, unseen questions, and four prompt templates. Format and length-matched controls show that harmful content is necessary but insufficient. A role times continuation factorial further reveals model-dependent provenance effects: Gemini follows both assistant and tool histories, whereas Grok largely resists tool-framed continuation. Several other frontier and open-weight models show no gap. Blinded human audits confirm every main contrast and show that the model judge underestimates active-condition failures. Thus continuation framing is a strong, model-dependent moderator of ICL-EM, not a universal consequence of harmful context.

</details>


### [331] [Metanormative Theory for RL-Based Moral Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08220)
*Aleks Knoks,Marija Slavkovik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器伦理与价值对齐领域的最新趋势——利用强化学习（RL）设计道德和价值对齐的人工智能体，并从元规范性理论（metanormative theory）的视角审视RL架构，从而为判断RL代理行为是否可被视为道德行为提供更清晰的标准，以及为评估和比较不同RL方法提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器伦理与价值对齐领域近期倾向于使用强化学习设计道德智能体，却忽视了本应发挥核心作用的哲学文献。作者希望将元规范性理论中的思想重新引入该领域，弥补哲学视角的缺失，并为RL代理的道德性判断提供理论依据。

Method: 论文采取双路径方法：（1）从近期元规范性理论研究中提取可用于设计道德与价值对齐智能体的关键思想；（2）以这些思想为分析透镜，审视强化学习架构本身，建立RL代理行为能否被归类为道德行为的判别标准。

Result: 本文提出了一套更清晰的判据，用于判定RL代理的行为何时可被归类为道德行为，并以此为基础，为评估和比较不同的基于RL的机器伦理与价值对齐方法提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 通过将元规范性理论思想与RL架构相结合，本文实现了哲学理论与工程实践的重新连接，为机器伦理和价值对齐领域提供了更坚实的理论基础和更明确的评估标准。

Abstract: The overlapping disciplines of machine ethics and value alignment are concerned with designing artificial agents that are aligned with human values and that act in ethically acceptable ways. A recent trend in these disciplines is the use of reinforcement learning (RL) to design such agents, sidelining the philosophical literature that used to play a more central role. Against this backdrop, this paper pursues two goals. The first is to draw out ideas from recent work in metanormative theory that can be useful for designing artificial moral and value-aligned agents. The second is to examine the RL architecture through the lens of these ideas. This will give us clearer criteria for when an RL agent's behavior can be classified as moral, as well as a basis for evaluating and comparing different RL-based approaches to machine ethics and value alignment.

</details>


### [332] [LatticeMind: A Conflict-Aware Memory Primitive for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08236)
*Heng Zhou,Lian Zhang,Yutao Fan,Tiancheng He,Siki Chen,Hejia Geng,Philip Torr,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LatticeMind，一种冲突感知的结构化记忆系统，在写入时处理多智能体LLM系统中的矛盾信息。它通过显式条目状态、廉价的符号冲突检查，以及仅对未解决的语义案例调用LLM调解。在去除来源名的ConflictBank评估中达到0.97准确率（基线为0.61），移除检查器或调解器各损失12-14分。在四个次要规划基准上，它在三个上优于朴素合并，但无法替代迭代搜索的深思熟虑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的失败往往不是因为缺少候选答案，而是缺乏一个持久的机制来决定当前应信任哪个不兼容的主张。现有方法（多数投票、辩论、基于评判者的选择）只输出结果，却不记录哪个主张胜出、哪个被质疑，或为何后续更新取代了它。

Method: LatticeMind是一种冲突感知的结构化记忆系统，在写入时处理矛盾：维护显式的条目状态，应用廉价的符号冲突检查，仅对未解决的语义案例调用LLM调解。

Result: 在去除来源名提示的label-blind ConflictBank评估中，LatticeMind达到0.97准确率，而最强聚合基线为0.61（p<10^-6的paired McNemar检验）。消融实验显示移除检查器或调解器会损失12-14分。在四个次要规划基准上，它在三个上优于朴素合并，但在奖励迭代搜索的任务上无法替代深思熟虑方法。

Conclusion: LatticeMind通过写入时的冲突感知记忆显著优于聚合基线，并展示了分层冲突处理（符号检查+LLM调解）的高效性，但在需要迭代搜索的规划任务上，它不应替代深思熟虑类方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often fail not for lack of candidate answers, but because they have no persistent mechanism for deciding which incompatible claim should currently be trusted. Majority vote, debate, and judge-based selection choose an output without recording which claim wins, which is contested, or why a later update supersedes it. We present \term{LatticeMind}, a conflict-aware structured memory that handles contradiction at write time. It maintains explicit item status, applies cheap symbolic conflict checks, and invokes LLM reconciliation only for unresolved semantic cases. On a label-blind ConflictBank evaluation that removes source-name hints, LatticeMind reaches 0.97 accuracy versus 0.61 for the strongest aggregation baseline, with the gap significant at $p<10^{-6}$ by paired McNemar test. Ablations show that removing the checker or the reconciler costs 12 to 14 points. On four secondary planning benchmarks the picture is mixed: LatticeMind beats naive merge on three of four, but does not replace deliberation methods on tasks rewarding iterative search.

</details>


### [333] [A Fair Objective for Human-Empowerment-Preserving AI: Desiderata, Design, and Likely Behavioral Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08240)
*Jobst Heitzig,Ram Potham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过显式增强人类权力并管理人机间权力平衡的方法来促进人机交互中的福祉与安全，设计了一个参数化、可分解的目标函数，表示不平等厌恶和风险厌恶的人类权力的长期总量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益强大，人机交互中的权力失衡可能对人类的福祉和安全构成威胁。现有AI对齐方法往往只关注如何让AI服从人类当前指令，而非主动赋予人类权力并维持健康的权力平衡。作者希望从第一性原理出发，探索一种原则性的方式来设计AI目标，使其明确地增强人类并管理权力平衡。

Method: 采用部分公理化的方法，基于理想性质（desiderata）推导出AI系统的目标函数。该函数参数化、可分解，表示不平等厌恶与风险厌恶的人类权力长期聚合。该函数能纳入人类有限理性和社会规范模型，并考虑多种可能的人类目标。作者证明特定期望性质如何强制特定的函数形式和约束参数范围，并在若干典型情境中展示软最大化该度量的后果，分析其可能隐含的工具性子目标。

Result: 证明了对不平等和风险的厌恶会强制特定函数形式并限制参数范围；在多种典型情境中展示了优化该目标函数的行为后果，并识别了其可能隐含的工具性子目标。

Conclusion: 通过公理化设计目标函数，可以为AI系统赋予明确的人类赋权与权力平衡意识，从而在原则上构建更安全、更有利于人类福祉的人机交互框架，同时也需警惕由此产生的工具性子目标可能带来的新问题。

Abstract: This paper explores the idea of promoting well-being and safety in human-AI interactions by forcing AI agents explicitly to empower humans and to manage the power balance between humans and AI agents in a desirable way. Using a principled, partially axiomatic approach based on desirable properties, we design a parametrizable and decomposable objective function for AI systems that represents an inequality- and risk-averse long-term aggregate of human power. It can take into account models of human bounded rationality and social norms, and crucially, considers a wide variety of possible human goals. We prove how certain desiderata enforce particular functional forms and restrict parameter ranges. We exemplify the consequences of softly maximizing this metric in several paradigmatic situations and describe what instrumental sub-goals it will likely imply.

</details>


### [334] [FemWear: A Specialized Wearable Foundation Model for Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08244)
*Yifan Wang,Chenzhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FemWear是一个专为女性健康任务设计的可穿戴基础模型，通过低秩残差适配器参数高效地复用预训练多模态可穿戴骨干网络（仅训练1.11%的编码器参数），在六个队列63项指标（含33项女性健康指标）上评估，改善了周期相位宏F1（+8.15%）及痛经、情绪、睡眠误差，但在严格的嵌套留一参与者审计中仅部分终点保持正向改进，未确立普遍性能优势或临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用可穿戴基础模型在大规模传感器流和人群中预训练，但并未针对女性健康任务设计。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建专门针对月经、症状、情绪、睡眠/恢复、自主神经、活动和妊娠相关结局的可穿戴基础模型。

Method: FemWear保留预训练多模态可穿戴骨干的patch投影和Transformer编码器，通过低秩残差适配器和因果任务家族头（训练239,236个参数，占21.54M编码器的1.11%）进行参数高效微调，学习一个共享的纵向表示以支持多类女性健康结局预测。评估采用固定参与者划分（三个种子）及严格的42参与者嵌套留一参与者审计。

Result: 固定分割下：周期相位宏F1提升8.15%，痛经、情绪症状、睡眠问题平均绝对误差分别降低9.32%、5.80%、9.43%。在严格审计中，24小时发作、72小时发作和痛经保持正向变化（+2.87%、+6.35%、+2.19%），但相位、情绪、睡眠中性或负向，且无终点在校正置信区间上严格为正。能力匹配实验中优于多层感知机但未超越共享GRU或多门控混合专家基线。训练仅校准将发作期望校准误差降低84.2-88.2%。

Conclusion: FemWear实现了针对女性健康研究的定向迁移和连贯的概率输出，但并未建立普遍的性能主导地位或临床有效性验证。研究结果强调了在严格审计设计下对基础模型声称进行严谨评估的重要性。

Abstract: General wearable foundation models are pretrained across broad sensor streams and populations, but are not designed around women's-health tasks. We introduce FemWear, a specialized wearable foundation model that parameter-efficiently repurposes a pretrained multimodal wearable backbone. FemWear retains the patch projection and Transformer encoder, training 239,236 parameters (1.11% of a 21.54M-parameter encoder) through low-rank residual adapters and causal task-family heads. It learns one shared longitudinal representation for menstrual, symptom, affective, sleep/recovery, autonomic, activity, and pregnancy-related outcomes. We evaluate six cohorts with 63 comparable primary metrics, including 33 from women's-health cohorts, while retaining the 32-task OpenMHC ability-retention benchmark. On a fixed participant split over three seeds, FemWear improved cycle-phase macro-F1 by 8.15% and reduced mean absolute error for cramps, mood symptoms, and sleep problems by 9.32%, 5.80%, and 9.43%, respectively. In a stricter 42-participant nested leave-one-participant-out audit, 24-hour onset, 72-hour onset, and cramps retained positive changes of 2.87%, 6.35%, and 2.19%; phase, mood, and sleep were neutral or negative, and no endpoint had a strictly positive corrected confidence interval. Capacity-matched experiments outperformed a latest-day multilayer perceptron but not shared-GRU or multi-gate mixture-of-experts baselines. Train-only calibration reduced onset expected calibration error by 84.2--88.2% with zero temporal-nesting violations. FemWear enables targeted transfer and coherent probability outputs for women's-health research, but does not establish universal performance dominance or clinical validity.

</details>


### [335] [SuperLocalMemory 4.0: The Governed Memory Operating System for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08253)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SuperLocalMemory 4.0，一个受治理的、本地优先的AI智能体记忆操作系统，融合多种检索机制、治理学习层、双时态召回、RBAC、GDPR导出与验证清除等功能。V4引入可靠性脊柱（写入路径：生成门控准入、策略注册表、可验证记忆事务、哈希校验清单），并报告2,200次确定性故障注入测试全部通过，治理写入开销p50为1.687ms、p99为2.728ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正成为共享基础设施，但持久记忆通常由分离的检索、治理和操作组件拼装而成，缺乏统一的、受治理的、本地优先的记忆操作系统。作者旨在提供一个整合且可验证的记忆运行时。

Method: 系统结合五种检索机制（稠密语义、BM25词法、时间、Hopfield联想、扩散激活）并通过倒数秩融合；包含治理学习与行为层、双时态召回、多范围（个人/共享/全局）记忆、RBAC、GDPR导出与验证清除、审计追踪、EU AI Act部署清单。V4引入可靠性写入路径：生成门控准入、策略注册表、可验证记忆事务（逐投影应用/验证/补偿/擦除所有者和哈希可校验完成清单）。提供CLI、MCP、HTTP守护进程、仪表盘、编辑器集成和框架适配器，支持完全本地、本地+设备端模型、提供商辅助模式。

Result: 在十一个故障注入与机制场景（各重复200次）中，2,200/2,200次确定性重复全部保持其范围内组件属性。受治理写入包络p50为3.522ms、p99为5.297ms，对比未治理基线（p50 1.835ms、p99 2.569ms），即进程内控制面开销p50为1.687ms、p99为2.728ms。作者强调这是范围内组件与机制测量，非端到端多进程或外部检索准确性基准。

Conclusion: SuperLocalMemory 4.0在统一受治理的本地优先记忆操作系统中实现了可靠写入路径与全面治理能力，并提供了透明的故障注入证据。论文整合了先前V3.3 Living Brain生命周期、隐私保护多智能体记忆与信息几何检索的工作。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming shared infrastructure, yet durable memory is commonly assembled from separate retrieval, governance, and operational components. We present SuperLocalMemory 4.0, a governed, local-first memory operating system for AI agents. The system combines dense semantic, BM25 lexical, temporal, Hopfield-associative, and spreading-activation retrieval through reciprocal-rank fusion; a governed learning and behaviour layer; bi-temporal recall; multi-scope personal, shared, and global memory; role-based access control; GDPR-oriented export and verified erasure; audit trails; and a deployment-context EU AI Act checklist.
  V4 introduces a reliability spine for its primary write path: generation-fenced admission, a policy registry, verifiable memory transactions with per-projection apply, verify, compensate, and erase owners, and hash-checkable completion manifests. The runtime is available through CLI, MCP, an HTTP daemon, a dashboard, editor integration, and framework adapters, and supports fully local, local-with-on-device-model, and provider-assisted modes.
  We evaluate eleven fault-injection and mechanism scenarios, each repeated 200 times. The released evidence bundle reports 2,200 of 2,200 deterministic repetitions upholding their scoped component properties. The governed write envelope measured 3.522 ms at p50 and 5.297 ms at p99, versus 1.835 ms and 2.569 ms for the ungoverned baseline, corresponding to in-process control-plane overheads of 1.687 ms at p50 and 2.728 ms at p99. These are scoped component and mechanism measurements, not an end-to-end multi-process or external retrieval-accuracy benchmark. The paper consolidates prior SuperLocalMemory work on privacy-preserving multi-agent memory, information-geometric retrieval, and the V3.3 Living Brain lifecycle.

</details>


### [336] [Your Prompt Is Not the Only Prompt: How Much Do LLMs Weight Structured-Output Schema Descriptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08254)
*Sin-Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过十个模型配置上的单字段分类任务（使用非ce标签），系统比较了分类标签定义放在系统提示、用户提示或JSON schema描述中的效果。结果发现schema位置并非总是最优，且schema描述并非惰性元数据——当提示与schema冲突时，schema指令可使准确率下降5-45个百分点，甚至覆盖提示指令。但引入必需的中间推理字段可将schema-only准确率提升15-24个百分点，且效果优于仅系统提示。总体结论是：系统提示仍是安全默认，但schema设计本身可能比指令位置是更强的杠杆，应视提示和schema为统一指令面并针对目标模型实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化输出（LLM填充预定义JSON schema）已成为数据标注和信息抽取的默认机制，但schema描述引入了第二条指令通道。现有实践对分类标签定义应置于系统提示、用户提示还是schema描述中缺乏系统性研究，且schema对模型行为的影响机制未被充分理解，因此需要实证比较不同指令放置方式的效果。

Method: 采用单字段分类任务，使用非ce标签，覆盖两家供应商的十个模型配置（包括GPT-4.1、GPT-5.4、Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6等）。实验包括三组对比：(1) 标签定义在系统提示、用户提示或schema描述中的性能比较；(2) 构造提示与schema冲突场景，测试schema指令的覆盖效应；(3) 在标签字段前加入必需的中间推理字段，评估对schema-only准确率的影响。

Result: (1) schema描述并非始终优于提示位置；GPT-4.1和GPT-5.4（无推理）的schema放置比系统提示低11-13个百分点。(2) 提示与schema冲突时，错误schema指令导致准确率下降5-45个百分点，Claude Haiku 4.5从52.5%跌至7%，GPT-5.5从100%跌至73%。(3) 添加必需中间推理字段将schema-only准确率提升15-24个百分点（在存在提升空间时），在所有测试案例中超过仅系统提示的性能，该效应在Claude Sonnet 4.6中等推理级别下依然成立。

Conclusion: schema对模型的影响是模型依赖性的。系统提示仍是定义的应选默认位置，但更重要的实践纪律是维护单一信息源、防止提示与schema漂移。schema设计本身可能是比指令位置更强的性能杠杆，实践者应将提示和schema视为统一的指令表面，并针对目标模型同时实证验证放置位置和字段设计。

Abstract: Structured output, where an LLM populates a predefined JSON schema, has become a default mechanism for data labeling and information extraction, but it also introduces a second instruction channel through schema descriptions. We tested whether classification-label definitions are better placed in the system prompt, user prompt, or schema description using a single-field classification task with nonce labels across ten model configurations from two vendors. Schema descriptions did not consistently outperform prompt-based placement; for GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.4 without reasoning, schema placement underperformed system prompts by 11-13 percentage points. Yet schemas are not inert metadata: when prompts and schemas conflicted, incorrect schema instructions caused accuracy drops of 5-45 points, with Claude Haiku 4.5 falling from 52.5% to 7%, indicating that schema instructions can override prompt instructions, and GPT-5.5 falling from 100% to 73%. Further, adding a required intermediate reasoning field before the label field improved schema-only accuracy by 15-24 points when headroom existed, exceeding system-prompt-only performance in every case tested. The effect held even for Claude Sonnet 4.6 at medium reasoning, where extended thinking alone did not produce a comparable gain. This suggests that schema design can affect how effectively models use information encoded in field descriptions. Overall, these results indicate that schema influence is model-dependent. In practice, the system prompt remains a safe default for definitions, but the bigger discipline is maintaining a single source of truth and preventing prompt/schema drift. More importantly, schema design itself may be a stronger lever than instruction placement. Practitioners should treat prompts and schemas as a unified instruction surface and empirically validate both placement and field design for their target model.

</details>


### [337] [OBLIVION: Workflow-Level Operational Skill Unlearning for Deployed Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08264)
*Zhengyang Shan,Xu Qian,Jiayun Xin,Kun Li,Yue Zhang,Minghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型代理部署中的"技能撤销"问题，提出OBLIVION基准与防御框架，通过跨表面相干擦除和冻结工作流补救，将技能复活攻击成功率从1.0降至0.114（锁定场景）和0.2（沙盒场景），同时保持正常功能不损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理正成为文件、内存、注册表和外部工具的操作界面，当技能从显式注册表中移除后，代理仍可从残留载体（如存档、转录、模式、内存条目）重建该技能。现有研究聚焦于参数级遗忘，但缺少对代理通过原始工具重新构建已撤销技能的防御方法。

Method: 提出OBLIVION框架：(1) 将每个情节建模为源到汇的工作流(source-to-sink workflow)；(2) 采用跨表面相干擦除(Cross-Surface Coherent Erasure)减少残留载体；(3) 在危险汇点附近使用冻结工作流补救(frozen workflow remediation)。

Result: 在锁定的88个攻击情节中，无防御基准的攻击成功率为1.0，OBLIVION将其降至0.114，影响加权暴露降至0.115，同时锁定效用保持1.0、良性阻断率为0。在独立的技能攻击派生沙盒中，攻击成功率从1.0降至0.2，影响加权暴露从1.0降至0.213，且所有效用控制均得以保留。

Conclusion: 结果支持超越显式技能条目检查的工作流级评估方法，表明在代理部署环境中，防御技能复活需要在工作流层面而非仅参数或注册表层面进行考量。

Abstract: Large language model agents are becoming operational interfaces to files, memories, registries, and external tools. This deployment shift creates a new skill revocation problem: after a skill is removed from an explicit registry, an agent may still reconstruct it from residual carriers such as archives, transcripts, schemas, or memory entries. We study this problem as operational skill unlearning, where the goal is not parameter-level forgetting, but preventing a deployed agent from rebuilding a revoked skill through primitive tools. We introduce OBLIVION, a controlled benchmark and defense harness for revoked-skill resurrection. OBLIVION models each episode as a source-to-sink workflow, applies Cross-Surface Coherent Erasure to reduce residual carriers, and uses frozen workflow remediation near dangerous sinks. On the locked 88 attack episodes, the no-defense arm reaches formal attack success rate 1.0. OBLIVION reduces the rate to 0.114 and impact-weighted exposure to 0.115 while keeping locked utility at 1.0 and benign block rate at 0. In a separate skill-attack-derived sandbox, OBLIVION reduces attack success from 1.0 to 0.2 and impact-weighted exposure from 1.0 to 0.213 while preserving all utility controls. These results support workflow-level evaluation beyond checking explicit skill entries.

</details>


### [338] [Exploring LLM Capabilities for Situational Understanding and COLREG compliance on real-world maritime navigation scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08281)
*Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Line K. H. Clemmensen,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨将大语言模型（LLM）应用于海事导航任务，构建了包含50个真实AIS导航场景的数据集，并评估多种LLM架构对COLREG规则和"良好船艺"概念的理解与推理能力。结果表明，未经微调的LLM（包括大型在线模型）在海事导航任务上表现困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在汽车领域展现出情境理解、推理和决策能力，作者希望探索将这类模型应用于海事导航领域，因为海事导航同时涉及成文规则（COLREG）和不成文的"良好船艺"最佳实践，这对LLM提出了兼具规则遵从和隐性知识理解的挑战。

Method: 作者从AIS数据中构建了一个包含50个多样化真实导航场景的数据集，为每个场景标注适用的COLREG规则、推荐行动及推理依据。随后评估了多种不同架构和规模的LLM，测试其对海事导航任务的理解和推理能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使对于较大的在线模型，在不进行微调的情况下，海事导航任务仍然难以解决。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的LLM在没有针对该领域进行专门微调时，尚不能可靠地完成海事导航任务，表明该领域需要进一步的研究或领域特定的微调。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown considerable capability for situational understanding, reasoning, and decision making in different domains, most notable in the automotive sector. Therefore, we explore current state-of-the-art LLMs as a tool for maritime navigation, which includes both codified rules in the Collision Regulations (COLREGs) and uncodified best practices summarized in the concept of ``Good Seamanship''. We construct a dataset consisting of 50 diverse, real-world navigation scenarios from AIS data, label scenarios with applicable COLREG rules, recommended actions, and the reasoning for the action. We explore a variety of different LLM architectures and sizes to determine their understanding of maritime navigation tasks as well as evaluate their reasoning capabilities in this domain. The results obtained indicate that the maritime navigation task remains difficult to solve without fine-tuning, even for larger online models.

</details>


### [339] [Fair on the Surface? Benchmarking Hidden-Output Fairness Gaps in LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08284)
*Chan Aristella Lu,Arya Fayyazi,Junhao Zhang,Saeid Shokoufa,Yue Xing,Zhen Xiang,Kyu Hyung Lee,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FairGap是首个联合评估LLM推荐系统在可观察输出层和隐藏表征层公平性的基准，通过受控反事实身份探针（性别、年龄、种族）量化输出偏移与内部表征偏移，并引入表征-输出对齐（ROA）指标来诊断二者关系。实验发现隐藏输出解耦普遍存在，输出层审计无法检测内部偏移，且激活导向干预在降低内部偏移的同时反而恶化输出公平性，揭示了两层公平之间的根本性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统的公平性审计仅关注可观察输出，隐含假设稳定输出反映稳定的内部处理，但这一假设从未被验证，也没有基准能够同时评估这两层。

Method: 构建FairGap基准，通过对性别、年龄和种族进行受控反事实身份探针，分别从输出偏移（OBS）和隐藏表征偏移（IBS）两个层面量化公平性，并用表征-输出对齐（ROA）指标及象限诊断法来刻画用户层面的隐藏-输出错配关系。

Result: 在6个开源LLM家族、3个领域上，ROA通常不超过0.22，说明隐藏输出解耦普遍存在；大量用户保持稳定输出但内部表征发生显著变化，此类模式仅依赖输出审计无法检测；激活导向可将IBS降低至8倍但同时恶化OBS。

Conclusion: LLM推荐系统在内部表征层面与输出层面的公平性存在根本性张力，现有仅基于输出的公平性评估框架不足以诊断这类问题，需要同时监测两层公平性。

Abstract: Fairness audits for LLM-based recommenders have largely focused on observable outputs, implicitly assuming that stable recommendations reflect stable internal processing. We challenge this assumption with FairGap, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate recommendation fairness at two levels: observable output shift (OBS) and hidden representation shift (IBS), measured through controlled counterfactual identity probes across gender, age, and race. Their relationship is summarized via Representation-Output Alignment (ROA), with quadrant diagnostics for identifying user-level hidden-output mismatch. Applied to six open-weight LLM families across three domains, FairGap reveals pervasive hidden-output decoupling: ROA rarely exceeds 0.22, and a non-negligible user population shows stable outputs despite substantial internal shifts, a mode that output-only audits cannot detect by design. Further, activation steering that reduces IBS by up to 8x simultaneously worsens OBS, demonstrating a fundamental tension between internal and output-level fairness that existing frameworks are unequipped to diagnose.

</details>


### [340] [Mitigating Over-Personalization in LLMs via Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08300)
*Hakeem Hannoon,Andrew Zhao,Mihir Narayan,Sharvin Goyal,Ivaxi Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究记忆增强型LLM中的两类失效模式——跨域泄漏（跨生活领域的记忆影响不相关场景的响应）和记忆诱导的谄媚（存储的用户信念使模型更倾向迎合而非如实回答）。作者提出一种仅改变推理时记忆呈现方式的方法（将无序记忆列表改为按领域分区的结构化格式），无需修改模型或记忆内容。在七种模型上的PersistBench基准测试中，该方法在保持效用的同时持续降低跨域泄漏，最强方法相对基线平均减少8.8%的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话助手越来越依赖持久性长期记忆来跨会话个性化响应，但重新引入存储的用户信息时，可能在不当或不相关的场景中影响响应。论文研究两种失效模式：跨域泄漏和记忆诱导的谄媚，旨在不改变模型和记忆内容的前提下缓解这些问题。

Method: 提出一种推理时的简单修改：将记忆以按领域（domain）分区的结构化格式呈现给模型，以替代常用的无序列表式"全部塞入上下文"格式。通过PersistBench基准，在七个模型上比较两种格式对跨域泄漏和实用性的影响。

Result: 结构化格式持续降低跨域泄漏同时保持实用效果，最强方法相对于基线平均减少8.8%的泄漏。

Conclusion: 仅通过改变记忆的呈现组织方式（按领域分区而非无序列表），就能在不修改模型参数或记忆内容的情况下有效缓解记忆增强LLM中的跨域泄漏问题，同时不损害模型效用。

Abstract: Conversational assistants increasingly rely on persistent long-term memory to personalize responses across sessions. However, when stored user information is reintroduced into the model context, it can also influence responses in inappropriate or unrelated settings. We study two such failure modes in memory-augmented LLMs: cross-domain leakage, where memories from one life domain affect responses in another, and memory-induced sycophancy, where stored user beliefs make models more likely to agree with the user rather than respond truthfully. We apply a simple inference-time modification to how memories are presented to the model, without changing the model or the memory contents. Across seven models on PersistBench, we compare the commonly used all-in context format, where memories are injected as an unstructured list, with structured formats that partition memories by domain. This simple modification consistently reduces cross-domain leakage while preserving utility, with our strongest method reducing leakage by $8.8\%$ on average relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [341] [Query-Only Backdoor Attacks on Self-Evolving Skills via Trajectory Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08303)
*Yuyang Luo,Haoran Wang,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹后门攻击（TBA），一种仅通过查询即可攻击自进化技能系统的攻击方法。攻击者通过精心构造的查询诱导智能体在轨迹中执行目标动作并显式声明激活条件，反复强化后使演化器将条件触发模式固化为可再生技能中的后门规则，在保持干净任务性能的同时植入条件后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能系统通过用可信的内部技能构建替代外部技能获取来减少技能注入攻击，但该演化管道可能引入新的攻击面：攻击者可通过智能体交互诱导受损轨迹，间接操纵技能演化方向，从而生成带有后门的技能。作者旨在证明这一新型威胁的存在。

Method: 提出TBA（Trajectory Backdoor Attack），一种仅查询的攻击方法。攻击者提交精心设计的查询，引导智能体执行目标动作并在轨迹中明确陈述对应的激活条件；在多个不同的触发任务中重复相同的条件-动作模式，而保持干净查询不变，从而促使演化器将该模式固化为可复用的触发依赖规则进入演化技能。

Result: 在三个基准测试、两个技能演化系统以及四个开源和闭源骨干模型上的实验表明，TBA能够可靠地植入条件后门，同时保持干净任务效用，效果达到甚至超过直接技能注入。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了轨迹驱动的技能演化系统中存在严重的安全漏洞，表明自进化技能管道并非天然免疫于攻击，需要针对轨迹层面的间接操纵设计新的防御机制。

Abstract: Agentic skills improve large language model (LLM) agents by encoding reusable procedures for complex tasks. However, manually authored skills often adapt poorly to long-horizon tasks and changing environments. To address the limitation, self-evolving skill systems have been developed to automatically construct and update skills from execution trajectories, shifting skill acquisition from external marketplaces to a trusted evolution pipeline. By replacing external skill acquisition with trusted internal construction, self-evolving skill systems reduce exposure to skill injection attacks that rely on direct skill manipulation. However, this skill evolution pipeline may introduce a new attack surface in which an attacker can indirectly steer skill evolution by inducing compromised trajectories through agent interactions. To demonstrate the threat, we propose Trajectory Backdoor Attack (TBA), a query-only attack that steers a trusted skill-evolution pipeline toward producing a backdoored skill. Specifically, we craft attacker-submitted queries to lead the agent to perform the target action and explicitly state the corresponding activation condition in the trajectory. We repeat the same condition-action pattern across diverse triggered tasks, while leaving clean queries unchanged, encouraging the evolver to consolidate the pattern as a reusable trigger-dependent rule into the evolved skill. Experiments on three benchmarks across two skill-evolution systems using four open- and closed-source backbone models demonstrate that TBA reliably implants conditional backdoors while preserving clean-task utility, matching or even surpassing direct skill injection. The results reveal a critical vulnerability in trajectory-driven skill evolution.

</details>


### [342] [StructReward: Efficient Structured Process Rewards for Self-Correcting Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08326)
*Yifan Li,Ruxin Sun,Tongzhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StructReward框架，通过结构化步骤级奖励对齐为多模态推理强化学习提供密集反馈信号，无需额外训练验证器或外部LLM评判，计算开销显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅基于最终答案正确性给二元奖励，丢失了中间推理步骤的监督信息；而过程奖励模型需要单独训练的验证器、昂贵的思维链标注或在线LLM评判，计算成本高。需要一种既能提供细粒度过程监督又计算高效的方案。

Method: StructReward将生成的解决方案表示为推理步骤序列，使用轻量级数值、符号和词汇匹配规则与过程标注的参考步骤对齐；将对齐标签聚合成密集过程奖励，并结合最终答案一致性和输出有效性奖励，通过门控GRPO目标进行优化；回收策略回放用于响应比较和反思性自纠错；利用强LLM将正确轨迹重写为面向反思的训练实例以增强策略的评估和细化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，结构化过程监督和回放回收为自改进的多模态推理提供了一条高效路径，在显著降低计算开销的同时提升了推理性能。

Conclusion: StructReward证明了在不依赖额外学习验证器或外部LLM评判的前提下，通过轻量级步骤级奖励对齐和策略回放回收，能够实现计算高效且有效的多模态推理强化学习自改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective approach for improving multimodal reasoning. However, most existing methods evaluate an entire response using a binary reward based only on final-answer correctness, thereby discarding the supervision available in intermediate reasoning steps. Process reward models offer finer-grained feedback, but they typically rely on separately trained verifiers, costly chain-of-thought annotations, or online judging by large language models (LLMs). In this work, we introduce StructReward, a compute-efficient framework that provides dense reinforcement signals through structured step-level reward alignment. StructReward represents each generated solution as a sequence of reasoning steps and aligns them with process-labeled reference steps using lightweight numerical, symbolic, and lexical matching rules. The aligned labels are aggregated into a dense process reward and combined with final-answer consistency and output-validity rewards through a gated Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective. We further recycle policy rollouts into complementary supervision for response comparison and reflective self-correction, rather than discarding them after policy updates. Separately, we use a strong LLM to rewrite sampled correct trajectories into reflection-oriented training instances, further strengthening the policy's ability to evaluate and refine its reasoning. Since reward computation is performed online without an additional learned verifier or external LLM judge, StructReward substantially reduces the computational overhead of multimodal reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that structured process supervision and rollout recycling provide an efficient path toward self-improving multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [343] [LLMVisor: A Real-Time Latency Attribution Model for Multi-Tenant LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08382)
*Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Jiaxin Shan,Le Xu,Tieying Zhang,Liguang Xie,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMVisor是一个基于roofline模型的延迟归因框架，用于多租户GPU集群中LLM推理调度的实时per-request延迟归因。它通过分段线性函数捕获内存受限和计算受限阶段，将批量延迟分解为可加性的每请求份额，在微秒级运行，相比token-count基线将相对误差降低多达2.5-4.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理转向多租户GPU集群，co-batching虽然提升了吞吐量，但模糊了单租户的使用情况并限制了控制能力。要实现推理引擎的分式共享，需要一种实时、准确的per-request归因原语，且开销足够低以运行在调度循环内部。

Method: LLMVisor采用roofline引导的延迟归因模型，通过简洁的分段线性函数形式捕获内存受限和计算受限两个阶段，特征与FLOPs和内存I/O流量成比例。该模型将批量延迟分解为可加性的每请求份额，并在微秒尺度高效运行。

Result: 在Llama 3.1-8B和Qwen 2.5-14B/32B模型、A100/H100 GPU以及不同张量并行度和工作负载混合下评估。相比token-count基线，LLMVisor在prefill阶段p90和p99相对误差分别降低最多2.5倍和3.3倍，在decode阶段分别降低最多3.5倍和4.4倍，并接近完美的R-squared。

Conclusion: LLMVisor作为一种轻量级且高精度的延迟归因原语，能够在调度循环内有效地分解每请求延迟，在批处理可变性和序列分歧下仍然保持准确，为多租户LLM推理引擎的分式共享和精细化资源控制提供了支撑。

Abstract: As LLM inference shifts to multi-tenant GPU clusters, co-batching improves throughput but obscures per-tenant usage and limits control. Enabling fractional sharing of the inference engine requires a real-time, per-request attribution primitive that is accurate and light enough to run inside the scheduling loop. We present LLMVisor, a roofline-guided latency attribution model that captures the memory-bound and compute-bound phases via a concise piecewise-linear form over features proportional to FLOPs and memory I/O traffic. LLMVisor decomposes batch latency into additive, per-request shares and runs efficiently at microsecond scale. We evaluate LLMVisor across Llama 3.1-8B and Qwen 2.5-14B/32B on A100/H100 GPUs under varying tensor parallelism and workload mixes. Compared to a token-count baseline, LLMVisor attains near-perfect R-squared and reduces relative error by up to 2.5x and 3.3x at p90 and p99, respectively, for prefill, and by up to 3.5x and 4.4x for decode, despite batching variability and sequence divergence.

</details>


### [344] [Not Worth Another Token: Marginal Value Estimation for Efficient Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08389)
*Harshitha Kolukuluru,Reshma Ashok,Kirat Arora,Evan William Ciccarelli,Nischal Ashok Kumar,Lunyiu Nie,Franck Dernoncourt,Samyadeep Basu,Ryan A. Rossi,Nedim Lipka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度研究智能体中上下文管理的边际价值估计问题，首次对预检索、后检索和预合成三个阶段进行了系统性的修剪策略对比。结果表明，修剪效果更多取决于应用阶段而非评分规则：早期修剪带来最大的端到端节省，轻量启发式方法可在损失少量质量的情况下减少最多73%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程研究智能体通过迭代检索、聚合和合成来解决开放任务，但随着过程推进，上下文快速增长而额外证据的边际价值往往递减，导致不必要的token开销、更高的延迟以及最终报告生成的噪声输入。因此需要研究上下文管理中的边际价值估计问题。

Method: 本文首次对深度研究智能体管道中的修剪策略进行系统性的阶段感知对比，在预检索、后检索和预合成三个阶段评估了轻量级启发式标准和学习的价值模型。

Result: 轻量级启发式方法最多可减少73%的token使用且质量损失很小；学习式修剪在特定权衡下保持竞争力；没有任何单一方法在质量、效率和忠实度三个维度上全面占优。关键发现是修剪的有效性更依赖于应用阶段而非评分规则。

Conclusion: 早期修剪能带来最大的端到端节省，而后期修剪主要优化最终合成上下文。这些发现为设计高效的长时程智能体系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Long-horizon research agents solve open-ended tasks through iterative retrieval, aggregation, and synthesis, but context grows rapidly while the marginal value of additional evidence often declines. This leads to unnecessary token cost, higher latency, and noisier inputs for final report generation. We study marginal value estimation for context management in deep research agents and present the first systematic stage-aware comparison of pruning strategies across the pipeline. We evaluate lightweight heuristic criteria and a learned value model at pre-retrieval, post-retrieval, and pre-synthesis stages. Our results show that pruning effectiveness depends more on where pruning is applied than on the specific scoring rule: early pruning yields the largest end-to-end savings, while later pruning mainly refines the final synthesis context. Lightweight heuristics reduce token usage by up to 73% with little quality degradation, learned pruning remains competitive on selected trade-offs, and no single method dominates across quality, efficiency, and faithfulness. These findings provide practical guidance for designing efficient long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [345] [CAP: A Scalable Benchmark for Evaluating Cross-Site Browser Agents with Complex Actions and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08392)
*Zejun Xu,Taiyi Chen,Jin Li,Yongtong Gu,Qi Cheng,Aixuan Lv,Shuai Zhu,Pengfei Zhu,Kaichen Yang,Boyu Sun,Yixian Yang,Mulong Xie,Xin Liu,Dagang Li,Xiaoteng Ma,Hongru Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAP基准，用于评估浏览器智能体在跨网站、类人网页任务上的表现。通过分解-重组流水线，将网站抽象为结构化站点卡片（包含用户功能、复杂操作和感知需求），再重组为现实跨站工作流。构建了覆盖108个真实网站、24个领域、420个任务的数据集，实验表明现有智能体成功率低下，感知密集型交互是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体评估主要关注端到端任务成功率，忽略了真实网页浏览中两类根本性难点：富用户界面上的复杂操作，以及动态渲染内容的视觉感知，尤其在跨多网站的工作流中。缺乏同时涵盖这两方面挑战的可扩展评估基准。

Method: 提出分解-重组（decomposition-and-recomposition）管线：先将每个网站抽象为结构化"站点卡片"（site card），记录其面向用户的功能、复杂执行操作和感知需求；再将站点卡片重组为现实的跨网站工作流。每个任务都基于多个具体操作进行细粒度诊断，并采用可验证的agent-as-a-judge评估框架。

Result: 构建了420个任务，覆盖108个真实网站和24个领域，在严格质量控制下完成。实验中，最先进的浏览器智能体在CAP基准上成功率较低，揭示了感知密集型交互仍是主要瓶颈，暴露了当前智能体与真实网页浏览需求之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: CAP基准成功填补了现有浏览器智能体评估在跨网站复杂UI操作和视觉感知方面的空白，证明了当前智能体在真实网页浏览场景中的表现仍远未达到实用水平，感知维度是亟待突破的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that interact with the web through browsers. While recent progress has been driven by benchmarks that evaluate end-to-end task success, these evaluations largely overlook two fundamental sources of difficulty in real web browsing: complex actions over rich user interfaces and visual perception of dynamically rendered content, especially in workflows that span multiple websites. We introduce CAP, a scalable benchmark for evaluating browser agents on cross-site, human-like web tasks that require non-trivial UI interactions and visual understanding. Specifically, we adopt a decomposition-and-recomposition pipeline that first abstracts each website into a structured site card capturing user-facing functions, complex execution operations, and perceptual requirements, and then recomposes these components into realistic cross-site workflows. Each task is therefore grounded in multiple specific operations on each website, enabling fine-grained diagnosis. Built on this framework, we construct 420 tasks across 108 real-world websites and 24 domains under careful quality control. Experiments on state-of-the-art browser agents using our verifiable agent-as-a-judge evaluation framework show low success rates and reveal that perception-heavy interactions remain a major bottleneck, exposing substantial gaps between current agents and real-world web browsing demands.

</details>


### [346] [Estimating Uncertainty in Galaxy Morphology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08398)
*Kai Cheng,Ruoqi Wang,Qiong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UEGMC框架，一种面向星系形态分类的即插即用不确定性估计框架，从冻结的深度基础模型骨干中直接预测不确定性，无需昂贵的采样计算，并在实验中取得了与现有方法相当的不确定性量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文数据具有固有噪声和星系演化的形态歧义性，不确定性评估对星系形态分类至关重要。然而当前基础模型作为确定性点估计器，无法量化不确定性，缺乏对星系形态分类结果不确定性的系统评估研究。

Method: UEGMC是一个事后处理框架，将星系形态分类中的不确定性按模型参数、天文数据、参考标准或内在物理歧义进行分类。该框架直接从冻结的基础模型骨干提取的表征中预测不确定性，避免了计算昂贵的采样过程，从而实现细粒度的不确定性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明UEGMC在不确定性量化性能上与现有方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: UEGMC为星系形态分类提供了高效且无需采样的不确定性估计方案，弥补了当前基础模型在不确定性量化方面的空白，有助于提升分类的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Astronomers classify galaxy morphology to investigate cosmic evolution. While deep foundation models are increasingly utilized in Galaxy Morphology Classification (GMC), little work has been done on evaluating the uncertainty of GMC results. Uncertainty evaluation is important because astronomical data are inherently noisy due to instrumental and environmental limitations. Also, the continuous evolution of galaxies creates intrinsic morphological ambiguity. However, current foundation models operate as deterministic point estimators, failing to quantify the uncertainty. To overcome this limitation, we propose UEGMC, a post-hoc framework of Uncertainty Estimation for Galaxy Morphology Classification. It categorizes uncertainty in GMC into distinct types by model parameters, astronomical data, reference standards, or intrinsic physical ambiguities, thereby facilitating better classification. Our framework can directly predict uncertainties from representations extracted from the frozen backbones of foundation models, without computationally expensive sampling, therefore enabling fine-grained uncertainty evaluations. Our experimental results demonstrate that UEGMC provides competitive uncertainty quantification performance compared with previous methods.

</details>


### [347] [Forgotten History or Test-of-Time? Retrospect and Prospect on RAG from an IR Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08445)
*Xiaoyan Zhao,Yujie Cai,Yang Zhang,Grace Hui Yang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证RAG的核心思想并非LLM时代的创新，而是早在2000年代初的IR和QA研究中就已存在。现代RAG和Agentic RAG应被视为数十年QA架构之上的新接口层，而非全新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为RAG被视为LLM局限性的产物这一主流观点忽略了其深厚的历史渊源，导致社区碎片化、术语变迁和近因偏差使得RAG与经典IR/QA研究之间的联系被低估，进而造成研究社区之间缺乏融合，且存在重复"重新发明轮子"的风险。

Method: 通过系统性地追溯现代RAG和Agentic RAG与早期（2000年代初）IR和QA研究的思想谱系，识别出检索与生成集成、知识增强、答案验证、迭代查询/提示精炼这四大核心概念的古典先例，并分析这种连续性未被充分认识的原因。

Result: 证明RAG将检索与语言生成集成、知识增强、答案验证和迭代式查询（或提示）精炼等核心概念，早在LLM出现之前就已在IR和QA领域被研究和实例化；并指出用户建模、答案验证、查询精炼等方面被忽视的既有工作可以指导下一代RAG设计。

Conclusion: 应将LLM视为建立在数十年QA架构之上的新接口层，而非检索增强智能的起点。这种历史重框架不仅具有学术价值，还能通过挖掘未被充分利用的早期IR研究成果，促进跨社区整合并减少无意的重复发现。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely regarded as a novel paradigm born from the limitations of large language models (LLMs)--a mechanism to ground their outputs in external knowledge. This view, however, is incomplete when considered within a broader historical context. In this paper, we argue that the core ideas underlying RAG are not new: foundational concepts such as integrating retrieval and language generation, knowledge augmentation, answer verification, and iterative query (or prompt) refinement had already been studied and instantiated in information retrieval (IR) and question answering (QA) research dating back to the early 2000s, well before the emergence of LLMs.
  We make this case by systematically tracing the intellectual lineage of modern RAG and Agentic RAG back to their classical IR and QA antecedents, and examining why this continuity has gone under-recognized -- a consequence of community fragmentation, shifting terminology, and the recency bias endemic to fast-moving fields. Rather than treating LLMs as the origin point of retrieval-augmented intelligence, we propose viewing them as a new interface layer atop a decades-old QA architecture. This reframing is not merely historical: by situating RAG within the longer trajectory of IR research, we surface underutilized prior work -- on user modeling, answer validation, and query refinement -- that can directly inform next-generation RAG design, reducing unintentional rediscovery and fostering genuine cross-community integration.

</details>


### [348] [TRACE-Memory: Public-Conditioned Retrieval and Utility-Aware Evidence Admission for Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08446)
*Jing Wang,Zhu Wang,Yifan Guo,Yulong Yang,Yunji Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-Memory提出两阶段选择性个性化框架：第一阶段生成查询以获取用户专属且缺失的信息，第二阶段依据响应级增量效用仅采纳有额外价值的证据单元（甚至可以为空集），通过SFT初始化、分阶段GRPO预热和嵌套多采样联合GRPO渐进训练，在Goodreads、Amazon Reviews和Reddit的4,500个任务上超越随机和词法记忆使用，与语义检索及前沿LLM记忆流水线相比具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化生成系统仅基于请求-记忆相关性检索用户历史并注入上下文，但相关历史可能涉及错误的偏好方面、包含重复的公共信息或提供不足的支持。作者认为个人记忆应仅在能超越纯公共响应效用时使用，即需要选择性个性化而非默认个性化。

Method: 提出TRACE-Memory两阶段框架。阶段1：查询请求和公共上下文中缺失的用户特定信息，检索覆盖导向的候选池；阶段2：根据响应级增量效用，采纳紧凑的、可溯源证据单元子集或空集。训练流程为结构化SFT初始化、降维空间分阶段GRPO预热、嵌套多样本联合GRPO渐进训练查询生成和证据采纳策略。

Result: 在来自Goodreads、Amazon Reviews和Reddit的4,500个受控与自然任务上，TRACE-Memory持续优于随机和词法记忆使用，优于语义检索；随着本地生成器容量增大，与前沿LLM记忆流水线保持竞争力；其证据采纳能依据公共上下文充分性进行条件判定，支持选择性个性化。

Conclusion: 选择性使用个人记忆而非默认注入所有相关历史是更优策略，TRACE-Memory通过两阶段选择与渐进强化学习训练验证了这一观点，表明证据采纳应以公共上下文充分性为条件，只在记忆带来增量效用时才予以使用。

Abstract: Personalized generation systems retrieve user history by request--memory relevance and inject it into the model context. Yet relevant history may concern the wrong preference aspect, duplicate public information, or provide insufficient support. We argue that personal memory should be used only when it adds utility beyond a public-only response. We propose TRACE-Memory, a two-stage framework for selective personalization. Stage 1 queries for user-specific information missing from the request and public context, then retrieves a coverage-oriented candidate pool. Stage 2 admits a compact subset of source-traceable evidence units, or the empty set, according to response-level incremental utility. We progressively train the query-generation and evidence-admission policies through structured SFT initialization, reduced-space stage-wise GRPO warm-up, and nested multi-sample Joint GRPO. Across 4,500 Controlled and Natural tasks from Goodreads, Amazon Reviews, and Reddit, TRACE-Memory consistently outperforms random and lexical memory use, improves over semantic retrieval, remains competitive with frontier-LLM memory pipelines as local generator capacity increases, and conditions evidence admission on public-context sufficiency, supporting selective rather than default personalization.

</details>


### [349] [What Keeps Agent Skills from Being Reusable? Evidence from 138K SKILL.md Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08453)
*Chi Zhang,Yimin Liu,Xinze Chen,Ping Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对138,133个公开SKILL.md文件（来自20,556个仓库）进行大规模实证分析，发现91.8%的Agent Skills存在至少一种缺陷，主要问题为元数据路由薄弱、内容臃肿或不可执行、资源组织不当等打包问题；并通过路由压力测试和修复实验验证了轻量级质量保障工作流的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Agent Skills被设计为可跨对话重用的组件，但许多公开技能似乎仅源自单一任务、仓库或会话，与其"可复用组件"的定位不符。作者希望系统化地量化这一差距，理解缺陷的类型、普遍性及其对功能的实际影响。

Method: 作者构建基于官方规范和最佳实践指南的两级缺陷分类体系（taxonomy），对138,133个公开SKILL.md文件进行静态分析；采用宽松和严格两种阈值验证鲁棒性；设计确定性路由压力测试（20,000个技能）评估路由元数据对检索可靠性的影响；并按平台和来源（spec-aware/AI-marked）分层对比缺陷率；最后开展轻量级修复实验验证质量保障工作流。

Result: 91.8%的技能包含至少一个缺陷（严格/宽松阈值下估计稳定在88.8%-94.6%）；主要缺陷是普通的打包问题而非恶意攻击；具备有效路由元数据的技能在路由压力测试中从启动描述中被更可靠地检索到；规范感知（specification-aware）技能缺陷更少，而AI标记技能在安全性和可移植性方面问题更多；轻量级修复实验支持质量保障工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: 公开Agent Skills存在系统性的质量问题，以可修复的打包缺陷为主。结合规范感知提示、轻量级linting、自动修复和安全门控的质量保障生成工作流，可以有效降低缺陷率并提升技能的可靠检索与安全可移植性。

Abstract: Under the current standard, Agent Skills are SKILL.md files that combine instructions with supporting resources, enabling Large Language Model (LLM) agents to reuse procedures beyond a single conversation. Yet many public skills appear to originate from a single task, repository, or conversation, even when they are shared as reusable components. We analyze this gap across 138,133 public SKILL.md files from 20,556 repositories using a two-tier defect taxonomy grounded in the official specification and best-practice guidance. We find that 91.8% of skills contain at least one detected defect, with stable estimates across lenient and strict thresholds (88.8-94.6%). The dominant failures are ordinary packaging problems rather than exotic attacks: weak routing metadata, bloated or non-actionable bodies, and poor resource organization. A deterministic routing stress test over 20,000 skills shows the functional impact: skills with valid routing metadata are retrieved more reliably from startup descriptions than skills with routing defects. Defect rates vary by platform and provenance: specification-aware skills contain fewer defects, while AI-marked skills show more safety and portability problems. Lightweight enforcement and repair experiments support a quality-assured generation workflow combining spec-aware prompting, lightweight linting, automated repair, and safety gating.

</details>


### [350] [Hierarchical Self-Improvement: A Framework for Task-Specific Evolvable Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08466)
*Tailin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化自我改进（HSI）框架，通过固定任务注入接缝实现任务专属且可连续演化的harness（脚手架）系统。单个冻结LLM在三个层次上工作：执行任务的harness、重写harness的evolver、在冻结外层锚点下重写evolver策略代码的meta-evolver。通过thinking-on/off设计隔离harness演化的贡献。实验在BALROG基准上取得显著提升（BabyAI +39.3, Crafter +33.0, TextWorld +25.0, MiniHack +15.0），并在BabaIsAI子套件上展现强泛化能力，但在超出骨干能力的任务（NLE）上无改进，验证了任务专属harness演化作为改进冻结LLM智能体的可行途径及其明确的经验边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的改进主要通过人工修改提示词、工具或工作流实现，而可执行脚手架（harness）通常被视为部署后固定的组件。作者认为harness应为任务专属且可连续演化，而非固定不变，从而探索一条新的智能体改进轴线。

Method: HSI框架包含三个层次范围：任务harness H负责执行任务；一个evolver负责重写harness；一个meta-evolver在冻结外层锚点下重写evolver的策略代码。通过thinking-on/off设计，在任务执行时禁用推理以隔离harness演化的贡献，在自我修改时启用推理。演化过程受两个边界约束：反馈保真度边界（需要信息丰富的奖励信号）和骨干能力边界（harness重新设计无法克服冻结模型能力限制）。

Result: 以DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview为冻结骨干的BALROG实验中，HSI在中等难度任务上持续超越初始harness：BabyAI +39.3、Crafter +33.0、TextWorld +25.0、MiniHack +15.0（原始%进度）。BabaIsAI子套件上展现强留出泛化（BreakStop 0.98最佳测试、GoTo 1.00，来自20%未见拆分）。在超出骨干能力任务（NLE）上无改进。

Conclusion: 任务专属harness演化是在明确经验边界内改进冻结LLM智能体的可行轴线；其有效性受反馈保真度和骨干能力两个机制约束，且具有清晰的适用限制。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents are often improved by modifying prompts, tools, or workflows manually, while the executable scaffold surrounding the model---the \emph{harness}---is typically treated as a fixed artifact after deployment. This work studies an alternative where the harness is \emph{task-specific and continuously evolvable}: each task family maintains its own harness, which is hot-swapped across iterations through a fixed task-injection seam and rewritten using environment feedback. We introduce \textbf{Hierarchical Self-Improvement (HSI)}, a framework in which a single frozen LLM $M$ operates across three hierarchical scopes: a task harness $H$ that executes tasks, an evolver that rewrites $H$, and a meta-evolver that rewrites the evolver's strategy code under a frozen outer anchor. A thinking-on/off design isolates the contribution of harness evolution by disabling reasoning during task execution while enabling it during self-modification. HSI is bounded by two factors: a \emph{feedback-fidelity bound}, since evolution requires informative reward signals to guide selection, and a \emph{backbone capability bound}, since harness redesign cannot overcome limitations of the frozen model. On BALROG with DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview as the frozen backbone, HSI achieves consistent gains over the initial harness on moderate-difficulty tasks ($+39.3$ on BabyAI, $+33.0$ on Crafter, $+25.0$ on TextWorld, and $+15.0$ on MiniHack, all in raw \% Progress), while obtaining strong held-out generalization on BabaIsAI sub-suites ($0.98$ best-test on BreakStop and $1.00$ on GoTo from a $20\%$ unseen split). On tasks beyond the backbone's capability (NLE), harness evolution provides no improvement. These results demonstrate task-specific harness evolution as a viable axis for improving frozen LLM agents under clear empirical limits. Code is available at https://github.com/TailinZhou/hsi.

</details>


### [351] [LLM within MCP Matters: Measuring Inefficient Resource Utilization Driven by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08467)
*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Kihyeon Jeong,Byeongkyu Jeon,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过54,000次试验、覆盖24个LLM（9个Claude、6个Gemini、9个GPT）在生产级法律信息MCP服务器上的研究，考察客户端LLM是否真正消费指令中嵌入的参考数据。研究发现：当移除竞争性的搜索工具时，23/24个模型能可靠读取嵌入式数据（命中率≥98%）；而仅保留搜索工具存在时，9个模型命中率降至15%以下，表明失败主要由行为偏好而非能力缺失主导。2^3因子分析显示指令级干预存在强交互效应，组合干预可恢复大多数模型性能，但单一干预可能对特定模型家族产生反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（模型上下文协议）标准化了服务器向LLM暴露数据和工具的方式，常见设计将频繁使用的参考数据（如标识符查找表）直接嵌入服务器系统提示词中。然而，尚不清楚客户端LLM是否真正消费这些指令中嵌入的数据，当查询涉及嵌入表条目时模型能否直接响应而非重复搜索。作者希望弄清失败的原因——是能力缺失还是行为偏好——并探索指令级干预能否改善这一状况。

Method: 研究者在一个生产级法律信息MCP服务器上开展54,000次试验，覆盖24个LLM（9个Claude、6个Gemini、9个GPT）。采用诊断条件（移除竞争性搜索工具）来区分能力缺失与行为偏好；随后进行2^3因子分析，测试三种指令级干预（及其组合）对模型消费嵌入式数据行为的影响。

Result: 当搜索工具不可用时，23/24个模型能可靠读取嵌入式数据（命中率≥98%），证明能力普遍存在；但仅当搜索工具存在时，9个模型命中率降至15%以下，凸显行为偏好是主要失败原因。2^3因子分析显示三种指令级干预存在强交互效应：三者组合可使20/24个模型恢复至少86%的命中率，但单一干预可能对特定模型家族产生反效果。

Conclusion: 针对不同服务器的提示工程只是权宜之计而非根本解决方案，因为干预效果因模型而异且存在交互效应。作者主张MCP宿主应用应提供显式机制，在客户端LLM的决策过程中将服务器指令置于工具选择之前，从架构层面解决指令嵌入式数据被忽视的问题。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how servers expose data and tools to Large Language Models (LLMs). A common server design embeds frequently used reference data, such as identifier lookup tables, directly in the server instructions: the system-prompt text a server hands to the host application. When a query concerns an entry of the embedded table, the model can act on it immediately instead of re-discovering the same information through a search tool. We test whether client LLMs actually consume such instruction-embedded data, reporting a 54,000-trial study across 24 LLMs (9 Claude, 6 Gemini, 9 GPT) on a production legal-information MCP server. A diagnostic condition that removes the competing search tool shows that failures are dominated by behavioral preference rather than missing capability. With search unavailable, 23 of 24 models read the embedded data reliably (hit ratio at least 98%); with a search tool merely present, 9 models drop below 15%. A 2^3 factorial analysis of three instruction-level interventions reveals strong interaction effects: combining all three restores at least 86% for 20 of 24 models, but individual interventions can backfire for specific model families. Per-server prompt engineering is therefore a workaround rather than a fix; we argue that MCP host applications should provide an explicit mechanism that places server instructions ahead of tool selection in the client LLM's deliberation.

</details>


### [352] [Aero Realtime: Fully Aligned Input-Output Streams for Low-Latency Streaming Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08469)
*Kaichen Zhang,Wei Huang,Keming Wu,Bo Li,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aero Realtime 是一个 4B 参数的流式多模态模型，采用双工（duplex）架构，将视频、音频和文本输出对齐到共享时间网格上，每个约80ms的音频槽位预测词元或静音词元，使输入输出同步推进，实现实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式多模态模型虽能增量处理观测，但仍遵循基于轮次的prefill-then-decode模式，无法实现真正的双工交互——新观测不能自然进入活跃的生成流中。已有的主动式备选方案（微轮询或外部响应门控）会碎片化连续交互、使响应时机与语言生成脱钩，并增加KV缓存友好的服务难度。

Method: 1) 将视频、音频和文本输出对齐在共享时间网格上，每个约80ms槽位预测词元或静音词元；2) 采用单一自回归目标同时学习何时响应与生成什么；3) 推理时仅追加最新的多模态槽位，携带上一输出状态并复用KV缓存实现高效增量执行；4) 提供完整训练与服务方案，包括实时QA构建、槽位对齐监督、硬件感知分布式训练、可恢复推理。

Result: 在4块NVIDIA A6000工作站GPU上，对持续20分钟连续流式视频保持84ms中位数和173ms P95处理延迟，始终保持在源时间线的200ms以内，验证了双工主动式多模态交互的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了全对齐的输入输出建模在双工、主动式和硬件对齐的多模态交互中的可行性，为流式多模态模型提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Existing streaming multimodal models process observations incrementally but still follow a turn-based prefill-then-decode pattern, making them non-duplex: new observations cannot naturally enter an active generation stream. Proactive alternatives use micro-turn polling or external response gates, which fragment continuous interaction, decouple response timing from language generation, and complicate KV-cache-friendly serving. We introduce Aero Realtime, a 4B streaming multimodal model with a duplex architecture for realtime generation. Aero Realtime aligns video, audio, and textual output on a shared temporal grid, where each approximately 80-ms audio slot predicts either a lexical token or a silence token. This allows input and output to advance together, enabling one autoregressive objective to learn both when to respond and what to generate. During inference, Aero Realtime appends only the newest multimodal slot, carries forward the previous output state, and reuses the KV cache for efficient incremental execution. We further provide a complete training and serving recipe, including realtime QA construction, slot-aligned supervision, hardware-aware distributed training, and resumable inference. On four NVIDIA A6000 workstation GPUs, Aero Realtime maintains 84-ms median and 173-ms P95 processing lag over 20 minutes of a continuously streamed video, remaining within 200~ms of the source timeline. These results demonstrate the feasibility of fully aligned input-output modeling for duplex, proactive, and hardware-aligned multimodal interaction.

</details>


### [353] [Yesterday's Shield, Today's Spear: A Self-Evolving Safety Guardrail in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08471)
*Cong Ming,Jingyi Chen,Bin Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Yingfei Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SESG 是一个运行于生产环境的多智能体安全护栏系统，通过监控线上流量检测新型越狱攻击和新增有害类别，并自动生成配对训练数据、重新平衡训练集并路由更新，使 1.7B 护栏模型在 16-24 小时内适应新威胁（仅需约 2 小时人工），在六轮在线进化中超越静态护栏和自适应基线，并已作为深信服的主要更新管线自主关闭了两个月内 14/15 个新威胁场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有已部署的 LLM 安全护栏本质上是静态的：训练一次后在发布时冻结，而新的越狱技术和此前未覆盖的有害类别在数天内就会出现，导致防御永远滞后于攻击一步。这使得需要一种能够持续自我更新的安全护栏系统。

Method: SESG 由多个智能体协同工作：监控智能体监测已部署护栏背后的实时流量，发现两类失败——形式新颖的越狱攻击和内容新颖的有害类别；生成智能体针对确认的失败合成配对训练数据；验证智能体根据部署模型出错的方面重新平衡批次，使模型的自身错误引导训练集；路由智能体将训练动作与诊断出的差距匹配并将新版本部署回生产环境。

Result: 通过六轮在线进化（V0 到 V6），1.7B 护栏模型在 16-24 小时内适应新威胁，仅需约 2 小时人工成本，而传统手动流程需要 40-90 小时。在六个新兴威胁上，它优于 0.6B 到 9B 的静态护栏和一个自适应基线，同时保持了通用筛查能力。自 2026 年 4 月起，SESG 作为深信服护栏的主要更新管线，两个月内自主关闭了 15 个新威胁场景中的 14 个。论文还发布了 6 个新威胁的 9 个测试集。

Conclusion: SESG 证明了安全护栏可以从静态防御转变为动态自我进化的系统，通过生产环境中的持续学习和自动更新，显著缩短了从新威胁出现到防御部署的时间窗口，同时保持了模型的一般安全性筛选能力，使防御能够跟上攻击演进的速度。

Abstract: Deployed LLM safety guardrails are predominantly static: trained once and frozen at release, while new jailbreak techniques and previously un-addressed harmful categories emerge within days, leaving the defense perpetually a step behind. We present SESG (Self-Evolving Safety Guardrails), a multi-agent system running in production. SESG monitors the live traffic behind a deployed guardrail and surfaces two classes of failure: jailbreaks novel in form and harmful categories novel in content. Once a failure is confirmed, a generation agent synthesizes paired training data targeted at it; a validation agent rebalances the batch toward the direction in which the deployed model errs, so that the model's own mistakes steer its training set; and a routing agent matches the training action to the diagnosed gap and returns the next version to production. Over six rounds of live evolution (V0 to V6), a 1.7B guardrail adapts to a new threat in 16-24 hours, with about 2 hours of human effort, versus the 40-90 hours of the manual process it replaces. On six emerging threats, it outperforms static guardrails from 0.6B to 9B and an adaptive baseline while preserving its general screening competence. Since April 2026, SESG has been the primary update pipeline of Sangfor's guardrail, autonomously closing 14 of 15 new threat scenarios in two months. We release 9 test sets for the 6 new threats at https://github.com/Trams1017/SESG. Warning: This paper contains examples that may be harmful or offensive.

</details>


### [354] [HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference: Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds for Zero-Shot LLM Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08485)
*Tak Ho Alex Li,Kaijie Liu,Lik-Hang Lee,Kin Chung Ho,Ping Shum,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HoloAegis提出了一种极简参数化的拓扑推理框架，将安全护栏从表示学习中解耦，仅通过冻结语义表示上的纯几何推理实现LLM安全检测，在8个基准上达到SOTA精度且延迟低于毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全护栏面临根本性张力：微调会扭曲预训练表示，而生成式评判器推理成本过高。作者质疑主流范式，探索是否可以通过冻结语义表示上的纯几何推理实现安全检测，从而同时规避表示退化和高昂推理成本。

Method: HoloAegis采用最小参数化拓扑推理框架：仅有的自由参数是锚点数量K和温度tau，均在构建后固定，无需梯度训练。未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面，之后所有决策完全基于几何。安全评估被形式化为对预计算系统拓扑锚典的吉布斯-玻尔兹曼自由能计算，并引入双时间尺度指数移动平均检测多轮语义漂移。核心理论洞见是拓扑边界稳定性猜想，即稀疏锚点质心比全向量空间方法更能稳定决策边界对抗高频词汇扰动。

Result: 在8个基准上，HoloAegis达到SOTA精度：AuthenHallu上AUC为1.0000，HarmBench上为0.9802，中文CHIFRAUD上跨语言迁移AUC达0.9758。同时具备亚毫秒级延迟、零冷启动数据和跨语言能力。

Conclusion: 作者挑战了安全必须依赖微调或生成式评判的主流范式，证明通过纯拓扑几何推理（最小参数化）即可在冻结表示上实现高效、精确且跨语言的安全检测，为LLM安全护栏提供了新的理论方向和实用方案。

Abstract: Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We challenge the prevailing paradigm by asking: can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen semantic representations? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning. We term our approach minimally parametric because the only free parameters are the anchor count K and the temperature tau, both fixed after construction and requiring no gradient-based training. An un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to a unit sphere, after which all decisions are purely geometric. We formalize safety evaluation as a Gibbs-Boltzmann Free Energy computation over a pre-computed System Topology Anchor Bank, and we introduce Dual Time-Scale Exponential Moving Averages to detect progressive multi-turn semantic drift. Our key theoretical insight is a Topological Boundary Stability Conjecture: we provide theoretical motivation and strong empirical evidence that sparse anchor centroids stabilize the decision boundary against high-frequency lexical perturbations far better than full vector space methods. Evaluated across 8 benchmarks, HoloAegis achieves state-of-the-art accuracy (1.0000 AUC on AuthenHallu, 0.9802 on HarmBench) with sub-millisecond latency, zero cold-start data, and cross-lingual transfer (0.9758 AUC on Chinese CHIFRAUD).

</details>


### [355] [TrustRoboReward: Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing for Multi-Paradigm Robot Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08491)
*Yidong Wang,Yan Zhan,Ziteng Feng,Zhenyu Cui,Ziyi Zhou,Renzhao Liang,Jiaxuan Zhu,Zilei Yang,Yiran Zhao,Zhongkuan Mao,Bo Jia,Hanchu Ni,Chenggang Xie,Biao Liu,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrustRoboReward，一个多范式奖励建模框架，通过 Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing (POISE) 方法校准点得分与配对偏好之间的不一致，将得分-配对反转冲突从 20.15% 降至 0%，在长程机器人操作任务中显著超越现有 VLM 奖励模型基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 AI 中的强化学习受限于奖励模型瓶颈：现有的开源 VLM 奖励评判器（如 RoboReward）仅采用简单的 1–5 轨迹进度打分，缺乏 RLHF、DPO 和 Bradley-Terry 框架所需的配对偏好监督，且未针对视频场景理解进行优化。简单地在 RoboReward 上增加配对比较和视频 QA 监督会导致配对偏好与点得分之间的不一致，引入训练噪声并损害下游性能，而现有的聚合方法（如 TrustJudge）无法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 TrustRoboReward 多范式奖励建模框架，包含 (1) 构建统一的四范式数据集：轨迹进度打分 (Score-A)、视频QA答案质量打分 (Score-B)，以及它们的配对版本 (Pair-A, Pair-B)；(2) 提出 POISE (Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing) 方法，利用配对标签校准点得分分布，消除得分-配对反转冲突（score-pair reversals），从而避免跨范式的反转冲突。

Result: 理论层面，POISE 将得分-配对反转冲突从 20.15% 降至 0%，而 TrustJudge 在同一语料上仍保留 20.46% 的冲突。实验方面，基于 Qwen3-VL-4B 训练的模型达到 77.96% 的总体奖励得分，几乎媲美 GPT-5-mini (78.09%，差距 0.13%)，比最强 RoboReward-4B 基线高出 10.13%。测试时得分-配对一致性提升至 71.90%，超越 RoboReward-4B (57.26%) 和 GPT-5-mini (68.09%)。集成 TrustJudge 聚合后总体得分达 78.57%，超过 GPT-5-mini 教师模型。

Conclusion: TrustRoboReward 通过 POISE 方法成功解决了 VLM 奖励模型中点得分与配对偏好不一致的关键问题，显著提升了长程机器人操作任务的奖励建模质量，并证明了优于现有方法（如 TrustJudge）的可行性。该方法为具身 AI 中的可扩展视觉反馈信号提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Reward models are a bottleneck for reinforcement learning in embodied AI. Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires scalable vision feedback beyond handcrafted rewards or task-specific annotations. Existing open-source VLM reward judges like RoboReward adopt simple 1--5 trajectory progress scoring, lacking pairwise preferences for RLHF, DPO and Bradley-Terry frameworks, while failing to optimize video scene understanding. Augmenting RoboReward with pairwise comparison and video-QA supervision causes inconsistency between pairwise preferences and pointwise scores, introducing training noise and hurting downstream performance---an issue aggregation methods such as TrustJudge cannot resolve. To address this, we propose TrustRoboReward, a multi-paradigm reward modeling framework equipped with Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing (POISE). We construct a unified four-paradigm dataset with trajectory progress scoring (Score-A), video-QA answer quality scoring (Score-B), and their pairwise counterparts (Pair-A, Pair-B). Pairwise labels align better with human judgment than pointwise scores, inspiring us to calibrate pointwise scores to avoid score-pair reversals against pairwise preferences. POISE rectifies pointwise scores and eliminates cross-paradigm reversal conflicts unresolved by TrustJudge. Theoretically, POISE reduces score-pair reversal conflicts from 20.15% to 0%, whereas TrustJudge retains 20.46% conflicts on the same corpus. Evaluated on our benchmark, Qwen3-VL-4B trained with POISE achieves an overall reward score of 77.96%, nearly matching GPT-5-mini (78.09%, gap 0.13%) and outperforming the strongest RoboReward-4B baseline by 10.13%. It also lifts test-time score-pair consistency to 71.90%, exceeding RoboReward-4B (57.26%) and GPT-5-mini (68.09%). Integrating TrustJudge aggregation during inference boosts the overall score to 78.57%, surpassing the GPT-5-mini teacher model.

</details>


### [356] [MathShikkha: A Controlled Study of Answer-Only and Chain-of-Thought Supervision for Bangla Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08503)
*Rahma Simin Ali,Jawad Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究低资源语言（孟加拉语）数学推理中，教师生成的思维链（CoT）监督是否优于普通监督微调。作者构建了MathShikkha数据集（含GPT-5.4生成的理由），在匹配协议下对四个4B-7B学生模型进行微调。结果发现：域内CoT对三个较强的模型无明显提升，但显著提升较弱的4B模型18.56分；在更大且经污染审计的BanglaMATH基准上，CoT对全部四个模型显著优于仅答案监督20.1-28.1分；仅答案微调会降低三个模型的域外准确率，而CoT则保持或提升。人工研究（Cohen's κ=0.76-1.00）发现CoT的主要效果是目标语言遵从性和产生可检查的推理，而非改善推理内容的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理在孟加拉语等低资源语言中仍然具有挑战性。作者希望探究教师生成的孟加拉语思维链监督是否能为普通监督微调带来额外收益，这是此前尚未被仔细研究的问题。

Method: 研究者构建了MathShikkha数据集（使用GPT-5.4生成孟加拉语推理理由），在匹配实验协议下对四个4B-7B学生模型进行微调。协议中答案仅（answer-only）和思维链（CoT）条件共享数据划分、响应仅损失掩码、解码和评分设置，仅训练目标不同。使用配对bootstrap 95%置信区间和精确McNemar检验进行统计评估，并在污染审计过的BanglaMATH基准上进行域外测试，同时安排了包含两位共同作者标注者、外部专家裁决的人工研究（Cohen's κ=0.76-1.00）。

Result: 域内场景中，CoT对三个较强骨干模型的提升无统计学显著性（配对bootstrap 95% CI含零；McNemar p≥0.17），尽管生成了15-52倍的token；但对较弱的4B模型提升18.56分（p<0.0001）。在更大、经污染审计的BanglaMATH基准上，CoT对全部四个模型显著优于仅答案监督，提升20.1-28.1分（均p<0.0001）。仅答案微调将三个模型的域外准确率降至基础模型之下，而CoT保持或提升了全部四个模型的域外性能。人工研究未发现CoT在推理内容标准上对基础模型有显著改进，其可测效果为目标语言遵从性和产生可检查的推理。

Conclusion: 理由监督的价值取决于骨干能力与分布偏移：在该设定下，CoT监督的主要受益点在于孟加拉语遵从性、可审计推理和域外鲁棒性，而非改善域内推理有效性。CoT的收益并非普适的，需要根据模型能力和任务分布来评估其适用性。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning remains challenging in low-resource languages such as Bangla. We study whether teacher-generated Bangla Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision provides benefits beyond ordinary supervised fine-tuning. We construct \textsc{MathShikkha}, a Bangla mathematical reasoning dataset with GPT-5.4-generated rationales, and fine-tune four 4B--7B student models under a matched protocol in which answer-only and CoT conditions share data splits, response-only loss masking, decoding, and scoring, differing only in the training target. In-domain, CoT provides no significant improvement over answer-only fine-tuning for three stronger backbones (paired bootstrap 95\% CIs include zero; exact McNemar $p \geq 0.17$), despite generating 15--52$\times$ more tokens, but significantly improves the weaker 4B model by 18.56 points ($p < 0.0001$). On the larger, contamination-audited BanglaMATH benchmark, this pattern reverses: CoT significantly outperforms answer-only supervision for all four models by 20.1--28.1 points (all $p < 0.0001$). Answer-only fine-tuning also reduces out-of-domain accuracy below the base model for three models, whereas CoT preserves or improves it for all four. A human study with two co-author annotators, external-expert adjudication, and Cohen's $κ= 0.76$--$1.00$ finds no significant CoT improvement over the base model on reasoning-content criteria; instead, its measurable effect is target-language adherence and producing inspectable reasoning. Overall, rationale supervision's value depends on backbone capability and distribution shift: in this setting, its main benefits are Bangla adherence, auditable reasoning, and out-of-domain robustness rather than improved in-domain reasoning validity.

</details>


### [357] [Understanding Calibration and Truncation Error Propagation in Training-Free Low-Rank Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08506)
*Mohanad Odema,Gabrielle De Micheli,Dayin Gou,Nilesh Malpeddi,Prathamesh Vaste,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种训练无关的低秩压缩框架改进方法，通过层间压缩与校准校正（Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction）和迭代压缩与秩分配校正（Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction）两种策略，解决现有SOTA低秩压缩框架中校准数据残差跨层累积以及层重要性分布假设失效的问题，在Llama和Qwen3模型上实现零样本任务约1-2.5个准确率点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关低秩压缩框架存在两个关键缺陷：(1)校准数据上的残差误差在压缩过程中跨层累积，导致压缩时模拟的表示与推理时实际经历的表示不对齐；(2)假设压缩后层重要性分布保持不变的假设不成立。这两者共同导致压缩过程与部署模型之间出现错位，影响压缩效果。

Method: 提出两种简单且训练无关的修正策略，可与现有框架兼容：(1)层间压缩与校准校正——在逐层压缩过程中对校准数据的激活进行校正，防止残差误差跨层累积；(2)迭代压缩与秩分配校正——通过迭代方式重新评估并校正各层的秩分配，以应对压缩后层重要性分布的变化。

Result: 将该方法应用于现有SOTA分解框架之上，在Llama和Qwen3模型上，覆盖多种基准测试和压缩率，零样本任务上相比逐权重和联合分解基线提升了约1-2.5个准确率点。

Conclusion: 通过针对压缩过程中两个核心错位来源的简单校正策略，训练无关低秩压缩框架的准确率可以进一步提升，且该方法无需训练、可与现有框架直接结合使用。

Abstract: Training-free low-rank compression frameworks have been gaining prominence for LLM compression given their effectiveness in reducing model parameter count while maintaining task-level accuracy. However, existing SOTA frameworks share two key limitations: (1) residual errors in calibration data activations accumulate across layers during compression, causing misalignment between representations simulated at compression time and those experienced at inference; (2) the assumption that layer importance distribution is preserved post-compression does not hold. Together, these two effects introduce misalignment in the compression process in relation to the deployed model. We study these effects and propose a simple, training-free methodology compatible with existing frameworks to mitigate them, comprising: (1) Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction; (2) Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction. Implemented atop an existing SOTA decomposition framework, and evaluated on Llama and Qwen3 models across various benchmarks and compression rates, our approach demonstrates up to ~1-2.5 accuracy point improvements over per-weight and joint decomposition baselines on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [358] [Time Present and Time Past: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Temporally Evolving Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08512)
*Mahbub E Sobhani,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Fahmid Hasan Chowdhury,Md Adnan Arefeen,Farig Sadeque,Md. Faizul Bari,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE，一个由专家验证的基准数据集，包含3,050个问答对，覆盖1969-2025年间孟加拉国政府发布的644份官方海关文书。该基准专门测试时间敏感的"版本解析"任务——即根据查询日期确定当时生效的文档版本。在统一协议下评估了九个近期LLM，最佳宏观平均准确率仅68.5%，版本解析准确率59.7%，而检测给定版本不适用于查询仅26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间敏感问答数据集仅将时间视为标注，未真正测试版本解析问题。然而实际场景中，法律、税法、软件文档等不断被修订、替换甚至回滚，同一问题在不同日期有不同正确答案。版本解析——识别查询日期时生效的版本——是核心挑战，但此前未被系统测试。

Method: 构建TIDE基准数据集：(1)收集孟加拉国政府1969-2025年发布的644份官方海关文书；(2)由专家验证生成3,050个QA对，覆盖8种任务类型；(3)文档深度混合编码、布局异构、采用双历法标注日期。评估方法：在统一协议下对九个近期LLM进行参数化、金上下文、检索三种访问模式评估，使用三法官LLM委员会评分并设置硬日期门以区分"语义正确"与"时间正确"。

Result: 最佳宏观平均准确率仅为68.5%。从隐式日期解析版本的成功率为59.7%，而检测提供的版本不适用于查询仅达26.7%。模型更容易找到正确版本而不是拒绝错误版本，且倾向于跟随自信的参数化答案而非提供的权威文本。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在时间敏感文档的版本解析任务上表现不佳，特别是在拒绝不正确版本方面存在显著短板。模型倾向于依赖参数记忆而非权威提供的上下文，这揭示了在官方文档场景中对时间感知推理能力的迫切需求。所有代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Evolving documents, such as laws, tax codes, and software documentation, are amended, replaced, and sometimes reverted over time, so a question has different correct answers at different dates. In contrast to encyclopedic knowledge, where an old fact is simply overwritten, an amendment is itself an official text that states what it replaces and when it takes effect, and the earlier version stays correct for its validity period. The central challenge is therefore version resolution, that is, identifying the version in force on the queried date. Existing temporal QA datasets treat time only as an annotation, so version resolution stays untested. We present TIDE, an expert-verified benchmark of 3,050 QA pairs over 644 official customs instruments issued between 1969 and 2025 by the Government of Bangladesh, covering eight task types over deeply code-mixed documents that are heterogeneous in layout and dated in two calendars. In addition, we evaluate nine recent LLMs under a single protocol across parametric, gold-context, and retrieval access, scored by a three-judge LLM council with a hard date gate separating correct meaning from correct time. The best macro-averaged accuracy is only 68.5%. Resolving a version from an implicit date reaches 59.7%, and detecting that the supplied version does not govern the query reaches only 26.7%. Models are more likely to find correct versions than to reject incorrect ones, and they tend to follow a confident parametric answer over the supplied authoritative text. All code and data are available at https://github.com/icsetepa44/TIDE

</details>


### [359] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了两种提升LLM推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），并在多个新领域和模型上进行了压力测试。结果显示RPC在新领域上相对自一致性无显著优势，LCF的逻辑有效性方向虽存在但较弱，且其正向效果不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPC和LCF这两种方法声称能提升LLM推理的可靠性，但原始评估由各自作者自行完成，从未被独立复现或在多种模型和领域上压力测试过；此外LCF没有公开代码。独立验证这些可靠性声明十分重要。

Method: 分别复现两种方法：RPC方法聚合token概率和自一致性进行推理；LCF方法训练投影器将隐藏状态分解为"内容"和"逻辑"两部分，并将逻辑部分编辑到有效区域。将两种方法扩展到四个新任务领域（text-to-SQL、法律提取、谬误识别、先例分级），使用Qwen3-8B及多个7-8B模型进行实验，并直接探测LCF的表示。

Result: RPC在作者的推理路径上精确复现原始网格结果；在四个新领域中，其相对自一致性的优势从未显著（并列或小混合差异，配对p>=0.28）；在BIRD领域增大样本到n=200后，其最大优势(+2.5)反转为-0.25。LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但较弱（最佳子层0.82可分性，对比语义属性对照的0.95）；其唯一正向效果(Qwen3 ΔProb)不显著(p=0.56)，且在另外三个模型中的两个上显著降低了ΔProb。

Conclusion: 两种方法的原始可靠性声明均未能经受独立复现与跨模型跨领域的压力测试：RPC的新领域优势不显著，LCF的效果微弱甚至在某些模型上有负面影响，提示需要更强的证据和更严谨的评估方式。
